*Lihua Zhou,Mao Ye,Xiatian Zhu,Nianxin Li,Changyi Ma,Shuaifeng Li,Yitong Qin,Hongbin Liu,Jiebo Luo,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RGSE，一种无需训练的测试时框架，通过进化搜索机制直接优化文本嵌入以对齐视觉嵌入，解决VLM开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下的性能下降问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于VLM的开放词汇目标检测方法在测试时分布偏移下性能严重下降，原因是文本嵌入与偏移后的区域提议视觉嵌入之间存在语义错位。而现有的测试时自适应方法要么依赖昂贵的反向传播，要么通过外部存储绕过语义错位问题，均无法以无训练方式直接高效地对齐文本与视觉。

Method: 提出奖励引导的语义演化（RGSE）框架，受进化搜索启发，将文本嵌入自适应视为语义搜索过程：对文本嵌入进行扰动生成候选变体，利用当前和历史高置信度视觉提议与候选变体的余弦相似度作为奖励信号进行评估，并通过奖励加权平均融合得到精炼的文本嵌入，整个过程无需反向传播。

Result: 在多个目标检测基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时仅增加极小的计算开销。

Conclusion: RGSE作为一种无需训练、无需反向传播的测试时文本嵌入自适应方法，能够高效对齐文本与视觉嵌入，显著提升VLM在分布偏移下的检测性能。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) such as Grounding DINO suffers from performance degradation under test-time distribution shifts, primarily due to semantic misalignment between text embeddings and shifted visual embeddings of region proposals. While recent test-time adaptive object detection methods for VLM-based either rely on costly backpropagation or bypass semantic misalignment via external memory, none directly and efficiently align text and vision in a training-free manner. To address this, we propose Reward-Guided Semantic Evolution (RGSE), a training-free framework that directly refines the text embeddings at test time. Inspired by evolutionary search, RGSE treats text embedding adaptation as a semantic search process: it perturbs text embeddings as candidate variants, evaluates them via cosine similarity with current and historical high-confidence visual proposals as a reward signal, and fuses them into a refined embedding through reward-weighted averaging. Without any backpropagation, RGSE achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple detection benchmarks while adding minimal computational overhead. Our code will be open source upon publication.

中文摘要翻译: 基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的开放词汇目标检测（如Grounding DINO）在测试时分布偏移下会出现性能下降，其主要原因是文本嵌入与区域提议的偏移后视觉嵌入之间存在语义错位。尽管近期针对VLM的测试时自适应目标检测方法要么依赖昂贵的反向传播，要么通过外部存储绕过语义错位，但没有一种方法能够以无需训练的方式直接且高效地对齐文本与视觉。为解决这一问题，我们提出奖励引导的语义演化（RGSE），一种无需训练的框架，可直接在测试时优化文本嵌入。受进化搜索启发，RGSE将文本嵌入自适应视为语义搜索过程：它对文本嵌入进行扰动以生成候选变体，利用当前和历史高置信度视觉提议的余弦相似度作为奖励信号对其进行评估，并通过奖励加权平均将其融合为精炼的嵌入。无需任何反向传播，RGSE在多个检测基准上取得了最先进的性能，同时仅增加极小的计算开销。我们的代码将在论文发表后开源。

</details>


### [32] [InterMesh: Explicit Interaction-Aware End-to-End Multi-Person Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04554)
*Kaili Zheng,Kaiwen Wang,Xun Zhu,Chenyi Guo,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterMesh通过显式建模人与物体及人与人之间的交互信息，利用人-物交互检测器增强查询表示，并结合轻量级模块(Contextual Interaction Encoder和Interaction-Guided Refiner)提升多人人体网格重建的精度，在多个数据集上达到SOTA，尤其在高交互场景下表现突出。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于DETR框架的端到端多人人体网格重建方法仅通过跨所有人查询的自注意力隐式建模交互关系，缺乏对人与物体、人与人之间交互的显式推理，导致复杂交互场景下的重建精度不足。

Method: 提出InterMesh框架，包含：(1) 利用人-物交互检测器提取结构化交互语义；(2) Contextual Interaction Encoder模块聚合上下文交互特征；(3) Interaction-Guided Refiner模块引导网格细化，以轻量级方式将交互信息集成到现有HMR架构中。

Result: 在3DPW、MuPoTS、CMU Panoptic、Hi4D和CHI3D数据集上均超越现有SOTA方法；在CMU Panoptic上MPJPE降低9.9%，在Hi4D上降低8.2%，验证了在复杂人-物和人人交互场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式引入人-环境交互信息能够有效提升人体网格重建的精度，InterMesh以简单高效的方式实现了对复杂交互场景的更好建模，为后续HMR研究提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Humans constantly interact with their surroundings. Existing end-to-end multi-person human mesh recovery methods, typically based on the DETR framework, capture inter-human relationships through self-attention across all human queries. However, these approaches model interactions only implicitly and lack explicit reasoning about how humans interact with objects and with each other. In this paper, we propose InterMesh, a simple yet effective framework that explicitly incorporates human-environment interaction information into human mesh recovery pipeline. By leveraging a human-object interaction detector, InterMesh enriches query representations with structured interaction semantics, enabling more accurate pose and shape estimation. We design lightweight modules, Contextual Interaction Encoder and Interaction-Guided Refiner, to integrate these features into existing HMR architectures with minimal overhead. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on 3DPW, MuPoTS, CMU Panoptic, Hi4D, and CHI3D datasets, demonstrating remarkable improvements over state-of-the-art methods. Notably, InterMesh reduces MPJPE by 9.9% on CMU Panoptic and 8.2% on Hi4D, highlighting its effectiveness in scenarios with complex human-object and inter-human interactions.

中文摘要翻译: 人类不断与环境进行交互。现有的端到端多人人体网格重建方法通常基于DETR框架，通过所有人体查询之间的自注意力机制捕获人与人之间的关系。然而，这些方法仅隐式地建模交互，缺乏对人类如何与物体交互以及彼此交互的显式推理。本文提出InterMesh，一个简单而有效的框架，将人-环境交互信息显式地融入人体网格重建流程。通过利用人-物交互检测器，InterMesh以结构化交互语义丰富查询表示，从而实现更准确的姿态和形状估计。我们设计了轻量级模块——上下文交互编码器(Contextual Interaction Encoder)和交互引导细化器(Interaction-Guided Refiner)，以最小开销将这些特征集成到现有HMR架构中。通过在3DPW、MuPoTS、CMU Panoptic、Hi4D和CHI3D数据集上的大量实验，我们验证了该方法相较于现有最优方法的显著提升。值得注意的是，InterMesh在CMU Panoptic上将MPJPE降低了9.9%，在Hi4D上降低了8.2%，突显了其在复杂人-物和人人交互场景中的有效性。

</details>


### [33] [Open-Source Image Editing Models Are Zero-Shot Vision Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04566)
*Wei Liu,Jiaxin Lin,Rui Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三个开源图像编辑模型（Qwen-Image-Edit、FireRed-Image-Edit、LongCat-Image-Edit）在密集视觉预测任务上的零样本能力，发现它们无需微调即可展现出非平凡的视觉理解能力，其中FireRed-Image-Edit在表面法线估计任务上甚至超越了经过微调的专用模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有研究表明大型生成模型可以解决未明确训练过的视觉任务，但现有证据依赖闭源模型或需要任务特定的指令微调，尚不清楚公开可用的图像编辑模型是否具备开箱即用的零样本视觉能力。

Method: 对三个开源图像编辑模型（Qwen-Image-Edit、FireRed-Image-Edit、LongCat-Image-Edit）在密集视觉预测任务上进行系统评估，不进行任何微调；在NYUv2和DIODE上评测单目深度估计，在NYUv2上评测表面法线估计，在Cityscapes上评测语义分割，涵盖几何与语义场景理解。

Result: FireRed-Image-Edit在NYUv2表面法线估计上平均角度误差为17.69°，超越了微调后的Marigold（20.86°），与指令微调的Vision Banana（17.78°）相当。LongCat-Image-Edit在NYUv2深度估计上δ₁=0.822，Qwen-Image-Edit在DIODE Indoor上δ₁=0.868。Qwen-Image-Edit在Cityscapes语义分割上19类mIoU为25.7，7类mIoU为49.5。

Conclusion: 开源图像编辑模型展现出非平凡的零样本视觉理解能力，零样本视觉能力是图像编辑预训练的一种涌现特性而非特定模型的偶然产物。代码、评估脚本和所有结果均已公开，作为未来工作的可复现基线。

Original Abstract: Recent studies have shown that large generative models can solve vision tasks they were not explicitly trained for. However, existing evidence relies on closed-source models~(Veo~3, Nano Banana Pro) or requires task-specific instruction tuning, leaving open whether publicly available image-editing models possess zero-shot vision abilities out of the box.
  We conduct a systematic evaluation of three open-source image-editing models -- Qwen-Image-Edit, FireRed-Image-Edit, and LongCat-Image-Edit -- on dense visual prediction tasks \emph{without any fine-tuning}. We benchmark monocular depth estimation on NYUv2 and DIODE, surface normal estimation on NYUv2, and semantic segmentation on Cityscapes, covering both geometric and semantic scene understanding.
  Results show that open-source image-editing models exhibit non-trivial zero-shot visual understanding. On NYUv2 surface normals, FireRed-Image-Edit achieves a mean angular error of $17.69^\circ$, surpassing the fine-tuned Marigold ($20.86^\circ$) and matching the instruction-tuned Vision Banana ($17.78^\circ$) without any task-specific training. On NYUv2 depth estimation, LongCat-Image-Edit obtains $δ_1{=}0.822$ with affine alignment, and Qwen-Image-Edit leads on DIODE Indoor ($δ_1{=}0.868$). On Cityscapes semantic segmentation, Qwen-Image-Edit reaches 25.7 mIoU at the 19-class level and 49.5 mIoU at a coarser 7-category level. By comparing three independently trained editors, we test whether zero-shot vision ability is an emergent property of image-editing pretraining rather than a model-specific artifact. Code, evaluation scripts, and all results are publicly released to serve as a reproducible baseline for future work.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究表明，大型生成模型能够解决它们未曾明确训练过的视觉任务。然而，现有证据依赖于闭源模型（如Veo 3、Nano Banana Pro）或需要任务特定的指令微调，因此公开可用的图像编辑模型是否具备开箱即用的零样本视觉能力仍是一个开放问题。我们对三个开源图像编辑模型——Qwen-Image-Edit、FireRed-Image-Edit和LongCat-Image-Edit——在密集视觉预测任务上进行了系统评估，未进行任何微调。我们在NYUv2和DIODE上评测了单目深度估计，在NYUv2上评测了表面法线估计，在Cityscapes上评测了语义分割，涵盖了几何与语义场景理解两个方面。结果表明，开源图像编辑模型展现出非平凡的零样本视觉理解能力。在NYUv2表面法线估计上，FireRed-Image-Edit的平均角度误差为17.69°，超越了经过微调的Marigold（20.86°），并与经过指令微调的Vision Banana（17.78°）相当，且无需任何任务特定训练。在NYUv2深度估计上，LongCat-Image-Edit在仿射对齐后达到δ₁=0.822，Qwen-Image-Edit在DIODE Indoor上以δ₁=0.868领先。在Cityscapes语义分割上，Qwen-Image-Edit在19类级别达到25.7 mIoU，在更粗粒度的7类级别达到49.5 mIoU。通过比较三个独立训练的编辑模型，我们检验了零样本视觉能力是图像编辑预训练的涌现特性而非特定模型的偶然产物。代码、评估脚本和所有结果均已公开发布，作为未来工作的可复现基线。

</details>


### [34] [Lightning Unified Video Editing via In-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04569)
*Shitong Shao,Zikai Zhou,Haopeng Li,Yingwei Song,Wenliang Zhong,Lichen Bai,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文稀疏注意力（ISA）框架，通过冗余上下文剪枝和动态查询分组机制，实现视频编辑中注意力的近无损加速，并构建了LIVEditor模型和1.7M高质量数据集，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，注意力模块延迟降低约60%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视频编辑向上下文学习（ICL）范式演进，但二次注意力复杂度带来了严重的计算瓶颈，现有方法无法在不牺牲保真度的情况下实现高效加速。

Method: 提出上下文稀疏注意力（ISA）框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 高效的预选择策略，根据上下文令牌显著性剪枝冗余上下文；(2) 动态查询分组机制，将高误差查询导向全注意力、低误差查询导向0阶泰勒稀疏注意力。同时构建了包含1.7M高质量样本的视频编辑数据集，并在此基础上搭建LIVEditor模型。

Result: LIVEditor在注意力模块延迟上降低约60%，同时在EditVerseBench、IVE-Bench和VIE-Bench等多个基准上超越现有最先进方法，实现了近无损加速且不降低视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 所提出的ISA框架是首个面向ICL视频编辑的近无损经验稀疏注意力方法，能够在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持模型性能，验证了上下文令牌显著性差异和查询锐度与近似误差相关性的理论发现。

Original Abstract: Video editing has evolved toward In-Context Learning (ICL) paradigms, yet the resulting quadratic attention costs create a critical computational bottleneck. In this work, we propose In-context Sparse Attention (ISA), the first near-lossless empirical sparse framework tailored for ICL video editing. Our design is grounded in two key insights: first, context tokens exhibit significantly lower saliency than source tokens; second, we theoretically prove and empirically validate that Query sharpness correlates with approximation error. Motivated by these findings, ISA implements an efficient pre-selection strategy to prune redundant context, followed by a dynamic query grouping mechanism that routes high-error queries to full attention and low-error ones to a computationally efficient 0-th order Taylor sparse attention. Furthermore, we build \textbf{\texttt{LIVEditor}} , a novel lightning video editing model via ISA and a proposed video-editing data pipeline that curated a 1.7M high-quality dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LIVEditor achieves a $\sim$60% reduction in attention-module latency while surpassing state-of-the-art methods across EditVerseBench, IVE-Bench, and VIE-Bench, delivering near-lossless acceleration without compromising visual fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 视频编辑已向上下文学习（In-Context Learning, ICL）范式演进，然而由此产生的二次注意力复杂度造成了严重的计算瓶颈。本文提出上下文稀疏注意力（In-context Sparse Attention, ISA）——首个专为ICL视频编辑设计的近无损经验稀疏框架。我们的设计基于两个关键发现：第一，上下文令牌的显著性显著低于源令牌；第二，我们从理论上证明并实验验证了查询锐度与近似误差之间的相关性。基于这些发现，ISA实现了一种高效的预选择策略来剪枝冗余上下文，随后引入动态查询分组机制，将高误差查询路由至全注意力，低误差查询路由至计算高效的0阶泰勒稀疏注意力。此外，我们构建了 \textbf{\texttt{LIVEditor}}——一种基于ISA的新型闪电视频编辑模型，并设计了一条视频编辑数据流水线，整合了170万高质量数据集。大量实验表明，LIVEditor在注意力模块延迟上降低约60%，同时在EditVerseBench、IVE-Bench和VIE-Bench上超越现有最先进方法，实现了近无损加速，且不牺牲视觉保真度。

</details>


### [35] [VL-UniTrack: A Unified Framework with Visual-Language Prompts for UAV-Ground Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04574)
*Boyue Xu,Ruichao Hou,Tongwei Ren,Gangshan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VL-UniTrack，一个由视觉-语言提示增强的完全统一框架，用于无人机-地面视觉跟踪。通过共享编码器、视觉-语言几何提示模块、提示引导的跨视图适配器以及置信度调制互蒸馏损失，解决了现有方法中特征提取隔离和过度依赖外观匹配的问题，在最新基准上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无人机-地面双流视觉跟踪方法存在特征提取隔离问题，且严重依赖隐式外观匹配，在视角差异巨大时难以建立可靠的对应关系，导致跟踪不可靠。

Method: 1) 共享编码器：将双视图特征统一编码，打破特征隔离；2) 视觉-语言几何提示模块：融合语言描述与视觉特征，生成可学习提示；3) 提示引导跨视图适配器：利用提示实现充分的跨视图特征交互并学习视图特定特征；4) 置信度调制互蒸馏损失：通过噪声传播缓解来正则化训练。

Result: 在最新基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架有效解决了双流方法特征隔离和依赖外观匹配的问题，显著提升了无人机-地面视觉跟踪的可靠性。

Original Abstract: UAV-ground visual tracking (UGVT) aims to simultaneously track the same object from both the UAV and the ground view. However, existing two-stream methods suffer from isolated feature extraction and rely heavily on implicit appearance matching, which struggles to establish reliable correspondence under drastic view differences, leading to tracking unreliability. To address these limitations, we propose VL-UniTrack, a fully unified framework enhanced by visual-language prompts. By encoding features from both views within a single shared encoder, our method breaks the barrier of feature isolation to facilitate sufficient cross-view interaction. To overcome the ambiguity caused by relying solely on appearance matching, we design visual-language geometric prompting module, which fuses language descriptions with visual features to generate learnable prompts. These prompts are then fed into our prompt-guided cross-view adapter module to enable sufficient cross-view feature interaction and to guide the learning of view-specific feature representations. Furthermore, a confidence-modulated mutual distillation loss is proposed to regularize the training by mitigating noise propagation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on the latest benchmark. The code can be downloaded in https://github.com/xuboyue1999/VL-UniTrack.git

中文摘要翻译: 无人机-地面视觉跟踪（UGVT）旨在从无人机和地面视角同时跟踪同一目标。然而，现有的双流方法存在特征提取隔离问题，且严重依赖隐式外观匹配，在视角差异巨大时难以建立可靠的对应关系，导致跟踪不可靠。为解决这些局限，我们提出了VL-UniTrack——一个由视觉-语言提示增强的完全统一框架。通过在单个共享编码器中对两个视角的特征进行编码，我们的方法打破了特征隔离的障碍，促进了充分的跨视图交互。为克服仅依赖外观匹配导致的模糊性，我们设计了视觉-语言几何提示模块，将语言描述与视觉特征融合以生成可学习提示。这些提示随后被输入到提示引导的跨视图适配器模块中，以实现充分的跨视图特征交互，并引导学习特定于视图的特征表示。此外，我们提出了一种置信度调制互蒸馏损失，通过缓解噪声传播来正则化训练。大量实验表明，我们的方法在最新基准上取得了最先进的性能。代码可在https://github.com/xuboyue1999/VL-UniTrack.git获取。

</details>


### [36] [From Diffusion to Rectified Flow: Rethinking Text-Based Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04590)
*Zishen Qu,Xuesong Li,Haijian Gu,Hongwei Kang,Quan Meng,Tianrui Niu,Xin Yang,Ruidong Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RLFSeg框架，使用Rectified Flow替代扩散模型，将预训练生成模型直接用于判别式分割任务，避免了噪声-去噪过程和时序优化，在零样本场景下显著优于以往方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的分割方法继承了扩散模型的生成特性（如噪声-去噪过程），这些特性不利于判别式分割任务，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出RLFSeg框架，利用Rectified Flow在潜在空间学习从图像到分割掩码的直接映射，免除噪声-去噪过程和时间步优化；引入标签细化（label refinement）和自适应一步采样（Adaptive One-Step Sampling）策略，在单步推理下仍能保持高精度。

Result: 在零样本场景下显著优于以往基于扩散模型的方法；在单步推理下仍能达到高精度。

Conclusion: 该框架无需对模型结构做任何修改即可将预训练生成模型重定向到判别式分割任务，具有广阔的应用潜力和重要的研究价值。

Original Abstract: Text-based image segmentation aims to delineate object boundaries within an image from text prompts, offering higher flexibility and broader application scope compared to traditional fixed-category segmentation tasks. Recent studies have shown that diffusion models (e.g., Stable Diffusion) can provide rich multimodal semantic features, leading to studies of using diffusion models as feature extractors for segmentation tasks. Such methods, however, inherit the generative natures of diffusion models that are harmful to discriminative segmentation tasks. In response, we propose RLFSeg, a novel framework that leverages Rectified Flow to learn direct mapping from the image to the segmentation mask within the latent space. The model is thus freed from the noise-denoise process and the need to optimize the time step of diffusion models, resulting in substantially better performance than previous diffusion-based methods, especially on zero-shot scenarios. By introducing label refinement and an Adaptive One-Step Sampling strategy, the model achieves higher accuracy even on a single inference step. The framework redirects a pretrained generative model to the discriminative segmentation task with zero modification to model structure, thus reveals promising application potential and significant research value.

中文摘要翻译: 基于文本的图像分割旨在根据文本提示描绘图像中的目标边界，相较于传统的固定类别分割任务具有更高的灵活性和更广的应用范围。近年研究表明，扩散模型（如Stable Diffusion）能够提供丰富的多模态语义特征，因此涌现出将扩散模型作为分割任务特征提取器的研究。然而，这类方法继承了扩散模型的生成特性，这些特性不利于判别式分割任务。为此，我们提出RLFSeg，一种新颖的框架，利用Rectified Flow在潜在空间中学习从图像到分割掩码的直接映射。该模型从而摆脱了噪声-去噪过程以及对扩散模型时间步优化的依赖，在性能上显著优于以往的基于扩散模型的方法，尤其在零样本场景下。通过引入标签细化与自适应一步采样策略，该模型即使在单步推理下也能实现更高的精度。该框架在不修改模型结构的前提下，将预训练生成模型重定向到判别式分割任务，展现出广阔的应用潜力与重要的研究价值。

</details>


### [37] [DiCLIP: Diffusion Model Enhances CLIP's Dense Knowledge for Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04593)
*Zhiwei Yang,Pengfei Song,Yucong Meng,Kexue Fu,Shuo Wang,Zhijian Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiCLIP框架，利用扩散模型增强CLIP在弱监督语义分割中的密集知识，通过视觉相关性增强（VCE）和文本语义增强（TSA）两个模块提升CAM生成质量，在PASCAL VOC和MS COCO上达到SOTA且训练成本更低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的WSSS方法仅利用其视觉-语言配对特性进行密集定位，忽略了CLIP在视觉和文本两个模态上本身缺乏足够的密集知识，导致CAM生成效果不理想。

Method: 提出DiCLIP框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 视觉相关性增强（VCE）模块，利用扩散模型的空间一致性缓解CLIP注意力的过平滑问题，设计注意力聚类精炼（ACR）模块提取多样性相关图作为CLIP自注意力的多样化偏置；(2) 文本语义增强（TSA）模块，利用扩散模型的生成能力维护动态键值缓存模型，将CAM生成从补丁-文本匹配转变为视觉知识检索范式。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC和MS COCO数据集上，DiCLIP超越了现有最先进方法，同时显著降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型增强CLIP在多模态下的密集知识，能够有效提升弱监督语义分割的性能并降低训练开销。

Original Abstract: Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation (WSSS) with image-level labels typically leverages Class Activation Maps (CAMs) to achieve pixel-level predictions. Recently, Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) has been introduced to generate CAMs in WSSS. However, previous WSSS methods solely adopt CLIP's vision-language paired property for dense localization, neglecting its inherently limited dense knowledge across both visual and text modalities, which renders CAM generation suboptimal. In this work, we propose DiCLIP, a novel WSSS framework that leverages the generative diffusion model to enhance CLIP's dense knowledge across two modalities. Specifically, Visual Correlation Enhancement (VCE) and Text Semantic Augmentation (TSA) modules are proposed for dense prediction enhancement. To improve the spatial awareness of visual features, our VCE module utilizes diffusion's reliable spatial consistency to mitigate the over-smoothing issue in CLIP's attention. It designs the Attention Clustering Refinement (ACR) module to reliably extract diverse correlation maps from the diffusion model. The correlation maps act as a diversity bias for CLIP's self-attention, recursively pushing its visual features towards a more discriminative dense distribution. To augment the semantics of text embeddings, our TSA module argues that a single text modality is insufficient to encompass the variability of visual categories. Thus, we leverage diffusion's generative power to maintain a dynamic key-value cache model, shifting CAM generation from a patch-text matching mechanism to a novel visual knowledge retrieval paradigm. With these enhancements, DiCLIP not only outperforms state-of-the-art methods on PASCAL VOC and MS COCO but also significantly reduces training costs. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/zwyang6/DiCLIP.

中文摘要翻译: 基于图像级标签的弱监督语义分割（WSSS）通常利用类激活图（CAM）来实现像素级预测。近年来，对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）被引入WSSS以生成CAM。然而，以往的WSSS方法仅采用CLIP的视觉-语言配对属性进行密集定位，忽略了其在视觉和文本模态上固有的密集知识局限性，这使得CAM的生成效果欠佳。本文提出DiCLIP，一种新颖的WSSS框架，利用生成式扩散模型增强CLIP在两个模态上的密集知识。具体而言，我们提出了视觉相关性增强（VCE）和文本语义增强（TSA）模块用于密集预测增强。为提升视觉特征的空间感知能力，VCE模块利用扩散模型可靠的空间一致性来缓解CLIP注意力中的过平滑问题。该模块设计了注意力聚类精炼（ACR）模块，以从扩散模型中可靠地提取多样的相关图。这些相关图作为CLIP自注意力的多样性偏置，递归地推动其视觉特征向更具判别性的密集分布演进。为增强文本嵌入的语义，TSA模块认为单一文本模态不足以涵盖视觉类别的变异性。因此，我们利用扩散模型的生成能力维护一个动态键值缓存模型，将CAM生成从补丁-文本匹配机制转变为一种新颖的视觉知识检索范式。通过这些增强，DiCLIP不仅在PASCAL VOC和MS COCO上超越了现有最先进方法，还显著降低了训练成本。代码已开源：https://github.com/zwyang6/DiCLIP。

</details>


### [38] [Reference-based Category Discovery: Unsupervised Object Detection with Category Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04606)
*Yichen Li,Qiankun Liu,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RefCD，一种无需任何人工标注即可实现类别感知检测的无监督检测器，通过参考图像的特征相似性引导类别特征学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统单样本检测方法虽解决了闭集检测问题，但数据标注成本高；通用无监督方法生成的伪框缺乏类别标签，无法实现类别感知分类。

Method: 提出基于参考的类别发现（RefCD）方法，通过计算预测对象与无标注参考图像之间的特征相似性，设计专门的特征相似性损失函数，显式引导潜在类别特定特征的学习。此外，RefCD无需参考图像也能支持类别无关检测，形成统一框架。

Result: 通过全面的类别感知和类别无关检测结果的定量与定性分析，验证了RefCD的有效性，表明其在无监督范式下无需类别标签也能学习类别信息。

Conclusion: RefCD在无监督范式下无需任何人工标注即可实现类别感知目标检测，弥补了无监督方法缺乏类别指导的不足，且可作为统一框架支持类别无关检测。

Original Abstract: Traditional one-shot detection methods have addressed the closed-set problem in object detection, but the high cost of data annotation remains a critical challenge. General unsupervised methods generate pseudo boxes without category labels, thus failing to achieve category-aware classification. To overcome these limitations, we propose Reference-based Category Discovery (RefCD), an unsupervised detector that enables category-aware\footnotemark[1] detection without any manually annotated labels. It leverages feature similarity between predicted objects and unlabeled reference images. Unlike previous unsupervised methods that lack category guidance and one-shot methods which require labeled data, RefCD introduces a carefully designed feature similarity loss to explicitly guide the learning of potential category-specific features. Additionally, RefCD supports category-agnostic detection without reference images, serving as a unified framework. Comprehensive quantitative and qualitative analysis of category-aware and category-agnostic detection results demonstrates its effectiveness, and RefCD can learn category information in an unsupervised paradigm even without category labels.

中文摘要翻译: 传统单样本检测方法解决了目标检测中的闭集问题，但数据标注的高成本仍然是一个关键挑战。通用的无监督方法生成的伪框缺乏类别标签，因此无法实现类别感知的分类。为了克服这些局限性，我们提出了基于参考的类别发现（RefCD），一种无需任何人工标注即可实现类别感知检测的无监督检测器。它利用预测对象与无标注参考图像之间的特征相似性。与先前缺乏类别指导的无监督方法以及需要标注数据的单样本方法不同，RefCD引入了一个精心设计的特征相似性损失函数，以显式引导潜在类别特定特征的学习。此外，RefCD无需参考图像即可支持类别无关检测，作为一个统一的框架。对类别感知和类别无关检测结果进行的全面定量与定性分析证明了其有效性，且RefCD能够在无监督范式下即使没有类别标签也能学习类别信息。

</details>


### [39] [Advancing Aesthetic Image Generation via Composition Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04609)
*Kai Zou,Zhiwei Zhao,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Composer框架，通过语义无关的方式显式建模图像构图，支持构图迁移、主题驱动构图检索和隐式构图规划，显著提升文生图任务的美学质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常通过隐式学习或基于语义的布局控制来增强构图，而非显式建模构图本身。构图原则独立于具体内容，但实践中常与语义耦合，因此需要一种语义无关的构图建模方法。

Method: 1) 从参考图像提取构图感知表示，结合条件引导模块控制扩散模型进行构图迁移；2) 利用LVLM的上下文学习能力实现主题驱动的构图检索；3) 对控制模块进行文本到构图的微调，实现无参考模式下的隐式构图规划；4) 使用生成模型构建200万图像-文本对的高质量数据集。

Result: Composer在文生图任务中显著提升了美学质量，并实现了个性化的构图控制与迁移，为用户提供了精确且灵活的创作过程。

Conclusion: Composer通过显式、语义无关的构图建模，有效弥补了现有方法在构图控制上的不足，在美学质量提升和灵活控制方面均取得了显著效果。

Original Abstract: Composition is a cornerstone of visual aesthetics, influencing the appeal of an image. While its principles operate independently of specific content, in practice, composition is often coupled with semantics. As a result, existing methods often enhance composition either through implicit learning or by semantics-based layout control, rather than explicitly modeling composition itself. To address this gap, we introduce Composer, a framework rooted in aesthetic theory, designed to model composition in a semantic-agnostic manner. First, it supports composition transfer by extracting key composition-aware representations from a reference image and leveraging a tailored conditional guidance module to control composition based on pre-trained diffusion models. Second, when users specify only text themes without a composition reference, Composer supports theme-driven composition retrieval by leveraging the in-context learning capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), achieving explicit composition planning. To enhance composition in a reference-free mode, we conduct text-to-composition fine-tuning on the trained control module to enable implicit composition planning. Furthermore, we curated a high-quality dataset comprising 2 million image-text pairs using state-of-the-art generative models to support model training. Experimental results demonstrate that Composer significantly enhances aesthetic quality in text-to-image tasks and facilitates personalized composition control and transfer, offering users precision and flexibility in the creative process.

中文摘要翻译: 构图是视觉美学的基础，影响着图像的吸引力。尽管构图原则独立于具体内容，但在实践中，构图常常与语义耦合。因此，现有方法通常通过隐式学习或基于语义的布局控制来增强构图，而非显式建模构图本身。为解决这一差距，我们引入了Composer——一个根植于美学理论的框架，旨在以语义无关的方式建模构图。首先，它通过从参考图像中提取关键构图感知表示，并利用定制的条件引导模块基于预训练扩散模型控制构图，从而支持构图迁移。其次，当用户仅指定文本主题而无构图参考时，Composer借助大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的上下文学习能力支持主题驱动的构图检索，实现显式的构图规划。为了在无参考模式下增强构图，我们对训练好的控制模块进行文本到构图的微调，以实现隐式构图规划。此外，我们使用最先进的生成模型构建了一个包含200万图像-文本对的高质量数据集，以支持模型训练。实验结果表明，Composer显著提升了文生图任务的美学质量，并促进了个性化构图控制与迁移，为用户在创作过程中提供了精确性与灵活性。

</details>


### [40] [Temporal Structure Matters for Efficient Test-Time Adaptation in Wearable Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04617)
*Zishu Zhou,Zaipeng Xie,Xuanyao Jie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIGHT，一种轻量级、无需反向传播的测试时自适应框架，利用穿戴式人体活动识别中窗口间的时序结构（特征偏差与时序连续性）进行在线模型自适应，在降低计算和内存成本的同时超越现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法多继承自视觉任务，未能充分利用WHAR流数据中固有的窗口间时序结构（如时间连续性、特征偏差），导致跨用户分布漂移下性能下降。

Method: SIGHT通过比较当前特征与原型期望状态计算预测惊喜度，利用特征偏差指导基于原型对齐的几何感知转换路由，并结合流级边际习惯追踪实现轻量级、无反向传播的在线自适应。

Result: 在真实数据集上，SIGHT在性能上超越现有TTA基线，同时显著降低了计算和内存开销。

Conclusion: 将时序结构视为特征条件推理信号而非输出空间平滑先验，能够有效提升WHAR在跨用户分布漂移下的模型自适应能力，且SIGHT框架适合实时边缘部署。

Original Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (WHAR) models often suffer from performance degradation under real-world cross-user distribution shifts. Test-time adaptation (TTA) mitigates this degradation by adapting models online using unlabeled test streams, yet existing methods largely inherit assumptions from vision tasks and underexploit the inherent inter-window temporal structure in WHAR streams. In this paper, we revisit such temporal structure as a feature-conditioned inference signal rather than merely an output-space smoothing prior. We derive the insight that temporal continuity and observation-induced feature deviations provide complementary cues for determining when to preserve or release temporal inertia and where to route prediction refinement during likely transitions. Building upon this insight, we propose SIGHT, a lightweight and backpropagation-free TTA framework for WHAR, enabling real-time edge deployment. SIGHT estimates predictive surprise by comparing the current feature with a prototype-based expected state, and then uses the resulting feature deviation to guide geometry-aware transition routing based on prototype alignment and stream-level marginal habit tracking. Evaluations on real-world datasets confirm that SIGHT outperforms existing TTA baselines while reducing computational and memory costs.

中文摘要翻译: 可穿戴人体活动识别（WHAR）模型在现实跨用户分布漂移下常出现性能下降。测试时自适应（TTA）通过在线利用无标签测试流来缓解这种下降，然而现有方法大多继承了视觉任务的假设，未能充分利用WHAR流中固有的窗口间时序结构。本文重新审视这种时序结构，将其视为一种特征条件推理信号，而非仅仅是输出空间的平滑先验。我们推导出如下洞见：时间连续性和观测引起的特征偏差提供了互补线索，用于决定何时保持或释放时间惯性，以及在可能的转换期间将预测校正导向何处。基于这一洞见，我们提出SIGHT，一种轻量级且无需反向传播的TTA框架，适用于实时边缘部署。SIGHT通过将当前特征与基于原型的期望状态进行比较来估计预测惊喜度，然后利用由此产生的特征偏差，基于原型对齐和流级边际习惯追踪来指导几何感知的转换路由。在真实数据集上的评估证实，SIGHT在降低计算和内存成本的同时，优于现有的TTA基线。

</details>


### [41] [UniPCB: A Generation-Assisted Detection Framework for PCB Defect Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04635)
*Huan Zhang,Lianghong Tan,Yichu Xu,Jiangzhong Cao,Huanqi Wu,Linwei Zhu,Xu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对PCB缺陷检测中缺陷样本稀缺、类别不平衡以及复杂背景下特征表示不足的双重挑战，本文提出UniPCB生成辅助检测框架：生成端采用多模态条件生成器（边缘、深度、文本）+ ScaleEncoder + FiLM空间自适应调制进行可控缺陷合成；检测端采用倒置残差移位注意力（IRSA）和跨级互补融合模块（CCFB）增强特征表示。在DsPCBSD+数据集上，检测任务mAP@0.5达98.0%，生成任务FID达129.61。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PCB缺陷检测面临两大难点：一是实际生产中缺陷样本稀少且类别分布极不均衡，导致模型训练不充分；二是复杂电路背景下特征表示能力不足，难以区分缺陷与背景干扰。同时，现有生成方法依赖单一模态条件且结构控制粗糙，检测方法虽改进架构却未解决数据瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出UniPCB生成辅助检测框架，包含两个分支：(1) 生成分支：多模态条件生成器并行提取边缘、深度和文本条件；ScaleEncoder将条件嵌入扩散U-Net的四个分辨率层级；Condition Modulation在各层级施加FiLM风格的空间自适应调制，实现结构对齐和缺陷感知的样本合成。(2) 检测分支：倒置残差移位注意力（IRSA）耦合自注意力与移位卷积，联合捕获全局上下文与局部纹理；跨级互补融合模块（CCFB）生成像素级门控，实现选择性跨层特征融合。合成样本直接扩充检测训练集，形成生成与检测的协同提升。

Result: 在DsPCBSD+数据集上，UniPCB缺陷检测达到mAP@0.5为98.0%、mAP@0.5:0.95为61.8%，超越所有对比方法；生成分支FID为129.61、SSIM为0.619，优于现有条件生成方法。

Conclusion: 本文通过将可控缺陷合成与任务特定检测相结合，证明了生成辅助范式能有效缓解数据瓶颈并提升检测性能，生成与检测的协同提升机制为PCB缺陷检测提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Printed Circuit Board (PCB) defect inspection faces two compounding challenges: scarce and imbalanced defect samples that limit model training, and insufficient feature representation under complex circuit backgrounds. Existing generation methods rely on single-modality conditions with coarse structural control, while detection methods improve architectures without addressing the data bottleneck. To resolve both challenges jointly, we propose a generation-assisted PCB defect inspection framework that integrates controlled defect synthesis with task-specific defect detection. On the generation side, a Multi-modal Condition Generator extracts complementary edge, depth, and text conditions in parallel. A ScaleEncoder then embeds these conditions into the diffusion U-Net at four resolutions, and a Condition Modulation applies FiLM-style spatially-adaptive modulation at each scale, enabling structurally aligned and defect-aware sample synthesis. On the detection side, an Inverted Residual Shift Attention couples self-attention with shift-wise convolution to jointly capture global context and local texture, and a Cross-level Complementary Fusion Block generates pixel-level gates for selective cross-level feature fusion. The synthesized samples directly enrich the detection training set, so that improvements in generation compound with improvements in detection. Extensive experiments on DsPCBSD+ demonstrate that UniPCB achieves mAP@0.5 of 98.0% and mAP@0.5:0.95 of 61.8% on defect detection, surpassing all compared methods, while the generation branch attains an FID of 129.61 and SSIM of 0.619, outperforming existing conditional generation approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 印刷电路板（PCB）缺陷检测面临两个叠加挑战：稀缺且不均衡的缺陷样本限制了模型训练，以及复杂电路背景下特征表示不足。现有生成方法依赖单模态条件且结构控制粗糙，检测方法虽改进架构却未解决数据瓶颈。为同时应对这两个挑战，我们提出了一种生成辅助的PCB缺陷检测框架，将受控缺陷合成与任务特定缺陷检测相结合。在生成端，多模态条件生成器并行提取互补的边缘、深度和文本条件，ScaleEncoder将这些条件嵌入扩散U-Net的四个分辨率层级，Condition Modulation在各尺度施加FiLM风格的空间自适应调制，实现结构对齐和缺陷感知的样本合成。在检测端，倒置残差移位注意力将自注意力与移位卷积耦合，联合捕获全局上下文与局部纹理；跨级互补融合模块生成像素级门控，实现选择性跨层特征融合。合成样本直接丰富检测训练集，使生成改进与检测改进相互增强。在DsPCBSD+上的大量实验表明，UniPCB在缺陷检测上达到了mAP@0.5为98.0%、mAP@0.5:0.95为61.8%，超越所有对比方法；同时生成分支的FID为129.61、SSIM为0.619，优于现有条件生成方法。

</details>


### [42] [CAST: Mitigating Object Hallucination in Large Vision-Language Models via Caption-Guided Visual Attention Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04641)
*Qiming Li,Zekai Ye,Xiaocheng Feng,Weihong Zhong,Libo Qin,Ruihan Chen,Lei Huang,Baohang Li,Kui Jiang,Yaowei Wang,Ting Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST是一种无需训练、即插即用的方法，通过利用描述性查询的注意力激活模式来引导视觉注意力，从而有效缓解大型视觉-语言模型中的对象幻觉问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LVLMs在生成内容时经常偏离视觉信息，产生对象幻觉。现有方法要么依赖昂贵的人工标注和训练成本，要么采用显著增加推理时间的解码策略，亟需一种低成本、高效的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: 观察到LVLM在处理描述性查询时对视觉信息的注意力显著增强，基于此提出CAST方法：通过探针技术识别对描述性查询高度敏感的注意力头，估计其输出的最优引导方向，从而增强模型对细粒度视觉信息的感知能力，有效缓解对象幻觉。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的LVLM和五个基准（涵盖判别式和生成式任务）上，CAST平均减少6.03%的对象幻觉，达到最先进性能，且几乎不增加推理开销，不损害其它基础能力。

Conclusion: CAST是一种训练免费、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法，通过注意力引导有效提升了LVLMs的视觉感知能力，在降低对象幻觉方面取得了优异效果，且不影响模型其他能力。

Original Abstract: Although Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on downstream tasks, they frequently produce contents that deviate from visual information, leading to object hallucination. To tackle this, recent works mostly depend on expensive manual annotations and training cost, or decoding strategies which significantly increase inference time. In this work, we observe that LVLMs' attention to visual information is significantly enhanced when answering caption queries compared to non-caption queries. Inspired by this phenomenon, we propose Caption-guided Visual Attention Steering (CAST), a training-free, plug-and-play hallucination mitigation method that leverages the attention activation pattern corresponding to caption queries to enhance LVLMs' visual perception capability. Specifically, we use probing techniques to identify attention heads that are highly sensitive to caption queries and estimate optimized steering directions for their outputs. This steering strengthens LVLM's fine-grained visual perception capabilities, thereby effectively mitigating object hallucination. CAST reduced object hallucination by an average of 6.03% across five widely used LVLMs and five benchmarks including both discriminative and generative tasks, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance while adding little inference cost and preserving other foundational capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在下游任务中展现了卓越的性能，但它们经常生成偏离视觉信息的内容，导致对象幻觉。为解决这一问题，近期的工作大多依赖于昂贵的人工标注和训练成本，或采用显著增加推理时间的解码策略。在本工作中，我们观察到LVLM在处理描述性查询时，其对视觉信息的注意力相较于非描述性查询显著增强。受此现象启发，我们提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的幻觉缓解方法——描述引导的视觉注意力引导（CAST），该方法利用描述性查询对应的注意力激活模式来增强LVLMs的视觉感知能力。具体而言，我们使用探针技术识别对描述性查询高度敏感的注意力头，并为其输出估计最优的引导方向。这种引导增强了LVLM的细粒度视觉感知能力，从而有效缓解了对象幻觉。CAST在五个广泛使用的LVLM和五个涵盖判别式与生成式任务的基准上，平均减少了6.03%的对象幻觉，在几乎不增加推理开销且保持其他基础能力的前提下，达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [43] [Contact Matrix: Enhancing Dance Motion Synthesis with Precise Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04662)
*Xuhai Chen,Zhi Cen,Huaijin Pi,Sida Peng,Xiaowei Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架用于生成双人舞中的反应性动作：第一阶段使用分体部VQ-VAE增强动作表示能力，第二阶段使用接触感知扩散模型联合生成动作和接触矩阵，在互动真实感和节奏同步性上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 双人舞中反应性动作生成面临数据稀缺、动作模式复杂、交互细节丰富且约束严格的挑战，现有方法难以在有限解空间中生成高质量、符合交互约束的动作。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段，采用分体部编码器和联合解码器的运动VQ-VAE，通过专用码本增强表示能力并在解码时动态建模身体部位间的依赖关系；第二阶段，提出接触感知扩散模型，联合生成运动序列和人物间的接触矩阵，实现显式交互建模并在采样过程中提供更精准的约束引导。

Result: 在双人舞任务上，该方法在FID_k（8.89 vs 25.30）、FID_cd（8.01 vs 9.97）和BED（0.4606 vs 0.2858）三个指标上均显著优于Duolando基线，表明更高的互动保真度和节奏同步性。

Conclusion: 分体部VQ-VAE结合接触感知扩散模型的两阶段框架能有效解决双人舞反应性动作生成中的交互约束和表示能力问题，生成更真实、同步性更高的双人互动动作。

Original Abstract: Generating realistic reactive motions, in which one person reacts to the fixed motions of others, is challenging due to strict interaction constraints and a limited feasible solution space. This paper focuses on a typical scenario: duet dance, where high-quality data is scarce, motion patterns are complex, and the details of human interactions are both intricate and abundant. To tackle these challenges, we propose a novel two-stage framework. In the first stage, we introduce a motion VQ-VAE with separate body-part encoders and a joint decoder, enabling specialized codebooks to enhance representation capacity while dynamically modeling dependencies across body parts during decoding, thereby preventing inconsistencies in the generated motions. In the second stage, we propose a contact-aware diffusion model for reactive motion generation that jointly generates motion and a contact matrix between individuals, enabling explicit interaction modeling and providing guidance toward more precise and constrained interaction dynamics during sampling. Experiments show that our method outperforms Duolando with lower $\text{FID}_k$ (8.89 vs. 25.30) and $\text{FID}_{cd}$ (8.01 vs. 9.97), as well as a higher BED (0.4606 vs. 0.2858), indicating improved interaction fidelity and rhythmic synchronization.

中文摘要翻译: 生成逼真的反应性动作——即一个人对他人固定动作做出反应——由于严格的交互约束和有限的可行解空间而极具挑战性。本文聚焦于一个典型场景：双人舞。该场景中高质量数据稀缺、运动模式复杂，且人类互动的细节既精细又丰富。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新颖的两阶段框架。在第一阶段，我们引入了一个带有分体部编码器和联合解码器的运动VQ-VAE，通过专用码本增强表示能力，同时在解码过程中动态建模身体部位间的依赖关系，从而防止生成动作中的不一致性。在第二阶段，我们提出了一种接触感知扩散模型用于反应性动作生成，该模型联合生成运动序列和个体间的接触矩阵，实现显式交互建模，并在采样过程中为更精确且受约束的交互动态提供引导。实验表明，我们的方法在多个指标上优于Duolando：FID_k从25.30降至8.89，FID_cd从9.97降至8.01，BED从0.2858提升至0.4606，表明互动保真度和节奏同步性均有显著提升。

</details>

*Xiaopei Zhu,Guanning Zeng,Zhanhao Hu,Jun Zhu,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对RGB-T多模态目标检测在物理世界中的安全性问题，提出了一种基于非重叠RGB-T图案（NORP）的对抗服装攻击方法，通过空间离散-连续优化（SDCO）实现全域视角攻击，并设计了融合阶段集成方法以增强跨不同融合架构的迁移性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: RGB-T目标检测在自动驾驶等应用中至关重要，但其在物理世界中的安全性问题被严重忽视，现有研究缺乏对RGB-T检测器物理攻击的有效手段。

Method: 提出非重叠RGB-T图案（NORP）的对抗服装设计，使用不重叠的可见光和热红外材料避免光照衰减；构建3D RGB-T人体与服装模型以模拟全视角（0°-360°）攻击；提出空间离散-连续优化（SDCO）方法优化NORP图案；引入融合阶段集成方法增强攻击跨不同融合架构的迁移性。

Result: 在多种融合架构的RGB-T检测器上进行了系统评估，在数字世界和物理世界均实现了高攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 提出的NORP对抗服装结合SDCO优化方法能够有效攻击RGB-T检测器，且融合阶段集成方法可提升攻击在不同融合架构之间的迁移能力。

Original Abstract: Visible-thermal (RGB-T) object detection is a crucial technology for applications such as autonomous driving, where multimodal fusion enhances performance in challenging conditions like low light. However, the security of RGB-T detectors, particularly in the physical world, has been largely overlooked. This paper proposes a novel approach to RGB-T physical attacks using adversarial clothing with a non-overlapping RGB-T pattern (NORP). To simulate full-view (0$^{\circ}$--360$^{\circ}$) RGB-T attacks, we construct 3D RGB-T models for human and adversarial clothing. NORP is a new adversarial pattern design using distinct visible and thermal materials without overlap, avoiding the light reduction in overlapping RGB-T patterns (ORP). To optimize the NORP on adversarial clothing, we propose a spatial discrete-continuous optimization (SDCO) method. We systematically evaluated our method on RGB-T detectors with different fusion architectures, demonstrating high attack success rates both in the digital and physical worlds. Additionally, we introduce a fusion-stage ensemble method that enhances the transferability of adversarial attacks across unseen RGB-T detectors with different fusion architectures.

中文摘要翻译: 可见光-热红外（RGB-T）目标检测是自动驾驶等应用中的关键技术，多模态融合增强了在低光照等挑战性条件下的性能。然而，RGB-T检测器的安全性，特别是在物理世界中，在很大程度上被忽视了。本文提出了一种新颖的RGB-T物理攻击方法，使用具有非重叠RGB-T图案（NORP）的对抗服装。为了模拟全视角（0°–360°）的RGB-T攻击，我们构建了人体和对抗服装的3D RGB-T模型。NORP是一种新的对抗图案设计，使用不重叠的可见光和热红外材料，避免了重叠RGB-T图案（ORP）造成的光照衰减。为了优化对抗服装上的NORP，我们提出了空间离散-连续优化（SDCO）方法。我们在具有不同融合架构的RGB-T检测器上系统评估了该方法，在数字世界和物理世界均展示了高攻击成功率。此外，我们引入了一种融合阶段集成方法，增强了对抗攻击在未见过的、具有不同融合架构的RGB-T检测器之间的迁移性。

</details>


### [45] [Multi-Level Bidirectional Biomimetic Learning for EEG-Based Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04680)
*Jingtao Liu,Peiliang Gong,Chuhang Zheng,Yiheng Liu,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MB2L框架，通过多层级双向仿生学习（含自适应模糊、仿生视觉特征提取和双向对比学习）解决脑电图-图像跨模态检索中配对数据有限和感知-结构失配的问题，在零样本检索任务上达到80.5% Top-1和97.6% Top-5准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有脑电图视觉神经解码面临两大挑战：一是配对数据有限导致跨模态对齐困难；二是高保真数字图像与生物视觉感知之间存在根本性失配（由视网膜映射和个体神经解剖差异造成），严重阻碍跨模态对齐。

Method: 提出MB2L多层级双向仿生学习框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应模糊与视觉先验，根据视网膜先验重加权视觉输入以缓解感知-结构失配；(2) 仿生视觉特征提取，学习与层级皮层处理一致的、具有主体不变性的多层级视觉表征；(3) 多层级双向对比学习，通过双向对比目标在共享语义空间中对齐脑电图与视觉特征。

Result: 在零样本脑电图到图像检索任务上，MB2L达到80.5% Top-1和97.6% Top-5准确率，显著优于先前方法，并在跨主体和跨实验设置下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将结构化生理归纳偏置引入表示学习能够有效弥合视觉感知与数字图像之间的差距，大幅提升脑电图视觉神经解码的性能和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: EEG-based visual neural decoding aims to align neural responses with visual stimuli for tasks such as image retrieval. However, limited paired data and a fundamental mismatch between high-fidelity digital images and biological visual perception - distorted by retinotopic mapping and subject-specific neuroanatomy - severely impede cross-modal alignment. To address this, we propose MB2L, a Multi-Level Bidirectional Biomimetic Learning framework that incorporates structured physiological inductive biases into representation learning. Specifically, we propose Adaptive Blur with Visual Priors to mitigate perceptual-structural mismatch by reweighting visual inputs according to retinotopic priors. We further propose Biomimetic Visual Feature Extraction to learn multi-level visual representations consistent with hierarchical cortical processing, enhancing subject-invariant encoding. These modules are jointly optimized via Multi-level Bidirectional Contrastive Learning, which aligns EEG and visual features in a shared semantic space through bidirectional contrastive objectives. Experiments show MB2L achieves 80.5% Top-1 and 97.6% Top-5 accuracy on zero-shot EEG-to-image retrieval, significantly outperforming prior methods and demonstrating strong generalization across subjects and experimental settings.

中文摘要翻译: 基于脑电图的视觉神经解码旨在将神经响应与视觉刺激对齐，以完成图像检索等任务。然而，有限的配对数据以及高保真数字图像与生物视觉感知之间的根本性失配——这种失配由视网膜映射和主体特异性神经解剖结构所导致——严重阻碍了跨模态对齐。为解决这一问题，我们提出了MB2L，一种多层级双向仿生学习框架，将结构化的生理归纳偏置引入表示学习。具体而言，我们提出了带有视觉先验的自适应模糊方法，通过根据视网膜先验重加权视觉输入来缓解感知-结构失配。我们进一步提出了仿生视觉特征提取方法，以学习与层级皮层处理一致的多层级视觉表征，从而增强主体不变性编码。这些模块通过多层级双向对比学习进行联合优化，该学习通过双向对比目标在共享语义空间中对齐脑电图和视觉特征。实验表明，MB2L在零样本脑电图到图像检索任务上达到了80.5%的Top-1和97.6%的Top-5准确率，显著优于先前方法，并在跨主体和跨实验设置下展现出强大的泛化能力。

</details>


### [46] [FaithfulFaces: Pose-Faithful Facial Identity Preservation for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04702)
*Yuanzhi Wang,Xuhua Ren,Jiaxiang Cheng,Bing Ma,Kai Yu,Sen Liang,Wenyue Li,Tianxiang Zheng,Qinglin Lu,Zhen Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FaithfulFaces框架，通过姿态共享身份对齐器与欧拉角嵌入，在复杂动态场景下实现姿态忠实的人脸身份保持视频生成，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有身份保持文本到视频生成方法在大姿态变化或面部遮挡下存在严重的身份扭曲问题，缺乏对复杂动态场景中姿态变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FaithfulFaces框架，核心是姿态共享身份对齐器（pose-shared identity aligner），包含姿态共享字典和姿态变化-身份不变性约束；通过将单视角输入映射为带显式欧拉角嵌入的全局人脸姿态表示，提供姿态忠实的面部先验，引导基座模型实现鲁棒的身份保持生成；同时构建了包含丰富面部姿态多样性的高质量视频数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明FaithfulFaces达到最先进性能，在发生姿态变化和遮挡时仍能保持优异的身份一致性和结构清晰度。

Conclusion: FaithfulFaces通过姿态共享的身份对齐机制有效解决了大姿态变化和遮挡下的身份保持问题，为复杂动态场景中的身份保持视频生成提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Identity-preserving text-to-video generation (IPT2V) empowers users to produce diverse and imaginative videos with consistent human facial identity. Despite recent progress, existing methods often suffer from significant identity distortion under large facial pose variations or facial occlusions. In this paper, we propose \textit{FaithfulFaces}, a pose-faithful facial identity preservation learning framework to improve IPT2V in complex dynamic scenes. The key of FaithfulFaces is a pose-shared identity aligner that refines and aligns facial poses across distinct views via a pose-shared dictionary and a pose variation-identity invariance constraint. By mapping single-view inputs into a global facial pose representation with explicit Euler angle embeddings, FaithfulFaces provides a pose-faithful facial prior that guides generative foundations toward robust identity-preserving generation. In particular, we develop a specialized pipeline to curate a high-quality video dataset featuring substantial facial pose diversity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FaithfulFaces achieves state-of-the-art performance, maintaining superior identity consistency and structural clarity even as pose changes and occlusions occur.

中文摘要翻译: 身份保持的文本到视频生成（IPT2V）使用户能够生成具有一致人脸的多样化创意视频。尽管近期取得了进展，但现有方法在面部姿态大幅变化或面部遮挡下常出现严重的身份扭曲。本文提出FaithfulFaces，一个姿态忠实的面部身份保持学习框架，旨在改善复杂动态场景中的IPT2V。FaithfulFaces的核心是一个姿态共享身份对齐器，通过姿态共享字典和姿态变化-身份不变性约束，对不同视角下的面部姿态进行精炼和对齐。通过将单视角输入映射为带有显式欧拉角嵌入的全局面部姿态表示，FaithfulFaces提供了姿态忠实的面部先验，引导生成基座模型实现鲁棒的身份保持生成。特别地，我们开发了一套专用流程，构建了一个具有丰富面部姿态多样性的高质量视频数据集。大量实验表明，FaithfulFaces达到了最先进性能，即使在姿态变化和遮挡发生时，仍能保持优异的身份一致性和结构清晰度。

</details>


### [47] [ULF-Loc: Unbiased Landmark Feature for Robust Visual Localization with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04730)
*Yingdong Gu,Shaocheng Yan,Zhenjun Zhao,Yuan Kou,Jianxin Luo,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3D高斯泼溅特征学习中的偏差问题，提出无偏地标特征框架ULF-Loc，通过几何加权特征融合替代有偏的α混合优化，在保持高效率的同时显著提升视觉定位精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的定位方法在特征学习中存在固有偏差，这是由于α混合优化导致单个高斯与其邻近高斯产生纠缠，使得学习到的特征不适合精确匹配任务。

Method: 1) 提出无偏地标特征框架ULF-Loc，用几何加权特征融合替代有偏的α混合优化；2) 引入关键点一致性地标采样选择可靠高斯；3) 采用局部几何一致性验证拒绝由渲染伪影导致的误匹配。

Result: 在Cambridge Landmarks数据集上，ULF-Loc的平均中位平移误差相比SOTA降低17%，同时训练时间仅需1/10、GPU内存仅需1/6。

Conclusion: 理论分析了3DGS特征学习中的偏差来源，提出的ULF-Loc通过无偏特征框架有效解决了该问题，在精度和效率上均达到领先水平。

Original Abstract: Visual localization is a core technology for augmented reality and autonomous navigation. Recent methods combine the efficient rendering of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with feature-based localization. These methods rely on direct matching between 2D query features and the 3D Gaussian feature field, but this often results in mismatches due to an inherent bias in the learned Gaussian feature. We theoretically analyze the feature learning process in 3DGS, revealing that the widely adopted $α$-blending optimization inherently introduces bias into 3D point features. This bias stems from the entanglement between individual Gaussians and their neighboring Gaussians, making the learned features unsuitable for precise matching tasks. Motivated by these findings, we propose ULF-Loc, an unbiased landmark feature framework that replaces biased feature optimization with geometry-weighted feature fusion. We further introduce keypoint-consensus landmark sampling to select reliable Gaussians and local geometric consistency verification to reject mismatches caused by rendering artifacts. On the Cambridge Landmarks dataset, ULF-Loc reduces the mean median translation error by 17\% compared to the state-of-the-art, while achieving superior efficiency with only 1/10 the training time and 1/6 the GPU memory of STDLoc.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉定位是增强现实和自主导航的核心技术。近期方法将3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的高效渲染与基于特征的定位相结合。这些方法依赖于2D查询特征与3D高斯特征场之间的直接匹配，但由于学习到的高斯特征存在固有偏差，常常导致误匹配。我们从理论上分析了3DGS中的特征学习过程，揭示了广泛采用的α混合优化在3D点特征中固有地引入了偏差。这种偏差源于单个高斯与其邻近高斯之间的纠缠，使得学习到的特征不适合精确匹配任务。受上述发现启发，我们提出ULF-Loc，一种无偏地标特征框架，用几何加权特征融合替代有偏的特征优化。我们进一步引入关键点一致性地标采样来选择可靠的高斯，以及局部几何一致性验证来拒绝由渲染伪影引起的误匹配。在Cambridge Landmarks数据集上，ULF-Loc的平均中位平移误差相比当前最优方法降低17%，同时实现了卓越的效率——训练时间仅为STDLoc的1/10，GPU内存仅为STDLoc的1/6。

</details>


### [48] [VC-FeS: Viewpoint-Conditioned Feature Selection for Vehicle Re-identification in Thermal Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04750)
*Yasod Ginige,Ransika Gunasekara,Darsha Hewavitharana,Manjula Ariyarathne,Peshala Jayasekara,Ranga Rodrigo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视角条件和区域特定特征比较的方法，利用RGB预训练ViT提取器有效适配热成像域，解决热成像中同类物体相似度高、纹理信息弱及视角变化导致的识别难题，在车辆和船舶数据集上分别超越SOTA 19.7%和12.8%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 热成像等单通道图像中，同类物体缺乏颜色信息且纹理信息弱，导致现有方法识别性能差；同时视角变化使特征差异增大，进一步增加了识别难度。

Method: 构建视角条件特征向量，并在不同特征空间中进行区域特定特征比较，从而利用现有RGB预训练的ViT特征提取器，将其适配到热成像领域的特定挑战。

Result: 在RGBNT100（IR）车辆数据集和自采的热成像船舶数据集上，mAP分别超越SOTA方法19.7%和12.8%。同时将公开首个用于船舶识别的热成像数据集。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效利用RGB预训练ViT在热成像域进行迁移，通过视角条件和区域特征比较显著提升了少纹理、无色彩条件下的物体识别性能。

Original Abstract: Identification of less-articulated objects using single-channel images, such as thermal images, is important in many applications, such as surveillance. However, in this domain, existing methods show poor performance due to high similarity among objects of the same category in the absence of color information (overlooking shape information) and de-emphasized texture information. Furthermore, variability in viewpoint adds more complexity as the features vary from side to side. We address these issues by constructing viewpoint-conditioned feature vectors and area-specific feature comparisons in separate feature spaces. These interventions enable leveraging the advancements of existing RGB-pre-trained ViT feature extractors while effectively adapting them to address the challenges specific to the thermal domain. We test our system with RGBNT100 (IR) vehicle dataset and a thermal maritime dataset acquired by us. Our results surpass the state-of-the-art methods by 19.7% and 12.8% for the above datasets in mAP scores, respectively. We also plan to make our thermal dataset available, the first of its kind for maritime vessel identification.

中文摘要翻译: 在监控等许多应用中，利用单通道图像（如热成像）识别低清晰度物体非常重要。然而，由于缺乏颜色信息（忽略了形状信息）且纹理信息被弱化，同类物体之间高度相似，现有方法在该领域表现较差。此外，视角的变化使得特征从一侧到另一侧发生变化，进一步增加了复杂性。我们通过在不同特征空间中构建视角条件特征向量和区域特定特征比较来解决这些问题。这些干预措施使得我们能够利用现有RGB预训练ViT特征提取器的优势，同时有效地将其适配到热成像领域特有的挑战。我们使用RGBNT100（红外）车辆数据集和我们采集的热成像船舶数据集对系统进行了测试。在上述数据集上，我们的结果在mAP分数上分别超越最先进的方法19.7%和12.8%。我们还计划公开我们的热成像数据集，这是首个用于船舶识别的此类数据集。

</details>


### [49] [Lightweight Cross-Spectral Face Recognition via Contrastive Alignment and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04769)
*Anjith George,Sebastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对异质人脸识别中现有方法计算开销大、难以部署于边缘设备的问题，提出了一种轻量级混合CNN-Transformer框架，仅需少量配对异质数据即可高效端到端训练，在保持低计算需求的同时实现了与最先进方法相当或更优的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有异质人脸识别方法虽性能显著提升，但多依赖计算开销大的模型，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，亟需一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Method: 将原本用于RGB同质人脸识别的混合CNN-Transformer模型进行适配改造，构建轻量级异质人脸识别框架，支持仅用少量配对异质数据进行高效端到端训练。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的异质人脸识别和标准人脸识别基准上，该方法在保持低计算需求的同时，达到了最先进或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具同质和异质场景下的适用性，为边缘设备上的异质人脸识别提供了一种高效实用的轻量级方案。

Original Abstract: Heterogeneous Face Recognition (HFR) aims at matching face images captured across different sensing modalities, such as thermal-to-visible or near-infrared-to-visible, enhancing the usability of face recognition systems in challenging real-world conditions. Although recent HFR methods have achieved significant improvements in performance, many rely on computationally expensive models, making them impractical for deployment on resource-limited edge devices. In this work, we introduce a lightweight yet effective HFR framework by adapting a hybrid CNN-Transformer model originally developed for RGB homogeneous face recognition. Our approach enables efficient end-to-end training with only a small amount of paired heterogeneous data, while still maintaining strong performance on standard RGB face recognition benchmarks. This makes it suitable for both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings. Comprehensive experiments on several challenging HFR and face recognition benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance while keeping computational requirements low.

中文摘要翻译: 异质人脸识别旨在匹配不同传感模态（如热成像到可见光或近红外到可见光）下采集的人脸图像，从而增强人脸识别系统在复杂现实条件下的可用性。尽管近年来的异质人脸识别方法在性能上取得了显著提升，但许多方法依赖计算开销大的模型，使其难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上。本文通过适配一个原本用于RGB同质人脸识别的混合CNN-Transformer模型，提出了一种轻量级且有效的异质人脸识别框架。我们的方法仅需少量配对异质数据即可实现高效的端到端训练，同时在标准RGB人脸识别基准上依然保持强劲性能。这使得该方法同时适用于同质和异质场景。在多个具有挑战性的异质人脸识别和人脸识别基准上的综合实验表明，我们的方法在保持低计算需求的同时，达到了最先进或具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [50] [Gaze4HRI: Zero-shot Benchmarking Gaze Estimation Neural-Networks for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04770)
*Berk Sezer,Ali Görkem Küçük,Erol Şahin,Sinan Kalkan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gaze4HRI——一个面向人机交互场景的大规模3D视线估计基准数据集，系统评估了现有零样本方法在光照、头-视线冲突、相机运动和目标运动等条件下的表现。实验发现所有方法均存在至少一种失效情况，其中陡峭向下视线是普遍失效点；而基于ETH-X-Gaze训练的PureGaze在其余条件下保持鲁棒性。研究表明数据多样性而非复杂时空建模是零样本鲁棒性的首要驱动力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有零样本外观3D视线估计方法在人机交互场景下的可靠性未经验证，现有基准忽略了动态相机视角、视频中移动目标等关键HRI条件，且跨数据集评估存在复杂度差距，无法真正评估模型鲁棒性。

Method: 构建大规模数据集Gaze4HRI（50+被试、3000+视频、60万+帧），系统设计光照、头-视线冲突、相机运动、目标运动等关键HRI变量条件，对多种SOTA方法进行标准化评估。分析PureGaze的自我对抗损失（self-adversarial loss）在视线特征净化中的作用。

Result: 所有评估方法至少在一个条件下失败；陡峭向下视线是所有方法的共同失效点；PureGaze（基于ETH-X-Gaze训练）在其他所有条件下保持唯一鲁棒性；数据多样性比复杂空间-时间建模和Transformer架构更能提升零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 数据多样性是零样本视线估计在非约束环境下鲁棒性的首要驱动因素，PureGaze的自我对抗损失框架提供了进一步的增强效果。该基准为实践者提供了实用指南，并重塑未来研究方向。

Original Abstract: While zero-shot appearance-based 3D gaze estimation offers significant cost-efficiency by directly mapping RGB images to gaze vectors, its reliability in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) settings remains uncertain. Existing benchmarks frequently overlook fundamental HRI conditions, such as dynamic camera viewpoints and moving targets in video. Furthermore, current cross-dataset evaluations often suffer from a complexity gap, where methods trained on diverse datasets are tested on significantly smaller and less varied sets, failing to assess true robustness. To bridge these gaps, we introduce Gaze4HRI, a large-scale dataset (50+ subjects, 3,000+ videos, 600,000+ frames) designed to evaluate state-of-the-art performance against critical HRI variables: illumination, head-gaze conflict, as well as the motion of camera and gaze target in video. Our benchmark reveals that all evaluated methods fail in at least one condition, identifying steeply-downward gaze as a universal failure point. Notably, PureGaze trained on the ETH-X-Gaze dataset uniquely maintains resilience across all other conditions. These results challenge the recent focus in the literature on complex spatial-temporal modeling and Transformer-based architectures. Instead, our findings suggest that extensive data diversity, as exemplified by the ETH-X-Gaze dataset, serves as the primary driver of zero-shot robustness in unconstrained environments, while resilience-enhancing frameworks, such as PureGaze's self-adversarial loss for gaze feature purification, provide a substantial further improvement. Ultimately, this study establishes a rigorous benchmark that provides practical guidelines for practitioners as well as reshaping future research. The dataset and codes are available at https://gazeforhri.github.io.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然零样本外观三维视线估计通过将RGB图像直接映射到视线向量具有显著的成本效率，但其在人机交互环境中的可靠性仍然不确定。现有基准常常忽略基本的人机交互条件，如动态相机视角和视频中的移动目标。此外，当前的跨数据集评估经常存在复杂度差距——在多样化数据集上训练的方法被在显著更小、更少变化的测试集上评估，未能衡量真正的鲁棒性。为弥合这些差距，我们提出了Gaze4HRI——一个大规模数据集（50名以上被试、3000多个视频、60万帧以上），旨在评估最先进方法在面对关键人机交互变量时的性能：光照条件、头-视线冲突，以及相机和目标在视频中的运动。我们的基准测试揭示，所有被评估的方法至少在一个条件下失败，其中陡峭向下视线被识别为所有方法的共同失效点。值得注意的是，基于ETH-X-Gaze数据集训练的PureGaze在其余所有条件下保持唯一的鲁棒性。这些结果挑战了文献近期对复杂时空建模和基于Transformer架构的关注。相反，我们的发现表明，广泛的数多样性（如ETH-X-Gaze数据集所体现）是非约束环境下零样本鲁棒性的首要驱动因素，而韧性增强框架（例如PureGaze用于视线特征净化的自我对抗损失）则提供了进一步的实质性改进。最终，本研究建立了一个严格的基准，为实践者提供了实用指南，同时也重塑了未来的研究方向。数据集和代码已公开于https://gazeforhri.github.io。

</details>


### [51] [MIRAGE: Retrieval and Generation of Multimodal Images and Texts for Medical Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04772)
*Miguel Diaz Benito,Cecilia Diana Albelda,Alvaro Garcia Martin,Jesus Bescos Cano,Marcos Escudero-Vinolo,Juan C. SanMiguel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE系统，一个基于公开预训练模型的多模态医学图像检索与生成工具，通过CLIP、扩散模型和大语言模型结合，为医学生提供无需编程技能的交互式可视化学习平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的医学图谱缺乏交互性且体积庞大，而在线图像搜索又存在标注错误或材料不完整的问题，医学生缺乏一个免费、透明、易用的交互式视觉学习工具。

Method: 基于微调的医学版CLIP（MedICaT-ROCO）将文本和图像映射到共享潜在空间实现语义检索；通过医学扩散模型（Prompt2MedImage）生成合成图像；利用大语言模型（Dolly-v2-3b）生成丰富的描述信息；支持双搜索模式以对比不同医学状况。

Result: 系统已部署在Kaggle平台上，完全基于公开预训练模型构建，无需本地计算资源或编程技能，可供全球医学生使用。

Conclusion: MIRAGE作为一个免费、透明、易用的教学工具，通过图像检索与生成为医学生提供交互式、个性化的视觉学习体验，具有良好的可复现性和可访问性。

Original Abstract: Access to diverse, well-annotated medical images with interactive learning tools is fundamental for training practitioners in medicine and related fields to improve their diagnostic skills and understanding of anatomical structures. While medical atlases are valuable, they are often impractical due to their size and lack of interactivity, whereas online image search may provide mislabeled or incomplete material. To address this, we propose MIRAGE, a multimodal medical text and image retrieval and generation system that allows users to find and generate clinically relevant images from trustworthy sources by mapping both text and images to a shared latent space, enabling semantically meaningful queries. The system is based on a fine-tuned medical version of CLIP (MedICaT-ROCO), trained with the ROCO dataset, obtained from PubMed Central. MIRAGE allows users to give prompts to retrieve images, generate synthetic ones through a medical diffusion model (Prompt2MedImage) and receive enriched descriptions from a large language model (Dolly-v2-3b). It also supports a dual search option, enabling the visual comparison of different medical conditions. A key advantage of the system is that it relies entirely on publicly available pretrained models, ensuring reproducibility and accessibility. Our goal is to provide a free, transparent and easy-to-use didactic tool for medical students, especially those without programming skills. The system features an interface that enables interactive and personalized visual learning through medical image retrieval and generation. The system is accessible to medical students worldwide without requiring local computational resources or technical expertise, and is currently deployed on Kaggle: http://www-vpu.eps.uam.es/mirage

中文摘要翻译: 获取多样化、标注完善的医学图像并配备交互式学习工具，对于培养医学及相关领域从业者的诊断技能和解剖结构理解至关重要。尽管医学图谱很有价值，但它们通常因体积庞大且缺乏交互性而不实用，而在线图像搜索则可能提供错误标注或不完整的材料。为解决这一问题，我们提出了MIRAGE——一个多模态医学文本与图像检索及生成系统，允许用户通过将文本和图像映射到共享潜在空间，从可信来源查找和生成临床相关图像，从而实现有语义意义的查询。该系统基于微调的医学版CLIP（MedICaT-ROCO），使用从PubMed Central获取的ROCO数据集进行训练。MIRAGE允许用户通过提示词检索图像，通过医学扩散模型（Prompt2MedImage）生成合成图像，并通过大语言模型（Dolly-v2-3b）获取丰富的描述信息。它还支持双搜索选项，能够对不同医学状况进行视觉比较。该系统的一个关键优势是其完全依赖公开可用的预训练模型，确保了可复现性和可访问性。我们的目标是提供一个免费、透明且易用的教学工具，特别是面向没有编程技能的医学生。该系统具备一个界面，能够通过医学图像检索和生成实现交互式、个性化的视觉学习。该系统可供全球医学生使用，无需本地计算资源或专业技术知识，目前已部署在Kaggle平台上：http://www-vpu.eps.uam.es/mirage

</details>


### [52] [QuadBox: Accelerating 3D Gaussian Splatting with Geometry-Aware Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04844)
*Xinze Li,Bohan Yang,Pengxu Chen,Yiyuan Wang,Hongcheng Luo,Wentao Cheng,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuadBox方法，通过四个轴对齐包围盒紧密封装投影高斯体，并引入单遍瓦片遍历算法QPass，在保持渲染质量的同时将3DGS渲染速度提升1.85倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的光栅化管线中，高效计算精确的高斯体-瓦片交集是一个关键挑战，现有方法在该任务上计算效率不足。

Method: 1) 推导几何感知的拉伸因子，构建与瓦片对齐的QuadBox，紧密覆盖椭圆投影并排除无关瓦片；2) 提出QPass，一种利用QuadBox离散特性的单遍瓦片遍历算法，仅需简单区间测试即可完成瓦片交集检查。

Result: 在公开数据集上，该方法将3DGS的渲染速度加速了1.85倍。

Conclusion: QuadBox通过离散化包围盒策略显著提升了3DGS渲染管线中高斯体-瓦片交集计算的效率，实现了渲染速度的大幅提升。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an advanced technique for real-time novel view synthesis by representing scene geometry and appearance using differentiable Gaussian primitives. However, efficiently computing precise Gaussian-tile intersections remains a critical task in the rasterization pipeline. To this end, we propose QuadBox, a method that leverages four axis-aligned bounding boxes to tightly encapsulate projected Gaussians in a discrete manner. First, we derive a geometry-aware stretching factor that enables the construction of a tile-aligned QuadBox, which covers the elliptical projection and largely excludes irrelevant tiles. Second, we introduce QPass, a single-pass tile traversal algorithm that exhaustively exploits the discrete nature of QuadBox, ensuring that the tile intersection check is performed with simple interval tests. Experiments on public datasets show that our method accelerates the rendering speed of 3DGS by 1.85$\times$. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}{https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox}.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）已成为一种先进的技术，通过使用可微分高斯原语表示场景几何和外观，实现实时新颖视图合成。然而，在光栅化管线中高效计算精确的高斯体-瓦片交集仍然是一项关键任务。为此，我们提出QuadBox方法，该方法利用四个轴对齐包围盒以离散方式紧密封装投影后的高斯体。首先，我们推导了一个几何感知的拉伸因子，从而能够构建与瓦片对齐的QuadBox，该包围盒覆盖椭圆投影并大幅度排除无关瓦片。其次，我们引入QPass，一种单遍瓦片遍历算法，该算法充分利用了QuadBox的离散特性，确保仅通过简单的区间测试即可执行瓦片交集检查。在公开数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法将3DGS的渲染速度加速了1.85倍。代码已开源在https://github.com/Powertony102/QuadBox。

</details>


### [53] [VTAgent: Agentic Keyframe Anchoring for Evidence-Aware Video TextVQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04870)
*Haibin He,Maoyuan Ye,Jing Zhang,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于问题引导的关键帧定位框架，通过显式锚定与问题相关的关键帧来解决视频文本VQA中定位瓶颈问题，在无训练、SFT和RL设置下均显著超越直接视频推理，取得新的SOTA结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Video-LLMs在视频文本VQA任务上表现有限，作者通过逐帧问答的上界分析发现性能瓶颈主要在于关键证据的定位能力而非推理能力本身，因此需要显式的关键帧定位机制来弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出问题引导的智能体框架，首先通过问题引导显式定位与问题相关的关键帧，再基于这些关键帧进行答案推理。该框架可在无训练（training-free）模式下运行，也可进一步结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）进行优化。

Result: 在多个基准上，相比直接视频推理，该方法在无训练设置下持续提升；结合SFT和RL后，准确率平均提升+12.12，ANLS平均提升+11.15，达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式的关键帧定位对于推进视频文本VQA至关重要，定位瓶颈是当前模型性能受限的主要原因，所提框架有效解决了该问题。

Original Abstract: Video text-based visual question answering (Video TextVQA) aims to answer questions by reasoning over visual textual content appearing in videos. Despite the strong multimodal video understanding capabilities of recent Video-LLMs, their performance on existing Video TextVQA benchmarks remains limited. To better understand this gap, we conduct an upper-bound analysis through frame-wise question answering, counting a sample as correct if any frame yields the right answer, which significantly outperforms direct video-based inference and reveals a substantial performance gap. The results suggest that the primary bottleneck lies in the localization of key question-relevant evidence, rather than in reasoning capacity itself. Building on this insight, we propose a question-guided agent framework that explicitly anchors the relevant keyframes before answering. The approach operates effectively in a training-free setting and consistently surpasses direct video inference. With additional supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), it achieves an average improvement of +12.12 in accuracy and +11.15 in ANLS across benchmarks, establishing new state-of-the-art results. Our study underscores the critical role of explicit keyframe anchoring for advancing Video TextVQA. The code will be publicly released.

中文摘要翻译: 视频文本视觉问答（Video TextVQA）旨在通过推理视频中出现的视觉文本内容来回答问题。尽管最近的Video-LLMs具有强大的多模态视频理解能力，但它们在现有Video TextVQA基准上的表现仍然有限。为了更好地理解这一差距，我们通过逐帧问答进行了上界分析——将任一帧给出正确答案的样本计为正确，该方法显著优于直接基于视频的推理，揭示了一个巨大的性能差距。结果表明，主要瓶颈在于关键问题相关证据的定位能力，而非推理能力本身。基于这一发现，我们提出了一种问题引导的智能体框架，在回答之前显式地锚定相关关键帧。该方法在无需训练的设置下即可有效运行，并持续超越直接视频推理。结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）后，该方法在多个基准上实现了准确率平均提升+12.12、ANLS平均提升+11.15，取得了新的最先进结果。我们的研究强调了显式关键帧锚定对于推进Video TextVQA的关键作用。代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [54] [DART: A Vision-Language Foundation Model for Comprehensive Rope Condition Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04943)
*Anju Rani,Daniel Ortiz-Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART是一个视觉-语言基础模型，通过统一多任务架构实现合成纤维绳索的全面状态监测，包括损伤分类、严重程度回归、少样本识别等，无需任务特定微调。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 合成纤维绳索的状态监测需要超越简单分类的能力，包括连续严重程度估计、维护建议、异常标记、劣化时间线和自动报告，而现有方法无法从单张检测图像中同时提供这些信息。

Method: DART扩展了联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）至跨模态领域，通过Severity-Conditioned Cross-Modal Fusion (SC-CMF)模块耦合Vision Transformer (ViT-H/14)与Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct。三个创新：（1）HD-MASK，基于显著性引导的掩码策略；（2）每类可学习的严重程度门控；（3）对比损伤解缠（CDD）损失。

Result: 在4,270张图像、14个细粒度损伤类别上训练，冻结的DART骨干支持下游任务：损伤分类准确率93.22%、宏F1 91.04%；严重程度回归Spearman ρ=0.94、1阶内准确率99.6%；20样本少样本识别宏F1 89.2%。

Conclusion: DART作为一个通用CM骨干，超越了传统分类功能，能够从单一共享表示中提供可操作的检测智能。

Original Abstract: The condition monitoring (CM) of synthetic fibre ropes (SFRs) used in offshore, maritime, and industrial settings demands more than a classifier: inspectors need continuous severity estimates, maintenance recommendations, anomaly flags, deterioration timelines, and automated reports, all from a single inspection image. We present DART (Damage Assessment via Rope Transformer), a vision-language foundation model that addresses the full rope inspection workflow through a unified multi-task architecture. DART extends the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) to the cross-modal domain by coupling a Vision Transformer (ViT-H/14) with Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct via a Severity-Conditioned Cross-Modal Fusion (SC-CMF) module. Three architectural innovations drive the model's versatility: (1) HD-MASK, a saliency-guided masking strategy that focuses self-supervised reconstruction on damage-dense patches; (2) per-class learnable severity gates that adaptively weight language grounding by damage category; and (3) a Contrastive Damage Disentanglement (CDD) loss that shapes the embedding space to simultaneously encode damage type, severity ordering, and cross-modal semantics. Trained once on 4,270 images spanning 14 fine-grained rope damage classes, the frozen DART backbone supports downstream tasks without any task-specific fine-tuning: damage classification (93.22 % accuracy, 91.04 % macro-F1, +38.5 pp over a vision-only baseline), continuous severity regression (Spearman rho = 0.94, within-1-ordinal accuracy 99.6 %), few-shot recognition (89.2 % macro-F1 at 20 shots). These results demonstrate that DART functions as a general-purpose CM backbone that goes well beyond classification, providing actionable inspection intelligence from a single shared representation.

中文摘要翻译: 用于海上、海事和工业环境中合成纤维绳索（SFRs）的状态监测（CM）需要的不仅仅是分类器：检查员需要从单张检测图像中获取连续的严重程度估计、维护建议、异常标记、劣化时间线和自动化报告。我们提出了DART（基于绳索Transformer的损伤评估），这是一个视觉-语言基础模型，通过统一的多任务架构解决完整的绳索检测工作流程。DART将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）扩展到跨模态领域，通过严重程度条件跨模态融合（SC-CMF）模块将Vision Transformer（ViT-H/14）与Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct相结合。三个架构创新驱动了模型的通用性：（1）HD-MASK，一种显著性引导的掩码策略，将自监督重建聚焦于损伤密集的图像块；（2）每类可学习的严重程度门控，按损伤类别自适应地加权语言基础；（3）对比损伤解缠（CDD）损失，塑造嵌入空间以同时编码损伤类型、严重程度排序和跨模态语义。DART在涵盖14个细粒度绳索损伤类别的4,270张图像上一次性训练，冻结的DART骨干支持下游任务而无需任何任务特定的微调：损伤分类（准确率93.22%，宏F1 91.04%，比纯视觉基线高38.5个百分点）、连续严重程度回归（Spearman ρ=0.94，1阶序数内准确率99.6%）、少样本识别（20样本宏F1 89.2%）。这些结果表明，DART作为一个通用CM骨干，超越了传统分类功能，能够从单一共享表示中提供可操作的检测智能。

</details>


### [55] [ICPR 2026 Competition on Privacy-Preserving Person Re-Identification from Top-View RGB-Depth Camera (TVRID)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04977)
*Raphaël Delécluse,Hazem Wannous,Laurent Guimas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛，该竞赛聚焦于隐私感知的俯视行人重识别，发布了包含86个身份、RGB-Depth配对数据的多视角数据集，并设定了RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID和跨模态检索三个赛道，实验结果表明难度排序为RGB > Depth > 跨模态，验证了模态不变学习的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 推动隐私感知场景下的俯视行人重识别研究，填补该领域缺乏标准化大规模RGB-Depth数据集的空白，并为跨模态行人检索提供可复现的基准测试平台。

Method: 使用四台同步的Intel RealSense D455顶视摄像头采集RGB与Depth配对流数据，涵盖平坦、上行、下行和倾斜四种几何视角变化；设定RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID及RGB↔Depth跨模态检索三个评测赛道，采用mAP和CMC-1指标进行统一服务器端评估。

Result: 最终结果显示出清晰的难度排序：RGB Re-ID > Depth Re-ID > 跨模态检索，表明模态约束下的检索更具挑战性，而利用模态不变学习可获得强性能。

Conclusion: TVRID为俯视、深度及跨模态行人重识别建立了一个可复现的基准测试平台，并公开了数据集、评估脚本及配套文档，以促进该领域后续研究。

Original Abstract: This companion paper reports the ICPR 2026 TVRID competition on privacy-aware top-view person re-identification. We present the competition setting, the released RGB-Depth dataset, and a summary of final results with descriptions of the top entries. TVRID contains 86 identities captured by four synchronized overhead Intel RealSense D455 cameras, with paired RGB/Depth streams and structured geometric variation across flat, ascent, descent, and oblique viewpoints. The evaluation protocol includes three tracks: RGB Re-ID, Depth Re-ID, and RGB$\leftrightarrow$Depth cross-modal retrieval. Submissions are ranked using mAP and CMC-1 under a unified server-side evaluation. The final results show a clear difficulty ordering (RGB $>$ Depth $>$ Cross-Modal), highlighting both the challenge of modality-constrained retrieval and the feasibility of strong performance with modality-invariant learning. By releasing the dataset at https://zenodo.org/records/17909410, the evaluation scripts at https://github.com/RaphaelDel/ICPR-TVRID, and the accompanying documentation, TVRID establishes a reproducible benchmark for top-view, depth-based, and cross-modal person re-id.

中文摘要翻译: 本配套论文报道了ICPR 2026 TVRID竞赛，该竞赛聚焦于隐私感知的俯视行人重识别。我们介绍了竞赛设置、发布的RGB-Depth数据集以及最终结果总结，包括对最优参赛方案的描述。TVRID包含由四台同步的顶置Intel RealSense D455相机捕获的86个身份，具有配对的RGB/Depth流以及涵盖平坦、上行、下行和倾斜视角的结构化几何变化。评估协议包括三个赛道：RGB Re-ID、Depth Re-ID以及RGB↔Depth跨模态检索。提交结果通过统一的服务器端评估，以mAP和CMC-1指标进行排名。最终结果显示出一个清晰的难度排序（RGB > Depth > 跨模态），既突出了模态约束下检索的挑战性，也验证了利用模态不变学习实现强性能的可行性。通过发布数据集（https://zenodo.org/records/17909410）、评估脚本（https://github.com/RaphaelDel/ICPR-TVRID）以及配套文档，TVRID为俯视、深度及跨模态行人重识别建立了一个可复现的基准测试平台。

</details>


### [56] [Attention-Based Chaotic Self-Supervision for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04985)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Amanda Pontes de Oliveira Ornelas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE）作为医学图像分类的自监督预训练策略，通过混沌变换替代随机掩码，并融合注意力机制结合通用与领域特征，在皮肤病变和糖尿病视网膜病变数据集上取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统自监督方法（如MAE）使用随机掩码可能无意中破坏医学图像中的细粒度诊断特征，需要一种能保留关键诊断信息的预训练策略。

Method: 提出混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE），对输入图像施加混沌变换而非随机掩码，训练自编码器重建原始图像；同时设计注意力融合机制，结合CDAE训练的编码器与标准编码器的特征，融合通用表示与领域特定表示。

Result: 在ISIC 2018上达到准确率0.9221、F1-macro 0.8530；在APTOS 2019上达到准确率0.8644、F1-macro 0.7433，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CDAE通过"逆混沌"方式迫使编码器学习鲁棒的领域特异性特征，结合注意力融合机制能有效提升医学图像分类性能。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification usually achieve promising results but typically rely on large, annotated datasets or standard transfer learning from ImageNet. Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful alternative, yet common methods like masked autoencoders (MAEs) may inadvertently destroy fine-grained diagnostic features by using random masking. In this paper, we propose a novel SSL pre-training strategy, the Chaotic Denoising Autoencoder (CDAE). Instead of masking, we apply a chaotic transformation to the input image, tasking an autoencoder to reconstruct the original. We hypothesize this forces the encoder to learn robust, domain-specific features by "inverting the chaos". Furthermore, we propose an attentive fusion mechanism that combines features from our CDAE-trained encoder with a standard encoder, leveraging the strengths of both general and domain-specific representations. Our method is evaluated on two public medical datasets: ISIC 2018 (skin lesions) and APTOS 2019 (diabetic retinopathy). The proposed model achieves high performance, with an accuracy of 0.9221 and an F1-macro of 0.8530 on ISIC 2018, and an accuracy of 0.8644 and F1-macro of 0.7433 on APTOS 2019, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

中文摘要翻译: 用于医学图像分类的深度学习模型通常能取得有前景的结果，但它们通常依赖于大规模标注数据集或来自ImageNet的标准迁移学习。自监督学习（SSL）已成为一种强大的替代方案，然而常用的方法如掩码自编码器（MAE）可能通过随机掩码无意中破坏细粒度的诊断特征。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的SSL预训练策略——混沌去噪自编码器（CDAE）。与掩码方式不同，我们对输入图像施加混沌变换，并训练自编码器重建原始图像。我们假设这种方法通过"逆混沌"迫使编码器学习鲁棒的领域特异性特征。此外，我们提出了一种注意力融合机制，将CDAE训练的编码器特征与标准编码器特征相结合，充分利用通用表示和领域特异性表示的优势。我们在两个公开医学数据集上进行评估：ISIC 2018（皮肤病变）和APTOS 2019（糖尿病视网膜病变）。所提出的模型取得了高性能，在ISIC 2018上准确率为0.9221，F1-macro为0.8530；在APTOS 2019上准确率为0.8644，F1-macro为0.7433，证明了我们方法的有效性。

</details>


### [57] [Low-Rank Adaptation of Geospatial Foundation Models for Wildfire Mapping Using Sentinel-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04989)
*Ali Shibli,Andrea Nascetti,Yifang Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三种地理空间基础模型（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）在野火烧毁区域制图任务中的表现，发现使用LoRA（低秩适配）方法仅更新不到1%的参数即可实现最强的跨域泛化性能，其中Prithvi-v2+LoRA组合取得最佳效果，为大规模野火烧毁区域制图提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管现有的地理空间基础模型为卫星图像提供了强大的通用表征能力，但如何高效地将这些模型适配到下游地球观测任务（尤其是在地理和时间域迁移场景下）仍缺乏清晰的认识和系统性的研究。

Method: 使用2017-2023年间3,820起野火事件的Sentinel-2数据，评估三种地理空间基础模型（Terramind、DINOv3、Prithvi-v2）在美加地区烧毁区域制图任务上的性能，系统比较了三种适配策略：全参数微调、仅解码器微调以及低秩适配（LoRA）。

Result: LoRA在所有实验中均表现出最强的跨域泛化能力，且仅更新不到1%的模型参数；Prithvi-v2结合LoRA取得了最高的整体精度，并且相比全参数微调取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 地理空间基础模型通过LoRA等轻量级参数高效方法进行适配，能够为大规模烧毁区域制图提供鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Wildfire burned-area mapping is essential for damage assessment, emissions modeling, and understanding fire-climate interactions across diverse ecological regions. Recent geospatial foundation models provide strong general-purpose representations for satellite imagery, yet there is still no clear understanding of how to efficiently adapt these models for downstream Earth observation tasks, particularly under geographic and temporal domain shift. This study evaluates three state-of-the-art Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) - Terramind, DINOv3, and Prithvi-v2 - for burned-area mapping across the United States and Canada using Sentinel-2 data. Leveraging 3,820 wildfire events from 2017-2023, we conduct spatial and temporal generalization tests across diverse biomes. We systematically compare full fine-tuning, decoder-only fine-tuning, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) for adapting each model. Across all experiments, LoRA provides the strongest cross-domain generalization while updating less than 1% of parameters, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and efficiency. Prithvi-v2 with LoRA achieves the highest overall accuracy and the largest improvement compared to full fine-tuning. These findings indicate that geospatial foundation models, when adapted using lightweight parameter-efficient methods such as LoRA, offer a robust and scalable solution for large-scale burned-area mapping. Code is available at https://github.com/alishibli97/wildfire-lora-gfm.

中文摘要翻译: 野火烧毁区域制图对于灾害评估、排放建模以及理解不同生态区域中火灾与气候的相互作用至关重要。近年来，地理空间基础模型为卫星图像提供了强大的通用表征能力，但对于如何高效地将这些模型适配到下游地球观测任务，特别是在地理和时间域迁移场景下，仍缺乏清晰的认识。本研究评估了三种最先进的地理空间基础模型（GFMs）——Terramind、DINOv3和Prithvi-v2，利用Sentinel-2数据在美加地区进行烧毁区域制图。基于2017年至2023年间3,820起野火事件，我们在多种生物群落中开展了空间和时间泛化测试。我们系统比较了全参数微调、仅解码器微调和低秩适配（LoRA）三种模型适配策略。在所有实验中，LoRA在更新不到1%参数的情况下实现了最强的跨域泛化能力，展示了准确性与效率之间的有利权衡。Prithvi-v2结合LoRA取得了最高的整体精度，并且相比全参数微调取得了最大的性能提升。这些发现表明，通过LoRA等轻量级参数高效方法进行适配的地理空间基础模型，能够为大规模烧毁区域制图提供鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案。代码可在 https://github.com/alishibli97/wildfire-lora-gfm 获取。

</details>


### [58] [CARD: A Multi-Modal Automotive Dataset for Dense 3D Reconstruction in Challenging Road Topography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05014)
*Gasser Elazab,Frank Neuhaus,Tilman Koß,Malte Splietker,Aditya Date,Michael Unterreiner,Maximilian Jansen,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARD是一个多模态驾驶数据集，提供准密集3D地面真值，覆盖减速带、坑洼、不规则路面和越野路段，通过多激光雷达融合每帧产生约50万有效深度像素，比KITTI深度补全多6.5倍，并提供了面向道路地形不规则性的2D边界框标注和标准化评估协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶数据集大多在良好铺装平坦道路上采集，且仅提供稀疏激光雷达地面真值，不足以评估深度估计与补全中的细粒度几何信息，缺乏覆盖不规则路面的高质量密集深度数据。

Method: 构建包含全局快门立体相机、前后激光雷达、LiDAR-惯性里程计6自由度位姿、车轮运动轨迹和完整标定的传感器套件，通过多激光雷达融合生成准密集3D地面真值，并提供面向道路地形不规则性的2D边界框标注。

Result: 多激光雷达融合每帧产生约50万有效深度像素，是KITTI深度补全的6.5倍，平均是其他公开驾驶数据集的10倍；数据集覆盖德国和意大利约110公里、4.7小时；建立了标准化评估协议并基准测试了最先进的深度估计模型。

Conclusion: CARD数据集弥补了现有驾驶数据集在不规则路面细粒度几何信息方面的空白，为深度估计和感知任务提供了更高质量的基准。

Original Abstract: Autonomous driving must operate across diverse surfaces to enable safe mobility. However, most driving datasets are captured on well-paved flat roads. Moreover, recent driving datasets primarily provide sparse LiDAR ground truth for images, which is insufficient for assessing fine-grained geometry in depth estimation and completion. To address these gaps, we introduce CARD, a multi-modal driving dataset that delivers quasi-dense 3D ground truth across continuous sequences rich in speed bumps, potholes, irregular surfaces and off-road segments. Our sensor suite includes synchronized global-shutter stereo cameras, front and rear LiDARs, 6-DoF poses from LiDAR-inertial odometry, per-wheel motion traces, and full calibration. Notably, our multi-LiDAR fusion yields ~500K valid depth pixels per frame, about 6.5x more than KITTI Depth Completion and 10x more on average than other public driving datasets. The dataset spans ~110 km and 4.7 hours across Germany and Italy. In addition, CARD provides 2D bounding boxes targeting road-topography irregularities, enabling accurate benchmarking for both geometry and perception tasks. Furthermore, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol for road surface irregularities on CARD and benchmark state-of-the-art depth estimation models to provide strong baselines. The CARD dataset is hosted on https://huggingface.co/CARD-Data.

中文摘要翻译: 自动驾驶必须在多样化的路面上运行才能实现安全移动。然而，大多数驾驶数据集都是在良好铺装的平坦道路上采集的。此外，最近的驾驶数据集主要为图像提供稀疏的激光雷达地面真值，这不足以评估深度估计和补全中的细粒度几何信息。为解决这些问题，我们提出了CARD，一个多模态驾驶数据集，在包含丰富减速带、坑洼、不规则路面和越野路段的连续序列上提供准密集的3D地面真值。我们的传感器套件包括同步全局快门立体相机、前后激光雷达、来自激光雷达-惯性里程计的六自由度位姿、每个车轮的运动轨迹以及完整的标定。值得注意的是，我们的多激光雷达融合每帧产生约50万个有效深度像素，比KITTI深度补全多约6.5倍，平均是其他公开驾驶数据集的10倍。该数据集覆盖德国和意大利约110公里、4.7小时。此外，CARD提供了针对道路地形不规则性的2D边界框标注，能够对几何和感知任务进行精确的基准测试。进一步地，我们在CARD上建立了针对路面不规则性的标准化评估协议，并基准测试了最先进的深度估计模型以提供强基准线。CARD数据集托管在https://huggingface.co/CARD-Data。

</details>


### [59] [Local Intrinsic Dimension Unveils Hallucinations in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05026)
*Bartlomiej Sobieski,Matthew Tivnan,Dawid Płudowski,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Pengfei Jin,Przemyslaw Biecek,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型中的结构幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性，通过局部内在维度（LID）识别其根源，并设计内在淬火（IQ）机制直接降低LID以缓解幻觉，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散模型生成的结构幻觉（如手指数量异常）破坏了底层结构规则，现有研究从模式插值等视角只提供了部分解释，缺乏统一的根本性理解。

Method: 提出互补视角：将结构幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性。基于这种不稳定性构建幻觉过滤器，优于时间过滤器。追溯不稳定性的根源为局部内在维度（LID），并设计内在淬火（Intrinsic Quenching, IQ）机制直接降低LID以缓解幻觉。

Result: 基于不稳定性构建的幻觉过滤器性能达到或超过最新提出的时间过滤器；IQ机制在多个基准测试中一致优于标准幻觉缓解基线方法，并在下游医学成像任务中有效保证解剖一致性。

Conclusion: 局部内在维度是结构幻觉的主要驱动因素，通过IQ机制主动降低LID能有效缓解幻觉，为扩散模型在医疗影像等要求高结构一致性的应用中提供了有前景的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models are prone to generating structural hallucinations - samples that match the statistical properties of the training data yet defy underlying structural rules, resulting in anomalies like hands with more than five fingers. Recent research studied this failure mode from several viewpoints, offering partial explanations to their occurrence, such as mode interpolation. In this work, we propose a complementary perspective that treats hallucinations as instabilities on the model-induced manifold. We begin by showing that a hallucination filter based on such instabilities matches or exceeds the performance of the recently proposed temporal one. By tracing the source of these instabilities, we identify local intrinsic dimension (LID) as their primary driver and propose Intrinsic Quenching (IQ), a direct corrective mechanism that deflates it to alleviate hallucinations. IQ consistently outperforms standard hallucination reduction baselines across a wide array of benchmarks and offers a highly promising solution for enforcing anatomical consistency in downstream medical imaging tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型容易生成结构幻觉——即样本虽匹配训练数据的统计特性，却违反底层结构规则，导致如手指超过五根等异常现象。近期研究从多个视角探讨了这一失效模式，为其发生提供了部分解释，例如模式插值。本文提出一个互补视角，将幻觉视为模型诱导流形上的不稳定性。我们首先证明，基于此类不稳定性构建的幻觉过滤器在性能上达到或超越了近期提出的时间过滤器。通过追溯这些不稳定性的来源，我们识别出局部内在维度（LID）是其主要驱动因素，并提出内在淬火（IQ）——一种直接降低LID以缓解幻觉的校正机制。IQ在广泛的基准测试中一致优于标准幻觉缓解基线方法，并为在下游医学成像任务中强制执行解剖一致性提供了一种极具前景的解决方案。

</details>


### [60] [Prompt-Anchored Vision-Text Distillation for Lifelong Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05027)
*Wen Wen,Hao Chen,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAD（Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation）框架，利用预训练视觉-语言模型中冻结的文本编码器作为语义锚点，通过非对称视觉-文本蒸馏实现终身行人重识别中的稳定性和可塑性平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无范例终身行人重识别方法多依赖纯视觉蒸馏或参数正则化，忽略了文本等辅助模态在保持语义稳定性和实现增量可塑性方面的潜力。

Method: 提出非对称视觉-文本蒸馏框架PAD。文本侧：冻结文本编码器，通过蒸馏prompt在固定语义空间中保持视觉-文本对齐，作为全局语义参考；视觉侧：采用基于EMA的教师模型与自适应prompt池，为新领域分配新槽位同时冻结旧槽位，实现领域级自适应。

Result: 在已知和未知领域上均显著优于现有最先进方法，实现了稳定性与可塑性的强平衡。

Conclusion: 冻结的视觉-语言模型文本编码器可作为跨领域的稳定语义锚点，非对称视觉-文本蒸馏策略能有效兼顾语义稳定性和增量适应能力。

Original Abstract: Lifelong person re-identification (LReID) aims to train a generalizable model with sequentially collected data. However, such models often suffer from semantic drift, limited adaptability, and catastrophic forgetting as new domains emerge. Existing exemplar-free approaches largely rely on visual-only distillation or parameter regularization, while overlooking the potential of auxiliary modalities, such as text, to preserve semantic stability and enable incremental plasticity. We observe that the frozen text encoder in pretrained vision-language models can serve as a stable semantic anchor across domains. To decouple the roles of vision and text, we propose Prompt-Anchored vision-text Distillation (PAD), an asymmetric vision-text framework for semantic alignment and cross-domain generalization. On the textual side, we distill prompts to preserve vision-text alignment under a fixed semantic space, acting as a global semantic reference rather than a dominant learning signal. On the visual side, an EMA-based teacher with an adaptive prompt pool enables domain-wise adaptation by allocating new slots while freezing past ones. Extensive experiments show that PAD substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods across seen and unseen domains, achieving a strong balance between stability and plasticity. Project page is available at https://github.com/zu-zi/PAD.

中文摘要翻译: 终身行人重识别（LReID）旨在利用顺序收集的数据训练具有泛化能力的模型。然而，随着新领域的出现，这类模型常常遭受语义漂移、适应性有限以及灾难性遗忘等问题。现有的无范例方法主要依赖于纯视觉蒸馏或参数正则化，忽视了文本等辅助模态在保持语义稳定性和实现增量可塑性方面的潜力。我们观察到，预训练视觉-语言模型中冻结的文本编码器可以充当跨领域的稳定语义锚点。为了解耦视觉和文本的角色，我们提出了基于提示锚定的视觉-文本蒸馏（PAD）——一种用于语义对齐和跨领域泛化的非对称视觉-文本框架。在文本侧，我们蒸馏提示（prompt）以在固定语义空间下保持视觉-文本对齐，作为全局语义参考而非主导学习信号。在视觉侧，基于指数移动平均（EMA）的教师模型配合自适应提示池，通过分配新槽位同时冻结旧槽位实现领域级自适应。大量实验表明，PAD在已知和未知领域上均大幅优于现有最先进方法，实现了稳定性与可塑性的强平衡。项目页面：https://github.com/zu-zi/PAD。

</details>


### [61] [When Relations Break: Analyzing Relation Hallucination in Vision-Language Model Under Rotation and Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05045)
*Philip Wootaek Shin,Ajay Narayanan Sridhar,Sivani Devarapalli,Rui Zhang,Jack Sampson,Vijaykrishnan Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉扰动（旋转和噪声）对视觉-语言模型关系幻觉的影响，发现即使是轻微的扰动也会显著降低关系推理能力，现有提示增强和预处理策略仅能部分缓解问题，表明当前VLM在感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间存在显著差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽然在多模态任务上表现强劲，但容易产生关系幻觉（relation hallucination），即对物体间交互关系的错误推理。本文旨在探究视觉扰动（旋转和噪声）如何影响这种关系推理能力，以及现有的增强和预处理策略能否有效缓解该问题。

Method: 本文通过引入两种视觉扰动（旋转和噪声）系统性地测试多种VLM在关系推理任务上的表现；同时评估了基于提示的增强策略以及预处理策略（方向校正和去噪）对缓解关系幻觉的效果。

Result: 即使轻微的视觉扰动也会显著降低模型的关系推理能力，且该现象在不同模型和数据集上一致存在；提示增强和预处理策略只能提供部分改善，无法完全消除关系幻觉。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间存在显著差距，亟需开发更具鲁棒性、对几何结构敏感的视觉-语言模型。
