Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong multimodal performance but remain prone to relation hallucination, which requires accurate reasoning over inter-object interactions. We study the impact of visual perturbations, specifically rotation and noise, and show that even mild distortions significantly degrade relational reasoning across models and datasets. We further evaluate prompt-based augmentation and preprocessing strategies (orientation correction and denoising), finding that while they offer partial improvements, they do not fully resolve hallucinations. Our results reveal a gap between perceptual robustness and relational understanding, highlighting the need for more robust, geometry-aware VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽能实现强大的多模态性能，但仍然容易产生关系幻觉（relation hallucination），即需要对物体间交互进行准确推理的能力不足。本文研究了视觉扰动（特别是旋转和噪声）的影响，发现即使是轻微的失真也会显著降低模型在多个模型和数据集上的关系推理能力。我们进一步评估了基于提示的增强策略以及预处理策略（方向校正和去噪），发现这些方法虽然能带来部分改善，但无法完全解决幻觉问题。我们的结果揭示了感知鲁棒性与关系理解之间的差距，突显了开发更具鲁棒性、对几何结构敏感的VLM的必要性。

</details>


### [62] [Direct Product Flow Matching: Decoupling Radial and Angular Dynamics for Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05054)
*Hongxu Chen,Yanghao Wang,Bowei Zhu,Hongxiang Li,Zhen Wang,Ziqi Jiang,Lin Li,Rui Liu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Warped Product Flow Matching (WP-FM)框架，通过解耦径向与角向动态，解决了现有流匹配方法在视觉-语言模型少样本适应中的几何先验不兼容问题，DP-FM变体在11个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流匹配（FM）方法在视觉-语言模型的少样本适应中，因预训练跨模态特征上不兼容的几何先验而受限。从极分解（径向和角向子流形）视角分析，发现三个被忽视的局限：1) 径向-角向耦合导致角向子流形上速度不均匀（角向动态失真）；2) 特征归一化丢弃了模态置信度，忽略径向动态；3) 无条件流忽略数据集特定信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出WP-FM统一黎曼框架，将跨模态对齐建模在弯曲乘积流形（warped product manifold）上。进一步推导出DP-FM（直接乘积流匹配），通过引入常弯曲度量（constant-warping metric）得到解耦的圆柱流形（即直接乘积流形），实现独立径向演化和恒定速度的角向测地传输。同时结合无分类器引导（CFG），以预训练VLM隐状态为条件注入缺失的数据集特定信息。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上，DP-FM在多步少样本适应任务中达到了新的最先进水平（SOTA），有效消除了角向动态失真并保持了径向一致性。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角重新审视流匹配方法，指出并解决了径向-角向耦合、径向动态忽略和上下文无关无条件流三个关键问题，提出的DP-FM在少样本适应任务中表现优越。

Original Abstract: Recent flow matching (FM) methods improve the few-shot adaptation of vision-language models, by modeling cross-modal alignment as a continuous multi-step flow. In this paper, we argue that existing FM methods are inherently constrained by incompatible geometric priors on pre-trained cross-modal features, resulting in suboptimal adaptation performance. We first analyze these methods from a polar decomposition perspective (i.e., radial and angular sub-manifolds). Under this new geometric view, we identify three overlooked limitations in them: 1) Angular dynamics distortion: The radial-angular coupling induces non-uniform speed on the angular sub-manifold, leading to regression training difficulty and extra truncation errors. 2) Radial dynamics neglect: Feature normalization discards modality confidence, failing to distinguish out-of-distribution and in-distribution data, and abandoning crucial radial dynamics. 3) Context-agnostic unconditional flow: Dataset-specific information loss during pre-trained cross-modal feature extraction remains unrecovered. To resolve these issues, we propose warped product flow matching (WP-FM), a unified Riemannian framework that reformulates alignment on a warped product manifold. Within this framework, we derive direct product flow matching (DP-FM) by introducing a constant-warping metric, which yields a decoupled cylindrical manifold (i.e., direct product manifold). DP-FM enables independent radial evolution and constant-speed angular geodesic transport, effectively eliminating angular dynamics distortion while preserving radial consistency. Meanwhile, we incorporate classifier-free guidance by conditioning the flow on the pre-trained VLMs' hidden states to inject missing dataset-specific information. Extensive results across 11 benchmarks have demonstrated that DP-FM achieves a new state-of-the-art for multi-step few-shot adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的流匹配（FM）方法通过将跨模态对齐建模为连续多步流，改进了视觉-语言模型的少样本适应能力。在本文中，我们认为现有FM方法因预训练跨模态特征上不兼容的几何先验而固有地受限，导致次优的适应性能。我们首先从极分解视角（即径向和角向子流形）分析这些方法。在这一新的几何视角下，我们识别出它们中三个被忽视的局限：1）角向动态失真：径向-角向耦合导致角向子流形上速度不均匀，带来回归训练困难和额外的截断误差。2）径向动态忽略：特征归一化丢弃了模态置信度，无法区分分布外和分布内数据，并放弃了关键的径向动态。3）上下文无关的无条件流：预训练跨模态特征提取过程中数据集特定信息的丢失仍未恢复。为解决这些问题，我们提出了弯曲乘积流匹配（WP-FM），一个统一的黎曼框架，在弯曲乘积流形上重新构建对齐。在此框架内，通过引入常弯曲度量，我们推导出直接乘积流匹配（DP-FM），它产生一个解耦的圆柱流形（即直接乘积流形）。DP-FM实现了独立的径向演化和恒定速度的角向测地传输，有效消除了角向动态失真同时保持径向一致性。同时，我们通过以预训练VLM的隐状态为条件引导流，注入缺失的数据集特定信息。在11个基准上的大量实验表明，DP-FM在多步少样本适应中达到了新的最先进水平。

</details>


### [63] [ScriptHOI: Learning Scripted State Transitions for Open-Vocabulary Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05057)
*Minh Anh Nguyen,Quang Huy Tran,Bao Ngoc Le,SuiYang Guang,Tuan Kiet Pham,Linh Chi Vo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScriptHOI框架，将交互短语分解为软脚本状态转移，通过槽位匹配校准HOI逻辑，并引入区间部分标签学习和反事实脚本对比损失，显著提升开放词汇HOI检测中罕见和未见交互的识别，同时减少假阳性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言HOI检测器常被物体可供性和短语共现主导，未能验证手部、工具、目标、接触模式和物体状态是否共同支持动作，导致预测错误（如仅因存在刀和蛋糕就预测切蛋糕）。

Method: 1) 将交互短语分解为身体角色、接触、几何、可供性、运动和物体状态六个槽位；2) 视觉状态分词器将检测到的人-物对解析为状态令牌；3) 槽位匹配器计算脚本覆盖度和脚本冲突度以校准HOI逻辑；4) 区间部分标签学习为未标注候选赋予脚本导出的概率上下界；5) 反事实脚本对比损失通过交换单个脚本槽位来抑制物体快捷方式。

Result: 在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI分割上，ScriptHOI在罕见和未见交互识别上取得提升，同时显著减少了可供性冲突的假阳性。

Conclusion: 通过结构化脚本表示和相应的训练策略，可有效抑制物体可供性捷径，提升模型对复杂交互语义的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires recognizing interaction phrases that may not appear as annotated categories during training. Recent vision-language HOI detectors improve semantic transfer by matching human-object features with text embeddings, but their predictions are often dominated by object affordance and phrase-level co-occurrence. As a result, a model may predict \textit{cut cake} from the presence of a knife and a cake without verifying whether the hand, tool, target, contact pattern, and object state jointly support the action. We propose \textbf{ScriptHOI}, a structured framework that represents each interaction phrase as a soft scripted state transition. Rather than treating a phrase as a single class token, ScriptHOI decomposes it into body-role, contact, geometry, affordance, motion, and object-state slots. A visual state tokenizer parses each detected human-object pair into corresponding state tokens, and a slot-wise matcher estimates both script coverage and script conflict. These two quantities calibrate HOI logits, expose missing visual evidence, and provide training constraints for incomplete annotations. To avoid suppressing valid but unannotated interactions, we further introduce interval partial-label learning, which constrains unannotated candidates with script-derived lower and upper probability bounds instead of assigning closed-world negatives. A counterfactual script contrast loss swaps individual script slots to discourage object-only shortcuts. Experiments on HICO-DET, V-COCO, and open-vocabulary HOI splits show that ScriptHOI improves rare and unseen interaction recognition while substantially reducing affordance-conflict false positives.

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇的人-物交互（HOI）检测要求模型识别训练过程中未作为标注类别出现的交互短语。最近的视觉-语言HOI检测器通过将人-物特征与文本嵌入匹配来提升语义迁移能力，但其预测常被物体可供性和短语级共现所主导。因此，模型可能仅凭刀和蛋糕的存在就预测"切蛋糕"，而无需验证手部、工具、目标、接触模式和物体状态是否共同支持该动作。我们提出\textbf{ScriptHOI}，一个将每个交互短语表示为软脚本状态转移的结构化框架。ScriptHOI并非将短语视为单一类别令牌，而是将其分解为身体角色、接触、几何、可供性、运动和物体状态槽位。视觉状态分词器将检测到的每个人-物对解析为对应的状态令牌，槽位匹配器同时估计脚本覆盖度和脚本冲突度。这两个量校准HOI逻辑分数，暴露缺失的视觉证据，并为不完整标注提供训练约束。为避免压制有效但未标注的交互，我们进一步引入区间部分标签学习，该学习方法用脚本导出的概率上下界约束未标注候选，而非将其归为封闭世界负样本。反事实脚本对比损失通过交换单个脚本槽位来抑制仅依赖物体的快捷方式。在HICO-DET、V-COCO和开放词汇HOI分割上的实验表明，ScriptHOI提升了罕见和未见交互的识别能力，同时显著减少了可供性冲突的假阳性。

</details>


### [64] [Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization for Camera-LiDAR Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05072)
*Yuan Wu,Zhiqiang Yan,Jiawei Lian,Zhengxue Wang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPR是一种面向3D占据预测的相机-激光雷达融合框架，通过高度引导投影重参数化（将LiDAR编码为BEV高度图，自适应调整柱体采样范围）和渐进式高度条件训练策略，有效解决了传统均匀柱体采样难以捕捉场景稀疏性与高度变化的问题，在保持实时推理的同时取得了最先进的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D占据预测方法大多采用固定投影空间，沿柱体均匀采样3D参考点，但这种均匀采样方式难以捕捉真实场景中的稀疏性和高度变化，导致特征对应模糊和特征聚合不可靠。

Method: HiPR首先将LiDAR点云编码为BEV高度图，获取点云的最大高度先验；然后利用高度先验自适应调整每个柱体的采样范围，实现投影空间的重参数化，使投影点重新分布到几何有意义的区域；同时掩码掉高度图中的无效部分以避免误导特征聚合；此外，引入渐进式高度条件训练策略，逐步从真值高度过渡到LiDAR高度，缓解噪声带来的训练不稳定问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，HiPR在保持实时推理的同时，持续优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: HiPR通过高度引导的投影重参数化和渐进式条件训练，有效克服了传统均匀柱体采样在3D占据预测中的局限性，验证了利用高度先验自适应调整投影空间的有效性。

Original Abstract: 3D occupancy prediction aims to infer dense, voxel-wise scene semantics from sensor observations, where the 2D-to-3D view transformation serves as a crucial step in bridging image features and volumetric representations. Most previous methods rely on a fixed projection space, where 3D reference points are uniformly sampled along pillars. However, such sampling struggles to capture the sparsity and height variations of real-world scenes, leading to ambiguous correspondences and unreliable feature aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose HiPR, a camera-LiDAR occupancy framework with Height-Guided Projection Reparameterization. HiPR first encodes LiDAR into a BEV height map to capture the maximum height of the point cloud. HiPR then adjusts the sampling range of each pillar using the height prior, enabling adaptive reparameterization of the projection space. As a result, the projected points are redistributed into geometrically meaningful regions rather than fixed ranges. Meanwhile, we mask out the invalid parts of the height map to avoid misleading the feature aggregation. In addition, to alleviate the training instability caused by noisy LiDAR-derived heights, we introduce a training-time Progressive Height Conditioning strategy, which gradually transitions the conditioning signal from ground-truth heights to LiDAR heights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiPR consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods while maintaining real-time inference. The code and pretrained models can be found at https://github.com/Rayn-Wu/HiPR.

中文摘要翻译: 3D占据预测旨在从传感器观测中推断稠密的、体素级的场景语义信息，其中2D到3D的视图变换是连接图像特征与体素表示的关键步骤。以往大多数方法依赖于固定的投影空间，即沿柱体均匀采样3D参考点。然而，这种采样方式难以捕捉真实场景的稀疏性和高度变化，导致对应关系模糊和特征聚合不可靠。为解决这些挑战，我们提出HiPR——一种带有高度引导投影重参数化的相机-激光雷达占据预测框架。HiPR首先将LiDAR编码为BEV高度图，以获取点云的最大高度。然后，HiPR利用高度先验调整每个柱体的采样范围，实现投影空间的自适应重参数化。由此，投影点被重新分布到几何有意义的区域，而非固定范围内。同时，我们掩码掉高度图中的无效部分，以避免误导特征聚合。此外，为缓解由含噪声的LiDAR高度引起的训练不稳定问题，我们引入了一种训练阶段的渐进式高度条件策略，逐步将条件信号从真值高度过渡到LiDAR高度。大量实验表明，HiPR在保持实时推理的同时，持续优于现有的最先进方法。代码和预训练模型可在 https://github.com/Rayn-Wu/HiPR 获取。

</details>


### [65] [FlowDIS: Language-Guided Dichotomous Image Segmentation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05077)
*Andranik Sargsyan,Shant Navasardyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowDIS 是一种基于流匹配框架的二值图像分割方法，通过学习时间依赖向量场将图像分布映射到掩码分布，并利用位置感知实例配对策略实现文本提示控制，在 DIS-TE 测试集上相比最优方法提升 5.5% 的 Fβω 指标并降低 43% 的 MAE。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有二值图像分割方法在处理前景细节保留和语义结构捕捉方面存在不足，难以同时兼顾精细纹理和完整语义结构。

Method: 提出 FlowDIS 方法，基于流匹配框架学习时间依赖的向量场，将图像分布逐步传输到对应的掩码分布；同时引入位置感知实例配对训练策略，支持通过文本提示进行可控的像素级精确分割。

Result: 在 DIS-TE 测试集上，FlowDIS 相比最优现有方法，Fβω 指标提升 5.5%，平均绝对误差（MAE）降低 43%。

Conclusion: FlowDIS 在有无语言引导的场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了流匹配框架在二值图像分割中的有效性及文本可控分割的可行性。

Original Abstract: Accurate image segmentation is essential for modern computer vision applications such as image editing, autonomous driving, and medical image analysis. In recent years, Dichotomous Image Segmentation (DIS) has become a standard task for training and evaluating highly accurate segmentation models. Existing DIS approaches often fail to preserve fine-grained details or fully capture the semantic structure of the foreground. To address these challenges, we present FlowDIS, a novel dichotomous image segmentation method built on the flow matching framework, which learns a time-dependent vector field to transport the image distribution to the corresponding mask distribution, optionally conditioned on a text prompt. Moreover, with our Position-Aware Instance Pairing (PAIP) training strategy, FlowDIS offers strong controllability through text prompts, enabling precise, pixel-level object segmentation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches both with and without language guidance. Compared with the best prior DIS method, FlowDIS achieves a 5.5% higher $F_β^ω$ measure and 43% lower MAE ($\mathcal{M}$) on the DIS-TE test set. The code is available at: https://github.com/Picsart-AI-Research/FlowDIS

中文摘要翻译: 精确的图像分割对于现代计算机视觉应用（如图像编辑、自动驾驶和医学图像分析）至关重要。近年来，二值图像分割（Dichotomous Image Segmentation, DIS）已成为训练和评估高精度分割模型的标准任务。现有 DIS 方法往往难以保留精细细节或充分捕捉前景的语义结构。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了 FlowDIS，一种基于流匹配框架的新型二值图像分割方法。该方法学习一个时间依赖的向量场，将图像分布传输到对应的掩码分布，并可选地以文本提示为条件。此外，通过我们提出的位置感知实例配对（Position-Aware Instance Pairing, PAIP）训练策略，FlowDIS 可通过文本提示实现强可控性，从而实现精确的像素级目标分割。大量实验表明，我们的方法在有语言引导和无语言引导的场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法。与先前最优 DIS 方法相比，FlowDIS 在 DIS-TE 测试集上的 Fβω 指标提升了 5.5%，平均绝对误差（MAE）降低了 43%。代码已开源：https://github.com/Picsart-AI-Research/FlowDIS

</details>


### [66] [A unified Benchmark for Multi-Frame Image Restoration under Severe Refractive Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05079)
*Maxim V. Shugaev,Md Reshad Ul Hoque,Bridget Kennedy,Joseph T. Riley,Fiona Hwang,Justin Hagen,Harvir Ghuman,Ethan Garcia-O'Donnell,Syed Noor Qadri,Freddie Santiago,Mun Wai Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对强非均匀折射动态介质（如湍流空气或水面）导致的视频几何畸变去除的综合基准。该基准涵盖从轻度湍流到强不连续折射变形的多种场景，包含实验室真实数据和基于物理折射建模的合成序列，并评估了从经典方法到基于扩散模型的先进方法（如DATUM和V-cache）的性能。这是首个大规模几何畸变去除分析基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要针对轻度大气湍流，缺乏系统性评估强非均匀折射条件下视频几何畸变去除方法的基准。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立第一个大规模、系统化的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建了包含实验室捕获真实数据和基于物理光折射建模的合成序列的基准数据集，涵盖四种畸变等级和多种表面波类型。评估了从简单基线、经典配准算法到基于学习的方法（包括DATUM和本文提出的基于扩散的V-cache）在内的多种方法。采用像素级（PSNR, SSIM）和感知级（LPIPS, DINO, CLIP）指标进行评估。

Result: 实验对多种方法进行了大规模比较分析，在高畸变和极端畸变场景下，扩散模型V-cache表现出色。该基准为视频几何畸变去除提供了首个大规模定量分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基准为从高度畸变光学环境中重建视频的算法开发和评估建立了新的基础。该基准将推动相关领域的研究进展。

Original Abstract: Video sequence capturing through refractive dynamic media, such as a turbulent air or water surface, often suffer from severe geometric distortions and temporal instability. While recent advances address mild atmospheric turbulence, no existing benchmarks systematically evaluate restoration methods under strong and highly nonuniform refractive conditions. We present a comprehensive benchmark for geometric distortion removal in video, covering a range from turbulence-like mild warping to strong discontinuous refractive deformations. The benchmark includes both laboratory-captured real data and synthetic sequences generated for static scenes via physics-based light refraction modeling across four distortion levels and multiple surface wave types. We evaluate a spectrum of methods from simple baselines and classical registration algorithms to advanced learning-based approaches including DATUM and our proposed diffusion based V-cache for high and extreme distortions regimes. Evaluation uses both pixel-level (PSNR, SSIM), and perceptual (LPIPS, DINO, CLIP) metrics providing the first large scale analysis of geometric distortion removal. Our benchmark establishes a new foundation for developing and evaluating algorithms capable of reconstructing video from highly distorted optical environments. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/iafoss/refractive-mfir-benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 通过折射动态介质（如湍流空气或水面）捕获的视频序列通常会遭受严重的几何畸变和时间不稳定性。尽管近期研究在应对轻度大气湍流方面取得了进展，但目前尚无现有基准系统性地评估在强且高度非均匀折射条件下的恢复方法。本文提出了一个全面的视频几何畸变去除基准，涵盖从类似湍流的轻度扭曲到强不连续折射变形等多种场景。该基准包括实验室捕获的真实数据以及通过基于物理的光折射建模生成的合成序列，涵盖四种畸变等级和多种表面波类型。我们评估了从简单基线、经典配准算法到先进基于学习方法（包括DATUM和我们提出的用于高畸变和极端畸变场景的基于扩散模型的V-cache）等多种方法。评估采用像素级（PSNR, SSIM）和感知级（LPIPS, DINO, CLIP）指标，提供了首个大规模几何畸变去除分析。我们的基准为从高度畸变光学环境中重建视频的算法开发和评估建立了新的基础。我们的代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/iafoss/refractive-mfir-benchmark 获取。

</details>


### [67] [Aes3D: Aesthetic Assessment in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05155)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Haoxian Zhou,Xuanhua Yin,Zihan Deng,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aes3D，首个面向3D神经渲染场景的美学评估框架，包含首个3D场景美学数据集Aesthetic3D和轻量级模型Aes3DGSNet，可直接从3D高斯原语预测场景级美学分数，无需渲染多视角图像。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D场景评估方法主要关注重建保真度和感知真实性，忽视了构图、和谐性、视觉吸引力等高层美学属性。原因是缺乏带美学标注的3DGS数据集，且3DGS作为底层原语表示难以捕获高层美学特征。

Method: 构建了首个3D场景美学评估数据集Aesthetic3D及其标注策略；提出Aes3DGSNet轻量级模型，通过美学监督学习直接对多视角3DGS场景表示进行美学评分，无需渲染多视角图像，降低计算成本。

Result: 实验表明该方法在保持轻量级设计的同时实现了强劲性能，为3D场景美学评估建立了新基准。

Conclusion: Aes3D有效填补了3D场景美学评估的空白，通过直接处理3D高斯原语实现高效美学评分，未来将公开代码和数据集。

Original Abstract: As 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) gains attention in immersive media and digital content creation, assessing the aesthetics of 3D scenes becomes important in helping creators build more visually compelling 3D content. However, existing evaluation methods for 3D scenes primarily emphasize reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism, largely overlooking higher-level aesthetic attributes such as composition, harmony, and visual appeal. This limitation comes from two key challenges: (1) the absence of general 3DGS datasets with aesthetic annotations, and (2) the intrinsic nature of 3DGS as a low-level primitive representation, which makes it difficult to capture high-level aesthetic features. To address these challenges, we propose Aes3D, the first systematic framework for assessing the aesthetics of 3D neural rendering scenes. Aes3D includes Aesthetic3D, the first dataset dedicated to 3D scene aesthetic assessment, built on our proposed annotation strategy for 3D scene aesthetics. In addition, we present Aes3DGSNet, a lightweight model that directly predicts scene-level aesthetic scores from 3DGS representations. Notably, our model operates solely on 3D Gaussian primitives, eliminating the need for rendering multi-view images and thus reducing computational cost and hardware requirements. Through aesthetics-supervised learning on multi-view 3DGS scene representations, Aes3DGSNet effectively captures high-level aesthetic cues and accurately regresses aesthetic scores. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance while maintaining a lightweight design, establishing a new benchmark for 3D scene aesthetic assessment. Code and datasets will be made available in a future version.

中文摘要翻译: 随着3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在沉浸式媒体和数字内容创作中受到关注，评估3D场景的美学对于帮助创作者构建更具视觉吸引力的3D内容变得重要。然而，现有的3D场景评估方法主要强调重建保真度和感知真实性，很大程度上忽视了构图、和谐性和视觉吸引力等高层美学属性。这一限制源于两个关键挑战：（1）缺乏带有美学标注的通用3DGS数据集，（2）3DGS作为底层原语表示的内在特性使其难以捕获高层美学特征。为应对这些挑战，我们提出Aes3D，首个用于评估3D神经渲染场景美学的系统性框架。Aes3D包含Aesthetic3D——首个专门用于3D场景美学评估的数据集，该数据集基于我们提出的3D场景美学标注策略构建。此外，我们提出Aes3DGSNet，一个轻量级模型，可直接从3DGS表示中预测场景级美学分数。值得注意的是，我们的模型仅基于3D高斯原语运行，无需渲染多视角图像，从而降低了计算成本和硬件需求。通过对多视角3DGS场景表示进行美学监督学习，Aes3DGSNet有效捕获了高层美学线索并准确回归美学分数。实验结果表明，我们的方法在保持轻量级设计的同时实现了强劲性能，为3D场景美学评估建立了新基准。代码和数据集将在未来版本中公开。

</details>


### [68] [Wasserstein-Aligned Localisation for VLM-Based Distributional OOD Detection in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05161)
*Bernhard Kainz,Johanna P Mueller,Matthew Baugh,Cosmin Bercea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WALDO是一个基于最优传输理论的免训练框架，通过将零样本异常定位重构为对比推理问题，利用DINOv2补丁分布和Goldilocks采样策略，在脑MRI异常定位中超越零样本基线19%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的零样本异常定位方法缺乏健康解剖结构的上下文信息，性能受到根本性限制。

Method: (i) 基于熵加权Sliced Wasserstein距离的解剖感知参考选择；(ii) Goldilocks区域采样，利用参考相似度与定位精度之间的非单调关系；(iii) 通过加权非极大值抑制进行自一致性聚合。

Result: 在NOVA脑MRI基准上，WALDO+Qwen2.5-VL-72B达到43.5% mAP@30，相对提升19%；GPT-4o达32.0%，Qwen3-VL-32B达32.0%；McNemar检验确认统计显著性(p<0.01)。

Conclusion: 适度相似度的参考能在对比视觉推理中最小化偏差-方差权衡，即Goldilocks效应。

Original Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly localisation via vision-language models (VLMs) offers a compelling approach for rare pathology detection, yet its performance is fundamentally limited by the absence of healthy anatomical context. We reformulate zero-shot localisation as a comparative inference problem in which anomalies are identified through structured comparison against reference distributions of normal anatomy. We introduce WALDO, a training-free framework grounded in optimal transport theory that enables comparative reasoning through: (i) entropy-weighted Sliced Wasserstein distances for anatomically-aware reference selection from DINOv2 patch distributions, (ii) Goldilocks zone sampling exploiting the non-monotonic relationship between reference similarity and localisation accuracy, and (iii) self-consistency aggregation via weighted non-maximum suppression. We theoretically analyse the Goldilocks effect through distributional divergence, and show that references with moderate similarity minimize a bias-variance trade-off in comparative visual reasoning. On the NOVA brain MRI benchmark, WALDO with Qwen2.5-VL-72B achieves $43.5_{\pm1.6}\%$ mAP@30 (95\% CI: [40.4, 46.7]), representing a 19\% relative improvement over zero-shot baselines. Cross-model evaluation shows consistent gains: GPT-4o achieves $32.0_{\pm6.5}\%$ and Qwen3-VL-32B achieves $32.0_{\pm6.6}\%$ mAP@30. Paired McNemar tests confirm statistical significance ($p<0.01$). Source code is available at https://github.com/bkainz/WALDO_MICCAI26_demo .

中文摘要翻译: 基于视觉语言模型的零样本异常定位为罕见病理检测提供了一种引人注目的方法，但其性能从根本上受到缺乏健康解剖上下文的限制。我们将零样本定位重新表述为一种比较推理问题，其中通过结构化地与正常解剖的参考分布进行比较来识别异常。我们提出了WALDO，一个基于最优传输理论的免训练框架，通过以下方式实现比较推理：(i) 基于熵加权Sliced Wasserstein距离，从DINOv2补丁分布中进行解剖感知的参考选择；(ii) Goldilocks区域采样，利用参考相似度与定位精度之间的非单调关系；(iii) 通过加权非极大值抑制进行自一致性聚合。我们通过分布散度从理论上分析了Goldilocks效应，并表明具有适度相似度的参考能在对比视觉推理中最小化偏差-方差权衡。在NOVA脑MRI基准上，WALDO结合Qwen2.5-VL-72B达到43.5±1.6%的mAP@30（95%置信区间：[40.4, 46.7]），相比零样本基线实现了19%的相对提升。跨模型评估显示出一致性的增益：GPT-4o达到32.0±6.5%，Qwen3-VL-32B达到32.0±6.6%的mAP@30。配对McNemar检验确认了统计显著性（p<0.01）。源代码可在https://github.com/bkainz/WALDO_MICCAI26_demo获取。

</details>


### [69] [PhysForge: Generating Physics-Grounded 3D Assets for Interactive Virtual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05163)
*Yunhan Yang,Chunshi Wang,Junliang Ye,Yang Li,Zanxin Chen,Zehuan Huang,Yao Mu,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysForge 提出了一种解耦的两阶段框架，结合物理蓝图规划（VLM）和物理引导的扩散模型（KineVoxel Injection），配合包含15万资产的四级物理标注数据集 PhysDB，实现功能合理、可仿真交互的3D资产生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D资产生成方法主要关注静态几何，忽略了物体在交互中所需的功能性物理属性（如材料、运动学约束等），导致生成的资产无法用于物理仿真和具身智能交互。

Method: 提出解耦的两阶段框架 PhysForge：第一阶段，VLM 作为"物理架构师"规划分层物理蓝图，定义材料、功能和运动学约束；第二阶段，物理引导的扩散模型通过 KineVoxel Injection (KVI) 机制，在生成高保真几何的同时精确合成运动学参数。框架依托包含15万资产和四级物理标注的 PhysDB 数据集。

Result: 实验表明 PhysForge 能生成功能合理、可直接用于物理仿真的3D资产，为交互式3D内容和具身智能体提供了鲁棒的数据引擎。

Conclusion: 将物理功能逻辑引入3D资产生成是可行的且有效的，PhysForge 为交互式虚拟世界和具身AI的资产生成提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Synthesizing physics-grounded 3D assets is a critical bottleneck for interactive virtual worlds and embodied AI. Existing methods predominantly focus on static geometry, overlooking the functional properties essential for interaction. We propose that interactive asset generation must be rooted in functional logic and hierarchical physics. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysForge, a decoupled two-stage framework supported by PhysDB, a large-scale dataset of 150,000 assets with four-tier physical annotations. First, a VLM acts as a "physical architect" to plan a "Hierarchical Physical Blueprint" defining material, functional, and kinematic constraints. Second, a physics-grounded diffusion model realizes this blueprint by synthesizing high-fidelity geometry alongside precise kinematic parameters via a novel KineVoxel Injection (KVI) mechanism. Experiments demonstrate that PhysForge produces functionally plausible, simulation-ready assets, providing a robust data engine for interactive 3D content and embodied agents.

中文摘要翻译: 生成具有物理基础的3D资产是构建交互式虚拟世界和具身智能的关键瓶颈。现有方法主要关注静态几何，忽略了交互所需的功能属性。我们提出，交互式资产生成必须根植于功能逻辑和分层物理原理。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了 PhysForge——一个解耦的两阶段框架，辅以 PhysDB——一个包含15万资产和四级物理标注的大规模数据集。首先，视觉语言模型（VLM）作为"物理架构师"，规划定义材料、功能和运动学约束的"分层物理蓝图"。其次，一个基于物理引导的扩散模型通过新颖的 KineVoxel Injection (KVI) 机制，在合成高保真几何的同时精确生成运动学参数，从而实现该蓝图。实验结果表明，PhysForge 能够生成功能合理、可直接用于仿真的资产，为交互式3D内容和具身智能体提供了鲁棒的数据引擎。

</details>


### [70] [OpenSearch-VL: An Open Recipe for Frontier Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05185)
*Shuang Chen,Kaituo Feng,Hangting Chen,Wenxuan Huang,Dasen Dai,Quanxin Shou,Yunlong Lin,Xiangyu Yue,Shenghua Gao,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenSearch-VL 是一个完全开源的多模态深度搜索智能体训练方案，通过高质量数据构建、多样化工具环境和多轮容错 GRPO 强化学习，在七个基准上平均提升超 10 个点，达到与商业模型可比的水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 顶级多模态搜索智能体难以复现，缺乏开源的高质量训练数据、透明的轨迹合成流程和详细的训练方案。

Method: (1) 通过 Wikipedia 路径采样、模糊实体重写和源锚视觉定位构建高质量训练数据；(2) 设计统一工具环境（文本搜索、图像搜索、OCR、裁剪、锐化、超分辨率、透视校正）；(3) 提出多轮容错 GRPO 训练算法，通过掩码失败后 token 和单侧优势裁剪来处理级联工具故障。

Result: 在七个基准上平均提升超过 10 个点，在多个任务上取得与专有商业模型相当的结果。

Conclusion: OpenSearch-VL 作为完全开源的方案，能够有效训练前沿多模态深度搜索智能体，并将公开所有数据、代码和模型以支持开放研究。

Original Abstract: Deep search has become a crucial capability for frontier multimodal agents, enabling models to solve complex questions through active search, evidence verification, and multi-step reasoning. Despite rapid progress, top-tier multimodal search agents remain difficult to reproduce, largely due to the absence of open high-quality training data, transparent trajectory synthesis pipelines, or detailed training recipes. To this end, we introduce OpenSearch-VL, a fully open-source recipe for training frontier multimodal deep search agents with agentic reinforcement learning. First, we curated a dedicated pipeline to construct high-quality training data through Wikipedia path sampling, fuzzy entity rewriting, and source-anchor visual grounding, which jointly reduce shortcuts and one-step retrieval collapse. Based on this pipeline, we curate two training datasets, SearchVL-SFT-36k for SFT and SearchVL-RL-8k for RL. Besides, we design a diverse tool environment that unifies text search, image search, OCR, cropping, sharpening, super-resolution, and perspective correction, enabling agents to combine active perception with external knowledge acquisition. Finally, we propose a multi-turn fatal-aware GRPO training algorithm that handles cascading tool failures by masking post-failure tokens while preserving useful pre-failure reasoning through one-sided advantage clamping. Built on this recipe, OpenSearch-VL delivers substantial performance gains, with over 10-point average improvements across seven benchmarks, and achieves results comparable to proprietary commercial models on several tasks. We will release all data, code, and models to support open research on multimodal deep search agents.

中文摘要翻译: 深度搜索已成为前沿多模态智能体的关键能力，使模型能够通过主动搜索、证据验证和多步推理来解决复杂问题。尽管进展迅速，但顶级多模态搜索智能体仍然难以复现，很大程度上是由于缺乏开源的高质量训练数据、透明的轨迹合成流程以及详细的训练方案。为此，我们提出 OpenSearch-VL，一个完全开源的训练前沿多模态深度搜索智能体的方案，采用智能体强化学习。首先，我们设计了一个专门的数据构建流程，通过 Wikipedia 路径采样、模糊实体重写和源锚视觉定位来构造高质量训练数据，这些方法共同减少了捷径和一步检索崩溃。基于该流程，我们构建了两个训练数据集：用于监督微调的 SearchVL-SFT-36k 和用于强化学习的 SearchVL-RL-8k。此外，我们设计了一个多样化的工具环境，统一了文本搜索、图像搜索、OCR、裁剪、锐化、超分辨率和透视校正，使智能体能够将主动感知与外部知识获取相结合。最后，我们提出了一种多轮容错 GRPO 训练算法，该算法通过掩码失败后的 token 来处理级联工具故障，同时通过单侧优势裁剪保留失败前有用的推理。基于这一方案，OpenSearch-VL 在七个基准上取得了平均超过 10 个点的性能提升，并在多个任务上取得了与专有商业模型相当的结果。我们将公开所有数据、代码和模型，以支持多模态深度搜索智能体的开放研究。

</details>


### [71] [LoViF 2026 The First Challenge on Holistic Quality Assessment for 4D World Model (PhyScore)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05187)
*Wei Luo,Yiting Lu,Xin Li,Haoran Li,Fengbin Guan,Chen Gao,Xin Jin,Yong Li,Zhibo Chen,Sijing Wu,Kang Fu,Yunhao Li,Ziang Xiao,Huiyu Duan,Jing Liu,Qiang Hu,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai,Manxi Sun,Zixuan Guo,Yun Li,Ziyang Chen,Manabu Tsukada,Zhengyang Li,Zhenglin Du,Yi Wen,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu,Lingling Li,Yiwen Ren,Zhilong Song,Dubing Chen,Yucheng Zhou,Tianyi Yan,Huan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛，该比赛旨在对世界模型生成的视频进行全面质量评估，涵盖2D和4D生成场景。参赛者需要构建一个联合预测视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频对齐和时间一致性四个维度的指标，并定位物理异常时间戳。数据集包含1,554个由七种世界模型生成的视频，分为三个赛道和26个类别。评估基于分数预测和异常定位的组合协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前评估实践中存在一个核心空白：仅靠感知质量不足以判断生成动态是否在物理上合理、时间上连贯且与输入条件一致。因此需要一种更全面的评估方法。

Method: 参赛者需构建一个联合预测四个维度（视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频对齐、时间一致性）的指标，并定位物理异常时间戳。数据集由七种世界模型生成的1,554个视频组成，分为文本到2D、图像到4D和视频到4D三个赛道，涵盖26个类别（包括动力学、光学、热力学等物理相关场景）。评估采用分数预测（SRCC/PLCC）和异常定位（TimeStamp_IOU）的组合协议。

Result: 通过训练人工标注和自动质量控制流程确保了标签可靠性。评估协议结合了TimeStamp_IOU和SRCC/PLCC指标。报告总结了挑战设计并提供了提交解决方案的方法级洞见。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛通过引入多维度质量评估和物理异常时间戳定位，弥补了当前视频生成评估中仅依赖感知质量的不足，为世界模型生成视频的全面质量评估提供了新的基准和方向。

Original Abstract: This paper reports on the LoViF 2026 PhyScore challenge, a competition on holistic quality assessment of world-model-generated videos across both 2D and 4D generation settings. The challenge is motivated by a central gap in current evaluation practice: perceptual quality alone is insufficient to judge whether generated dynamics are physically plausible, temporally coherent, and consistent with input conditions. Participants are required to build a metric that jointly predicts four dimensions, i.e., Video Quality, Physical Realism, Condition-Video Alignment, and Temporal Consistency. Depart from that, participants also need to localize physical anomaly timestamps for fine-grained diagnosis.
  The benchmark dataset contains 1,554 videos generated by seven representative world generative models, organized into three tracks (text-2D, image-to-4D, and video-to-4D) and spanning 26 categories. These categories explicitly cover physics-relevant scenarios, including dynamics, optics, and thermodynamics, together with diverse real-world and creative content. To ensure label reliability, scores and anomaly timestamps are produced through trained human annotation with an additional automated quality-control pass.
  Evaluation is based on both score prediction and anomaly localization, with a composite protocol that combines TimeStamp_IOU and SRCC/PLCC. This report summarizes the challenge design and provides method-level insights from submitted solutions.

中文摘要翻译: 本文报告了LoViF 2026 PhyScore挑战赛，这是一项针对世界模型生成视频在2D和4D生成设置下进行全面质量评估的竞赛。该挑战的动机源于当前评估实践中的一个核心空白：仅凭感知质量不足以判断生成的动态是否在物理上合理、时间上连贯且与输入条件一致。参赛者需要构建一个联合预测四个维度的指标，即视频质量、物理真实性、条件-视频对齐和时间一致性。除此之外，参赛者还需要定位物理异常时间戳以进行细粒度诊断。
  基准数据集包含由七种代表性世界生成模型生成的1,554个视频，分为三个赛道（文本到2D、图像到4D和视频到4D），涵盖26个类别。这些类别明确涵盖了物理相关场景，包括动力学、光学和热力学，以及多样化的现实世界和创意内容。为确保标签可靠性，分数和异常时间戳通过训练有素的人工标注并附加自动质量控制流程生成。
  评估基于分数预测和异常定位，采用结合TimeStamp_IOU和SRCC/PLCC的复合协议。本报告总结了挑战设计，并提供了来自提交解决方案的方法级洞见。

</details>


### [72] [D-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Continuously Tuning Step-Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05204)
*Dengyang Jiang,Xin Jin,Dongyang Liu,Zanyi Wang,Mingzhe Zheng,Ruoyi Du,Xiangpeng Yang,Qilong Wu,Zhen Li,Peng Gao,Harry Yang,Steven Hoi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D-OPSD训练范式，通过在线策略自蒸馏实现少步扩散模型的高效监督微调，在不牺牲少步推理能力的前提下学习新概念和风格。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少步扩散模型（如Z-Image-Turbo、FLUX.2-klein）无法直接应用连续监督微调，因为常用微调技术会损害其固有的少步推理能力。

Method: 利用现代扩散模型中LLM/VLM编码器的上下文学习能力，将训练构建为在线策略自蒸馏过程：模型同时作为教师和学生，学生仅以文本特征为条件，教师以文本提示和目标图像的多模态特征为条件，在模型自身的轨迹上最小化两个预测分布之间的差异。

Result: D-OPSD使模型在保持原有少步生成能力的前提下，能够学习新概念、新风格等。

Conclusion: D-OPSD是一种有效的训练范式，解决了少步扩散模型监督微调中少步推理能力退化的问题。

Original Abstract: The landscape of high-performance image generation models is currently shifting from the inefficient multi-step ones to the efficient few-step counterparts (e.g, Z-Image-Turbo and FLUX.2-klein). However, these models present significant challenges for directly continuous supervised fine-tuning. For example, applying the commonly used fine-tuning technique would compromises their inherent few-step inference capability. To address this, we propose D-OPSD, a novel training paradigm for step-distilled diffusion models that enables on-policy learning during supervised fine-tuning. We first find that the modern diffusion model where the LLM/VLM serves as the encoder can inherit its encoder's in-context capabilities. This enables us to make the training as an on-policy self-distillation process. Specifically, during training, we make the model acts as both the teacher and the student with different contexts, where the student is conditioned only on the text feature, while the teacher is conditioned on the multimodal feature of both the text prompt and the target image. Training minimizes the two predicted distributions over the student's own roll-outs. By optimized on the model's own trajectory and under it's own supervision, D-OPSD enables the model to learn new concept, style, etc. without sacrificing the original few-step capacity.

中文摘要翻译: 高性能图像生成模型的格局目前正从低效的多步模型向高效的少步模型（例如Z-Image-Turbo和FLUX.2-klein）转变。然而，这些模型对直接连续监督微调提出了重大挑战。例如，应用常用的微调技术会损害其固有的少步推理能力。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了D-OPSD，一种针对步蒸馏扩散模型的新颖训练范式，该范式能够在监督微调过程中实现在线策略学习。我们首先发现，以LLM/VLM作为编码器的现代扩散模型能够继承其编码器的上下文学习能力。这使我们能够将训练构建为一个在线策略自蒸馏过程。具体来说，在训练过程中，我们让模型在不同上下文条件下同时充当教师和学生：学生仅以文本特征为条件，而教师则以文本提示和目标图像的多模态特征为条件。训练过程最小化模型自身轨迹上两个预测分布之间的差异。通过在模型自身的轨迹上进行优化并在其自身的监督下学习，D-OPSD使得模型能够学习新概念、新风格等，而无需牺牲原有的少步生成能力。

</details>


### [73] [Taming Outlier Tokens in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05206)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Tsu-Jui Fu,Liang-Chieh Chen,Zhe Gan,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中的异常点（outlier tokens）问题，提出双阶段寄存器（DSR）方法在编码器和去噪器中抑制异常点，从而提升生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉Transformer（ViT）中存在少量高范数token，它们吸引了不成比例的注意力但携带有限局部信息，然而这一问题在生成模型（尤其是DiT）中的角色尚未被充分探索。作者发现异常点现象同时出现在RAE-DiT管线的编码器和去噪器中，且简单掩码无效，说明问题根源在于局部块语义被破坏。

Method: 提出双阶段寄存器（Dual-Stage Registers, DSR），包含三部分：1）训练好的寄存器（trained registers）；2）递归测试时寄存器（recursive test-time registers）；3）针对去噪器的扩散寄存器（diffusion registers）。在编码器和去噪器两端分别引入寄存器来存储和吸收异常信息。

Result: 在ImageNet和大规模文生图任务上，DSR方法一致地减少了异常点伪影并提升了生成质量。实验表明掩码异常点token并不能改善性能，而DSR的寄存器干预措施有效。

Conclusion: 异常点控制是构建更强DiT的重要组件，通过DSR双阶段寄存器干预可有效缓解异常点问题，提升图像生成质量。

Original Abstract: We study outlier tokens in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for image generation. Prior work has shown that Vision Transformers (ViTs) can produce a small number of high-norm tokens that attract disproportionate attention while carrying limited local information, but their role in generative models remains underexplored. We show that this phenomenon appears in both the encoder and denoiser of modern Representation Autoencoder (RAE)-DiT pipelines: pretrained ViT encoders can produce outlier representations, and DiTs themselves can develop internal outlier tokens, especially in intermediate layers. Moreover, simply masking high-norm tokens does not improve performance, indicating that the problem is not only caused by a few extreme values, but is more closely related to corrupted local patch semantics. To address this issue, we introduce Dual-Stage Registers (DSR), a register-based intervention for both components: trained registers when available, recursive test-time registers otherwise, and diffusion registers for the denoiser. Across ImageNet and large-scale text-to-image generation, these interventions consistently reduce outlier artifacts and improve generation quality. Our results highlight outlier-token control as an important ingredient in building stronger DiTs.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了扩散Transformer（DiT）在图像生成中的异常点（outlier tokens）。先前工作表明，视觉Transformer（ViT）会产生少量高范数token，这些token吸引了不成比例的注意力却携带有限的局部信息，但它们在生成模型中的作用尚未被充分探索。我们发现，这一现象同时出现在现代表示自编码器（RAE）-DiT管线的编码器和去噪器中：预训练的ViT编码器会产生异常表示，而DiT自身也会在内部（尤其是中间层）发展出异常token。此外，简单地掩码高范数token并不能提升性能，这说明问题不仅由少数极端值引起，而是与局部块语义的破坏更密切相关。为解决此问题，我们引入了双阶段寄存器（Dual-Stage Registers, DSR），一种针对两个组件的基于寄存器的干预方法：训练好的寄存器（可用时）、递归测试时寄存器（otherwise），以及针对去噪器的扩散寄存器。在ImageNet和大规模文本到图像生成任务上，这些干预措施一致地减少了异常伪影并提升了生成质量。我们的结果强调了异常点控制是构建更强DiT的重要部分。

</details>


### [74] [Syn4D: A Multiview Synthetic 4D Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05207)
*Zeren Jiang,Yushi Lan,Yihang Luo,Yufan Deng,Zihang Lai,Edgar Sucar,Christian Rupprecht,Iro Laina,Diane Larlus,Chuanxia Zheng,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Syn4D是一个用于动态场景密集三维重建与跟踪的多视角合成数据集，包含完整的几何与运动标注，支持多时间点多摄像头的3D反投影，在4D重建、点跟踪、摄像机重定向和人体姿态估计等任务上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 动态场景的密集3D重建与跟踪因缺乏高质量（密集、完整、精确几何标注）数据集而进展缓慢，为解决这一数据瓶颈问题而构建Syn4D数据集。

Method: 构建多视角合成动态场景数据集Syn4D，提供真实的相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪和参数化人体姿态标注，核心特性是支持任意像素在任意时间点反投影到任意相机的3D空间。

Result: 在4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向和人体姿态估计等多个下游任务上的广泛实验表明Syn4D数据集具有实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: Syn4D数据集能够促进动态场景理解与时空建模领域的研究。

Original Abstract: Dense 3D reconstruction and tracking of dynamic scenes from monocular video remains an important open challenge in computer vision. Progress in this area has been constrained by the scarcity of high-quality datasets with dense, complete, and accurate geometric annotations. To address this limitation, we introduce Syn4D, a multiview synthetic dataset of dynamic scenes that includes ground-truth camera motion, depth maps, dense tracking, and parametric human pose annotations. A key feature of Syn4D is the ability to unproject any pixel into 3D to any time and to any camera. We conduct extensive evaluations across multiple downstream tasks to demonstrate the utility and effectiveness of the proposed dataset, including 4D scene reconstruction, 3D point tracking, geometry-aware camera retargeting, and human pose estimation. The experimental results highlight Syn4D's potential to facilitate research in dynamic scene understanding and spatiotemporal modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频进行动态场景的密集三维重建与跟踪仍然是计算机视觉中一个重要的开放性挑战。该领域的进展受到高质量数据集（具有密集、完整且精确的几何标注）稀缺的制约。为解决这一限制，我们提出了Syn4D——一个包含真实相机运动、深度图、密集跟踪以及参数化人体姿态标注的动态场景多视角合成数据集。Syn4D的一个关键特性是能够将任意像素反投影到任意时间点和任意相机的三维空间中。我们在多个下游任务上进行了广泛评估，包括4D场景重建、3D点跟踪、几何感知相机重定向和人体姿态估计，以证明所提出数据集的实用性和有效性。实验结果表明，Syn4D具有促进动态场景理解与时空建模研究的潜力。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Noise-Accelerated Kramers Escape and Coherence Resonance in a 5D Neural Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04088)
*Yefan Wu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了有界乘性噪声（在严格Feller边界条件下）不是简单的抖动源，而是能够主动重塑神经兴奋性的动力学力。通过对5D Hodgkin-Huxley型皮层起搏器模型的研究，发现噪声可引发三相景观的跃迁：亚阈值区的随机唤醒（Kramers逃逸）、亚临界Hopf分岔附近的相干共振，以及超阈值振荡区中噪声加速的广义Kramers逃逸，最终将规则起搏转变为高频不规则爆发，解释了病理性过度兴奋的物理机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 内在通道噪声的状态依赖性（受严格Feller边界约束）在神经处理中的动力学作用被长期忽视，现有研究多将其视为抖动源而非主动动力学力。本文旨在揭示这种有界乘性噪声如何作为主动力重塑神经兴奋性，特别是解释其在高维振荡器中诱发病理性过度兴奋的物理机制。

Method: 采用5D Hodgkin-Huxley型皮层起搏器模型，利用全截断半隐式Euler格式确保严格的概率守恒和区域保持积分，通过全面的参数扫描分析噪声诱导的相变，并结合电导扰动实验验证生物鲁棒性。

Result: 发现了受分岔结构调控的三相噪声诱导跃迁景观：在亚阈值区，乘性噪声通过Kramers逃逸触发随机唤醒；在亚临界Hopf分岔附近产生鲁棒的相干共振；在超阈值振荡区，边界约束的乘性噪声主动放大从超极化慢流形逃逸的速率，将规则起搏转变为高频不规则爆发。

Conclusion: 建立了边界约束噪声驱动高维振荡器进入病理性过度兴奋状态的物理严格机制，证明了乘性噪声在神经动力学中不是被动扰动，而是主动塑造神经活动状态的动力学力。

Original Abstract: Intrinsic channel noise is fundamental to neural processing, yet its state-dependent nature, when constrained by strict Feller boundary conditions, is often overlooked. Here, we demonstrate that this bounded multiplicative noise is not merely a source of jitter but an active dynamical force that fundamentally reshapes neural excitability. Investigating a 5D Hodgkin-Huxley-type cortical pacemaker model, we utilize a full-truncation semi-implicit Euler scheme to ensure rigorous probability conservation and domain-preserving integration. Through comprehensive parameter sweeps, we uncover a rich triphasic landscape of noise-induced transitions dictated by the underlying bifurcation structure. Deep in the subthreshold regime, multiplicative noise acts as a constructive force, triggering stochastic awakening via Kramers escape. Near the subcritical Hopf bifurcation, this evolves into highly robust coherence resonance (CR). Crucially, in the supra-threshold oscillatory regime, our framework reveals a striking dynamical shift: a generalized, noise-accelerated Kramers escape. Under extreme multiplicative noise - characteristic of sparse channel populations - strictly bounded fluctuations actively amplify escape rates from the hyperpolarized slow manifold, transforming regular pacing into high-frequency, irregular bursting. Conductance perturbation experiments confirm the profound biological robustness of this transition. These findings establish a physically rigorous mechanism for how boundary-constrained noise drives high-dimensional oscillators toward states of pathological hyperexcitability.

中文摘要翻译: 内在通道噪声是神经处理的基础，但其受严格Feller边界条件约束的状态依赖性往往被忽视。本文证明，这种有界乘性噪声不仅仅是抖动源，更是一种主动动力学力，能够从根本上重塑神经兴奋性。通过研究一个5D Hodgkin-Huxley型皮层起搏器模型，我们采用全截断半隐式Euler格式确保严格的概率守恒和区域保持积分。通过全面的参数扫描，我们揭示了受底层分岔结构调控的丰富三相噪声诱导跃迁景观。在亚阈值区深处，乘性噪声作为建设性力，通过Kramers逃逸触发随机唤醒。在亚临界Hopf分岔附近，这演变为高度鲁棒的相干共振。关键的是，在超阈值振荡区中，我们的框架揭示了一个引人注目的动力学转变：广义的、噪声加速的Kramers逃逸。在极端乘性噪声——稀疏通道群落的特征——下，严格有界的涨落主动放大从超极化慢流形的逃逸速率，将规则起搏转变为高频不规则爆发。电导扰动实验证实了这一转变的深刻生物鲁棒性。这些发现建立了一个物理严格的机制，解释了边界约束噪声如何驱动高维振荡器进入病理性过度兴奋状态。

</details>


### [76] [CTM-AI: A Blueprint for General AI Inspired by a Model of Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04097)
*Haofei Yu,Yining Zhao,Lenore Blum,Manuel Blum,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CTM-AI，一种结合意识图灵机（CTM）与现代基础模型的通用AI系统蓝图，通过多处理器信息选择、整合与交换，在多模态和工具使用任务上取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前AI系统范围狭窄，缺乏人类所具备的灵活、自适应和多感官智能。关于AI能否实现类人通用性甚至意识的长期争论持续存在，本文旨在探索能否从意识理论中汲取灵感，构建通用AI系统的新架构。

Method: 提出CTM-AI蓝图，将意识图灵机（CTM）这一意识的形式化机器模型与当前的基础模型（如视觉-语言模型、API等）相结合。系统包含大量强大处理器，涵盖从专用专家到未专业化的通用学习者。对于待解决问题，从多个处理器中选择、整合并适当交换信息以完成任务。

Result: 在MUStARD上达到72.28，UR-FUNNY上达到72.13，优于多模态和多智能体框架；在StableToolBench和WebArena-Lite上相比基线提升10个以上的百分点。

Conclusion: CTM-AI提供了一个有原则、可测试的通用AI蓝图，其灵感来源于意识模型，在多项基准上取得了显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Despite remarkable advances, today's AI systems remain narrow in scope, falling short of the flexible, adaptive, and multisensory intelligence that characterizes human capabilities. This gap has fueled longstanding debates about whether AI might one day achieve human-like generality or even consciousness, and whether theories of consciousness can inspire new architectures for AI. This paper presents an early blueprint for implementing a general AI system, CTM-AI, combining the Conscious Turing Machine (CTM), a formal machine model of consciousness, with today's foundation models. CTM-AI contains an enormous number of powerful processors ranging from specialized experts (e.g., vision-language models and APIs) to unspecialized general-purpose learners poised to develop their own expertise. Crucially, for whatever problem must be dealt with, information from many processors is selected, integrated, and exchanged appropriately to solve the task. CTM-AI achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on MUStARD (72.28) and UR-FUNNY (72.13), outperforming multimodal and multi-agent frameworks. On tool-using and agentic tasks, CTM-AI achieves 10+ points of improvement on StableToolBench and WebArena-Lite. Overall, CTM-AI offers a principled, testable blueprint for general AI inspired by a model of consciousness.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管取得了显著进展，但当今的AI系统仍然范围狭窄，远未达到人类所具备的灵活、自适应和多感官智能。这一差距引发了长期以来的争论：AI是否有一天能实现类人的通用性甚至意识，以及意识理论能否启发AI的新架构。本文提出了一个实现通用AI系统的早期蓝图——CTM-AI，它将意识图灵机（CTM，一种意识的形式化机器模型）与当今的基础模型相结合。CTM-AI包含数量庞大的强大处理器，从专用专家（例如视觉-语言模型和API）到未专业化的通用学习者（它们随时可以发展出自己的专长）。关键在于，无论需要处理什么问题，系统都会从多个处理器中选择、整合并适当交换信息来解决问题。CTM-AI在MUStARD（72.28）和UR-FUNNY（72.13）上达到了最先进的准确率，超越了多模态和多智能体框架。在工具使用和智能体任务上，CTM-AI在StableToolBench和WebArena-Lite上取得了10个以上的百分点提升。总体而言，CTM-AI受意识模型启发，为通用AI提供了一个有原则、可测试的蓝图。

</details>


### [77] [A foundation model of vision, audition, and language for in-silico neuroscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04326)
*Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jérémy Rapin,Yohann Benchetrit,Teon Brooks,Katelyn Begany,Joséphine Raugel,Hubert Banville,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: TRIBE v2是一个三模态（视频、音频和语言）基础模型，通过超过1000小时的fMRI数据训练，能够高精度预测多种自然和实验条件下的人类大脑活动，并在计算机模拟中复现了数十年的实证研究成果，为探索人脑功能组织提供了统一的AI框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 认知神经科学领域过于碎片化，现有模型各自针对特定实验范式，缺乏一个能够统一刻画人脑认知过程的通用模型。

Method: 构建了一个三模态（视频、音频和语言）基础模型TRIBE v2，利用超过1000小时、涵盖720名被试的fMRI统一数据集进行训练，实现对多种自然和实验条件下人脑活动的高分辨率预测。

Result: 1）模型对新刺激、新任务和新被试的大脑响应预测准确度显著超越传统线性编码模型，精度提升数倍；2）在经典的视觉和神经语言学范式上进行计算机模拟实验，成功复现了数十年来大量实证研究的成果；3）通过提取可解释的潜层特征，揭示了多感官整合的精细脑区拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 人工智能（特别是多模态基础模型）能够作为统一框架，有效探索人脑的功能组织，弥合认知神经科学中不同研究范式之间的鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Cognitive neuroscience is fragmented into specialized models, each tailored to specific experimental paradigms, hence preventing a unified model of cognition in the human brain. Here, we introduce TRIBE v2, a tri-modal (video, audio and language) foundation model capable of predicting human brain activity in a variety of naturalistic and experimental conditions. Leveraging a unified dataset of over 1,000 hours of fMRI across 720 subjects, we demonstrate that our model accurately predicts high-resolution brain responses for novel stimuli, tasks and subjects, superseding traditional linear encoding models, delivering several-fold improvements in accuracy. Critically, TRIBE v2 enables in silico experimentation: tested on seminal visual and neuro-linguistic paradigms, it recovers a variety of results established by decades of empirical research. Finally, by extracting interpretable latent features, TRIBE v2 reveals the fine-grained topography of multisensory integration. These results establish artificial intelligence as a unifying framework for exploring the functional organization of the human brain.

中文摘要翻译: 认知神经科学目前高度碎片化，各类模型仅针对特定的实验范式而设计，因此无法形成统一的人类大脑认知模型。在此，我们提出TRIBE v2——一个三模态（视频、音频和语言）基础模型，能够在多种自然和实验条件下预测人类大脑活动。通过利用涵盖720名被试、超过1,000小时fMRI数据的统一数据集，我们证明该模型能够准确预测新刺激、新任务和新被试的高分辨率大脑响应，超越传统线性编码模型，在精度上实现了数倍的提升。关键的是，TRIBE v2支持计算机模拟实验：在经典的视觉和神经语言学范式上进行测试时，它能够复现数十年来大量实证研究建立的结果。最后，通过提取可解释的潜层特征，TRIBE v2揭示了多感官整合的精细脑区拓扑结构。这些结果确立了人工智能作为探索人脑功能组织的统一框架。

</details>


### [78] [Think-Aloud Reshapes Automated Cognitive Model Discovery Beyond Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05091)
*Hanbo Xie,Akshay K. Jagadish,Lan Pan,Robert C. Wilson*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用“出声思考”（Think Aloud）语言痕迹作为额外数据约束，辅助大语言模型自动发现认知模型。在风险决策领域，该方法显著提升了模型在留出数据上的预测性能，并使69.4%参与者的模型结构从“显式比较”转向“综合效用”类别，表明过程级语言数据不仅能改善模型拟合，还能系统性地重塑认知模型结构，揭示仅靠行为数据无法恢复的机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 仅依赖行为数据发现的认知模型通常是不确定的（under-determined），需要额外的数据约束来提高模型的可辨识性和预测能力。

Method: 利用大语言模型进行自动化模型发现，在风险决策领域引入“出声思考”（Think Aloud）语言痕迹作为行为数据之外的过程级数据约束，共同指导认知模型的自动构建。

Result: 1) 加入出声思考后发现的新模型在留出数据上预测性能显著提升；2) 69.4%的参与者模型从“显式比较”（Explicit comparator）结构转变为“综合效用”（Integrated utility）结构。

Conclusion: 过程级语言数据不仅能改进模型拟合质量，还能系统性地重塑认知模型的结构类别，揭示仅凭行为数据无法恢复的认知机制。

Original Abstract: Computational cognitive models discovered using large language models have so far relied solely on behavioral data. However, it is well-known that models produced from the behavioral trajectory alone are typically under-determined. In this work, we explore the use of Think Aloud traces as an additional form of data constraint during automated model discovery. When applied to the domain of risky decision-making, we find that the models discovered with think-aloud achieve significantly improved predictive performance on held-out data. Additionally, we find that the discovered models belong to different structural classes than those discovered from behavior alone for the majority of participants (69.4\%), specifically, it shifts from Explicit comparator towards Integrated utility. These results suggest that process-level language data not only improve model fit, but also systematically reshape the structure of the discovered cognitive models, enabling the identification of mechanisms that are not recoverable from behavior alone.

中文摘要翻译: 目前，利用大语言模型发现的认知计算模型完全依赖于行为数据。然而，众所周知，仅从行为轨迹生成的模型通常是不确定的。在本工作中，我们探索将“出声思考”痕迹作为自动化模型发现过程中的一种额外数据约束。当应用于风险决策领域时，我们发现基于出声思考发现的模型在留出数据上取得了显著更优的预测性能。此外，对于大多数参与者（69.4%），我们发现所发现的模型属于与仅从行为数据发现的模型不同的结构类别，具体表现为模型从“显式比较”转向“综合效用”。这些结果表明，过程级语言数据不仅能改善模型拟合，还能系统性地重塑所发现的认知模型结构，从而识别出仅凭行为数据无法恢复的机制。

</details>

### [79] [External Validation of Deep Learning Models for BI-RADS Breast Density Prediction from Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05082)
*Yuxuan Chen,Arianna Bunnell,Yanqi Xu,Haoyan Yang,Thomas K. Wolfgruber,John A. Shepherd,Yiqiu Shen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究在独立外部队列上验证了三种深度学习模型（DenseNet121、ViT-B/32、ResNet50）从乳腺超声检查预测乳腺密度。模型在极致致密型乳腺中表现最佳（AUROC 0.868-0.899），DenseNet121总体最优（micro-averaged AUROC 0.885）。外推至10年风险预测时，AI密度结合年龄的预测能力低于传统钼靶密度，但无统计学显著性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证深度学习模型从乳腺超声图像预测乳腺密度在外部独立队列（不同种族构成）上的泛化能力，并评估AI衍生的密度评分在长期乳腺癌风险预测中的下游应用价值。

Method: 使用DenseNet121、ViT-B/32和ResNet50三种深度学习模型，在2,000例超声检查（含500例癌症病例和1,500例阴性对照）的外部验证集上，以患者水平的AUROC评估四种密度类别（A/B/C/D）的分类性能。下游将年龄和AI密度代入Tyrer-Cuzick模型进行10年风险预测，与基于年龄和钼靶密度的参考模型比较。

Result: 三类模型在极致致密型（D类）中表现最佳（AUROC 0.868-0.899），脂肪型（A类）0.814-0.838、散在型（B类）0.764-0.799，不均匀致密型（C类）较低（0.699-0.729）。DenseNet121总体最优（micro-averaged AUROC 0.885）。外部测试与内部测试性能相当。风险预测中，AI密度+年龄的AUROC（0.541）低于钼靶密度+年龄的AUROC（0.570），但差异不显著（p=0.23）。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型在乳腺密度评估上具有良好的外部泛化能力，尤其对极致致密型乳腺；不均匀致密型仍是难点，需要针对性优化。AI衍生的密度评分在十年风险预测中尚未显著优于传统钼靶密度。

Original Abstract: We externally validated three deep learning models (DenseNet121, ViT-B/32, and ResNet50) for predicting mammographic breast density from breast ultrasound exams on an independent cohort. The external validation set comprised 2,000 ultrasound exams, including 500 cancer cases defined by an initial negative exam (BI-RADS 1 or 2) followed by a cancer diagnosis within 6 months to 10 years, and 1,500 negative controls matched by manufacturer and study year. Performance was measured using patient-level AUROC across four density categories: A (fatty), B (scattered), C (heterogeneous), and D (extremely dense). As a downstream assessment, we also evaluated 10-year risk prediction by incorporating age and AI-derived density into the Tyrer-Cuzick model and comparing performance against a reference model using age and mammography-reported density. All three models performed best in extremely dense breasts (AUROC 0.868-0.899), with strong performance in fatty (0.814-0.838) and scattered density (0.764-0.799), and lower performance in heterogeneously dense breasts (0.699-0.729). DenseNet121 achieved the highest overall performance (micro-averaged AUROC 0.885), and performance across categories was comparable between internal and external testing. For risk modeling, age combined with AI-derived density yielded a lower AUROC than age combined with mammography-reported density (0.541 vs. 0.570; p = 0.23), with no statistically significant difference. These findings indicate that deep learning models generalize well to external data with different racial composition for breast density assessment. While performance is strongest in extremely dense breasts, heterogeneously dense remains more challenging, highlighting the need for targeted optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 我们在独立队列上外部验证了三种深度学习模型（DenseNet121、ViT-B/32和ResNet50）从乳腺超声检查预测乳腺摄影密度的能力。外部验证集包含2,000例超声检查，其中500例癌症病例定义为初始检查阴性（BI-RADS 1或2）后6个月至10年内确诊乳腺癌，以及1,500例按制造商和研究年份匹配的阴性对照。使用患者水平的AUROC评估四种密度类别的性能：A类（脂肪型）、B类（散在型）、C类（不均匀致密型）和D类（极致致密型）。作为下游评估，我们将年龄和AI衍生的密度纳入Tyrer-Cuzick模型进行10年风险预测，并与使用年龄和乳腺摄影报告密度的参考模型进行性能比较。所有三个模型在极致致密型乳腺中表现最佳（AUROC 0.868-0.899），在脂肪型（0.814-0.838）和散在型（0.764-0.799）中表现强劲，在不均匀致密型乳腺中表现较低（0.699-0.729）。DenseNet121取得了最高的总体性能（微平均AUROC 0.885），且各类别的性能在内部测试与外部测试之间相当。在风险建模中，年龄与AI衍生密度的组合产生的AUROC低于年龄与乳腺摄影报告密度的组合（0.541 vs. 0.570；p = 0.23），差异无统计学显著性。这些发现表明，深度学习模型在乳腺密度评估中能够很好地泛化到具有不同种族构成的外部数据。虽然模型在极致致密型乳腺中表现最强，但不均匀致密型仍然更具挑战性，凸显了针对性优化的必要性。

</details>


### [80] [CTseg: A Tool for Brain CT Segmentation, Spatial Normalisation, and Volumetrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05154)
*Mikael Brudfors*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: CTseg是一个基于Multi-Brain生成建模框架的免费脑CT分割、空间归一化和体积测量软件，可将SPM的分析流程从MRI扩展至CT。本文通过配对MR/CT扫描数据从四个维度系统验证了CTseg的性能，结果表明其显著优于直接将SPM的MRI统一分割应用于CT图像的基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CTseg虽已在卒中、痴呆、脑形态测量等临床研究中被采用，但一直缺乏针对独立参考标准的系统性验证。本文旨在通过配对MR/CT头扫描数据，从分割精度、空间归一化一致性、脑体积一致性及下游分类性能四个维度对CTseg进行全面评估。

Method: 使用配对MR/CT头扫描数据，从四个维度评估CTseg：(1) 以MRI导出的银标准为参考评估分割精度；(2) 通过组平均锐度和体素变异系数评估空间归一化一致性；(3) 采用组内相关系数和Bland-Altman分析评估脑体积一致性；(4) 利用归一化组织图进行性别分类的下游任务评估。基线方法为直接将SPM的MRI统一分割应用于CT图像。

Result: CTseg在分割和空间归一化方面显著优于基线方法，在全脑体积（TBV）一致性上更强，在颅内总体积（TIV）一致性上与基线相当。

Conclusion: CTseg作为一种免费的脑CT分割、空间归一化和体积测量工具，经过系统验证表现出优于直接使用SPM MRI分割方法的性能，可有效将SPM的分析链从MRI扩展至CT，且无需预处理或重采样，适用于常规医院CT扫描。软件和实验代码均开源可用。

Original Abstract: This paper presents and validates CTseg, a freely available software for brain CT segmentation, spatial normalisation, and volumetrics. CTseg builds on the Multi-Brain generative modelling framework, providing a CT-specific pipeline that produces tissue maps, deformation fields, and brain volume estimates in the same format as SPM's unified segmentation, thereby extending SPM's established analysis chain from MRI to CT. CTseg is designed for routine hospital CT scans without requiring preprocessing or resampling in deployment. Although CTseg has been adopted in clinical research spanning, among other things, stroke, dementia, and brain morphometry, a systematic validation against an independent reference standard has been lacking. Using paired MR/CT head scans, we evaluate CTseg across four dimensions: segmentation accuracy against an MRI-derived silver standard; spatial normalisation consistency through group-average sharpness and voxelwise coefficient of variation; brain volume agreement via intraclass correlation and Bland-Altman analysis; and downstream sex classification performance from normalised tissue maps. As a baseline, we apply SPM's MRI-based unified segmentation directly to the CT images. CTseg significantly outperformed this baseline for segmentation and normalisation, showed stronger TBV agreement, and achieved comparable TIV agreement. CTseg is freely available at https://github.com/WCHN/CTseg, and all experiment code is included in the repository for full reproducibility.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出并验证了CTseg——一款用于脑CT分割、空间归一化和体积测量的免费软件。CTseg基于Multi-Brain生成建模框架，提供了专用于CT的处理流程，可生成与SPM统一分割相同格式的组织图、变形场和脑体积估计，从而将SPM成熟的分析链从MRI扩展至CT。CTseg专为常规医院CT扫描设计，部署时无需预处理或重采样。尽管CTseg已被应用于包括卒中、痴呆和脑形态测量在内的临床研究中，但一直缺乏针对独立参考标准的系统性验证。利用配对MR/CT头扫描数据，我们从四个维度评估CTseg：以MRI导出的银标准为参考的分割精度；通过组平均锐度和体素变异系数评估的空间归一化一致性；通过组内相关系数和Bland-Altman分析评估的脑体积一致性；以及基于归一化组织图的下游性别分类性能。作为基线，我们将SPM基于MRI的统一分割直接应用于CT图像。CTseg在分割和空间归一化方面显著优于该基线，在全脑体积一致性上表现更强，在颅内总体积一致性上与基线相当。CTseg可在https://github.com/WCHN/CTseg免费获取，所有实验代码均包含在仓库中以实现完全可重复性。

</details>


### [81] [MRI-Eval: A Tiered Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Performance on MRI Physics and GE Scanner Operations Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05175)
*Perry E. Radau*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRI-Eval，一个针对MRI物理和GE扫描仪操作知识的分层基准测试。研究发现，在多项选择题（MCQ）中，前沿模型准确率高达93.2%-97.1%，但在无选项的自由回忆（stem-only）设置下，准确率骤降至37.1%-61.1%，尤其是GE扫描仪操作知识仅13.8%-29.8%，表明高MCQ表现可能掩盖薄弱的自由文本回忆能力，应谨慎使用LLM输出进行GE特定协议指导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MRI大语言模型基准测试主要依赖教科书式多项选择题，顶级闭源模型已得分很高，缺乏区分度；且没有系统基准评估面向研究型MRI实践至关重要的供应商特定扫描仪操作知识。

Method: 构建了包含1365道评分题目的MRI-Eval基准，覆盖9个类别和3个难度层级，题目来源包括教科书、GE扫描仪手册、编程课程材料和专家生成题目。采用三种评估方式：标准多项选择题（MCQ）作为主要评估、无选项的自由回忆（stem-only）使用独立LLM评判、以及带有错误用户主张的启动式无选项（primed stem-only）。评估了GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Llama 3.3 70B五个模型家族。

Result: MCQ整体准确率93.2%-97.1%，GE扫描仪操作在所有模型中得分最低（88.2%-94.6%）。在无选项设置下，前沿模型准确率降至58.4%-61.1%，Llama 3.3 70B降至37.1%；GE扫描仪操作无选项准确率仅为13.8%-29.8%。

Conclusion: 高多项选择题表现可能掩盖薄弱的自由文本回忆能力，尤其是供应商特定的操作知识。MRI-Eval作为相对比较基准而非绝对能力度量最有价值，支持在使用LLM原始输出进行GE特定协议指导时保持谨慎。

Original Abstract: Background: Existing MRI LLM benchmarks rely mainly on review-book multiple-choice questions, where top proprietary models already score highly, limiting discrimination. No systematic benchmark has evaluated vendor-specific scanner operational knowledge central to research MRI practice. Purpose: We developed MRI-Eval, a tiered benchmark for relative model comparison on MRI physics and GE scanner operations knowledge using primary multiple-choice questions (MCQ), with stem-only and primed diagnostic conditions as complementary analyses. Methods: MRI-Eval includes 1365 scored items across nine categories and three difficulty tiers from textbooks, GE scanner manuals, programming course materials, and expert-generated questions. Five model families were evaluated (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Llama 3.3 70B). MCQ was primary; stem-only removed options and used an independent LLM judge; primed stem-only tested responses to incorrect user claims. Results: Overall MCQ accuracy was 93.2% to 97.1%. GE scanner operations was the lowest category for every model (88.2% to 94.6%). In stem-only, frontier-model accuracy fell to 58.4% to 61.1%, and Llama 3.3 70B fell to 37.1%; GE scanner operations stem-only accuracy was 13.8% to 29.8%. Conclusion: High MCQ performance can mask weak free-text recall, especially for vendor-specific operational knowledge. MRI-Eval is most informative as a relative comparison benchmark rather than an absolute competency measure and supports caution in using raw LLM outputs for GE-specific protocol guidance.

中文摘要翻译: 背景：现有的MRI大语言模型基准测试主要依赖教科书式的多项选择题，顶级闭源模型已在此类题目上得分很高，限制了区分能力。目前尚无系统基准评估对研究型MRI实践至关重要的供应商特定扫描仪操作知识。目的：我们开发了MRI-Eval，一个用于相对模型比较的分层基准，评估MRI物理和GE扫描仪操作知识，采用主要多项选择题（MCQ）作为主要评估方式，并以无选项自由回忆和启动式诊断条件作为补充分析。方法：MRI-Eval包含1365道评分题目，涵盖九个类别和三个难度层级，题目来源包括教科书、GE扫描仪手册、编程课程材料以及专家生成题目。评估了五个模型家族（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro、Llama 3.3 70B）。主要评估方式为MCQ；无选项评估移除了选项并使用独立LLM评判器；启动式无选项评估测试模型对错误用户主张的响应。结果：MCQ整体准确率为93.2%至97.1%。GE扫描仪操作是每个模型中得分最低的类别（88.2%至94.6%）。在无选项评估中，前沿模型准确率降至58.4%至61.1%，Llama 3.3 70B降至37.1%；GE扫描仪操作的无选项准确率仅为13.8%至29.8%。结论：高MCQ表现可能掩盖薄弱的自由文本回忆能力，尤其是供应商特定的操作知识。MRI-Eval作为相对比较基准而非绝对能力度量最有信息价值，并支持在使用LLM原始输出进行GE特定协议指导时保持谨慎。

</details>
