### [82] [Near-field channel estimation via wavefront parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04238)
*Heedong Do,Namyoon Lee,Angel Lozano*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对近场视距条件下的多天线信道估计问题，通过将弯曲波前展开为球面幂级数多项式，转化为多维多项式相位估计问题，并应用一种新的多项式相位估计器，实现了信道估计精度与复杂度之间的更优权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近场环境中多天线信道的波前呈弯曲状，传统的平面波假设不再适用，需要更精确的信道估计方法，同时希望在估计精度与计算复杂度之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 将弯曲波前通过球面的幂级数展开表示为多项式形式，从而将阵列上的信道估计建模为多维多项式相位估计问题，并采用一种能够处理任意维度和多项式阶数的新开发的多项式相位估计器。

Result: 所提出的方法在信道估计精度与复杂度之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于多项式相位估计的方法能有效处理近场视距条件下的多天线信道估计问题，优于传统方法。

Original Abstract: This paper deals with the estimation of multiantenna channels in the line-of-sight conditions that are prevalent in the near field. By expressing the curved wavefront as a polynomial via a power series expansion of a sphere, the estimation of the channel over the array can be formulated as a multidimensional polynomial phase estimation problem. The application of a newly developed polynomial phase estimator, able of handling arbitrary dimensions and polynomial degrees, yields a superior tradeoff between channel estimation accuracy and complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究近场中普遍存在的视距条件下的多天线信道估计问题。通过将弯曲波前表示为球面幂级数展开的多项式形式，阵列上的信道估计可被建模为多维多项式相位估计问题。应用一种新开发的、能够处理任意维度和多项式阶数的多项式相位估计器，在信道估计精度与复杂度之间实现了更优的权衡。

</details>


### [83] [Large Gain Degradation of Reflective Intelligent Surfaces in Realistic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04354)
*Dmitry Chizhik,Jinfeng Du*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文探讨了可重构智能表面（RIS）在城市非视距场景中的实际增益，发现其相比现有环境散射机制（如路灯杆散射、拐角绕射）仅有微弱优势，且信道角度扩展会显著降低RIS性能，挑战了RIS在真实环境中用于覆盖扩展的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 评估RIS在真实城市非视距（NLOS）环境中相比现有环境传播机制（如路灯散射和拐角绕射）的实际覆盖增益，质疑RIS在现实场景中的有用性。

Method: 推导了RIS增益因信道角度扩展而退化的简单公式，并在28 GHz频段下，对0.3 m × 0.3 m和1 m × 1 m RIS与城市街角环境散射机制进行了定量比较分析。

Result: 0.3 m × 0.3 m RIS在28 GHz、200米街角处仅比环境散射多提供约5 dB功率；考虑角度扩展后，RIS功率下降约14 dB，低于环境散射。1 m × 1 m RIS在考虑角度扩展导致的25 dB退化后，优势不足2 dB。

Conclusion: 在真实环境中，角度扩展会严重削弱RIS的增益，使其相比现有环境散射机制优势甚微，这质疑了RIS辅助覆盖扩展在实际场景中的有用性。

Original Abstract: Reflective Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) are considered promising in improving coverage in Non-Line of Sight (NLOS) wireless links, especially at mm wave or higher frequency bands. Coverage provided by RIS is here compared to coverage from such ambient propagation mechanisms as scattering from street poles (e.g. lampposts), and corner diffraction. A simple formula for RIS gain degradation due to channel angle spread is derived. It is found an ideal 0.3 m x 0.3 m RIS at 28 GHz promises to deliver only about 5 dB more power at 200 m around an urban street corner than the ambient scatter already there. Consideration of angle spread brings about some 14 dB drop in RIS power, bringing it well below ambient mechanisms. A 1 m x 1 m RIS at 28 GHz, offers under 2 dB advantage over ambient scatter after including the 25 dB gain degradation due to angle spread. This raises questions about usefulness of RIS-assisted coverage extension in realistic environments.

中文摘要翻译: 可重构智能表面（RIS）被认为在改善非视距（NLOS）无线链路覆盖方面具有前景，尤其是在毫米波或更高频段。本文将RIS提供的覆盖与城市环境中的环境传播机制（如路灯杆散射和拐角绕射）进行了比较。推导了一个用于计算RIS增益因信道角度扩展而退化的简单公式。研究发现，在28 GHz下，一个理想的0.3 m × 0.3 m RIS在城市街角200米处仅比已有的环境散射多提供约5 dB的功率。考虑角度扩展后，RIS功率下降约14 dB，使其远低于环境机制。在28 GHz下，一个1 m × 1 m RIS在计入角度扩展导致的25 dB增益退化后，相比环境散射的优势不足2 dB。这质疑了RIS辅助覆盖扩展在真实环境中的有用性。

</details>


### [84] [Near-Field Channel Estimation for Extremely Large-Scale Circular RIS-Aided mmWave MIMO-NOMA System with Beam Squint Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04380)
*Wanyuan Cai,Shunli Hong,Youming Li,Menglei Sheng,Mingjun Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对6G毫米波RIS系统中的近场信道估计问题，提出了一种基于超大圆形RIS（XL-CRIS）架构和低秩张量分解的多级信道估计框架，并推导了向量形式的克拉美-罗界作为性能基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在波束斜视效应下，近场信道估计对6G毫米波可重构智能表面系统至关重要，现有方法难以兼顾角度不变近场区域构建与高效参数提取。

Method: 首先设计超大圆形RIS架构以构建角度不变近场区域，形成统一信道建模框架；然后将接收到的宽带MIMO-NOMA信号建模为三阶张量，开发多级信道估计框架，将多变量问题分解为若干低维子问题，通过共享置换矩阵自然保留路径参数配对；最后推导向量形式CRB作为理论性能基准。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，并与已有方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的XL-CRIS架构和多级张量分解信道估计方法能够有效应对波束斜视效应，实现高效的近场信道估计。

Original Abstract: Near-field channel estimation under beam squint effect is critical to future 6G millimeter-wave (mmWave) systems equipped with reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS). In this paper, firstly, we design an extremely large-scale circular RIS (XL-CRIS) architecture to construct an angle-invariant near-field region for MIMO-NOMA system, which can maintain a constant effective aperture, allowing for a unified channel modeling framework. Then, to enable efficient parameter extraction, we model the received wideband MIMO-NOMA signal as a third-order tensor which is used to develop a multi-stage channel estimation framework. Accordingly, we decompose the multi-variable problem into several low-dimensional sub-problems, while naturally preserving path-wise parameter pairing through the shared permutation matrix. Finally, we derive a vector-form CRB as a theoretical performance benchmark. To illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, numerical experiments are carried out and compared with the discussed methods.

中文摘要翻译: 在配备可重构智能表面（RIS）的未来6G毫米波（mmWave）系统中，波束斜视效应下的近场信道估计至关重要。本文首先设计了一种超大圆形RIS（XL-CRIS）架构，用于为MIMO-NOMA系统构建角度不变的近场区域，该架构能保持恒定的有效孔径，从而建立统一的信道建模框架。然后，为实现高效参数提取，我们将接收到的宽带MIMO-NOMA信号建模为三阶张量，并据此开发了一种多级信道估计框架。通过该框架，我们将多变量问题分解为若干低维子问题，同时通过共享置换矩阵自然保留路径参数配对。最后，我们推导了向量形式的克拉美-罗界（CRB）作为理论性能基准。通过数值实验并与已有方法进行对比，验证了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [85] [Deep Learning-Based Computer Vision for Beam Selection and Proactive Blockage Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04514)
*Sachira Karunasena,Erfan Khordad,Tom Drummond,Rajitha Senanayake*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出两个视觉辅助框架解决毫米波通信的两大挑战：利用RGB图像和接收功率进行波束选择（准确率98.96%），以及基于改进目标跟踪器的主动阻塞预测（三帧提前预测准确率>98%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 毫米波通信面临两大关键挑战：一是传播损耗需要昂贵的窄波束对齐，二是穿透损耗导致视距路径阻塞时链路中断。现有方法未充分解决同时存在非均匀移动的发射器和障碍物场景下的阻塞预测问题。

Method: 1）视觉辅助波束选择框架：融合RGB图像与接收功率分布，用于高效发射器识别和波束预测；2）主动阻塞预测框架：采用改进的目标跟踪器结合基于加权质心的深度估计，首次分析发射器和障碍物同时非均匀移动的场景。

Result: 波束选择框架达到98.96%的Top-5波束预测准确率，在所有指标上超越现有最优方法至少6%；阻塞预测框架在完全未见的数据上实现三帧提前预测准确率超过98%。

Conclusion: 提出的两个视觉辅助框架分别有效解决了毫米波通信的传播损耗和穿透损耗问题，在波束选择和阻塞预测任务上均取得了显著的性能提升，并建立了强大的性能基准。

Original Abstract: Millimeter-wave communication faces two critical challenges: propagation losses requiring costly narrow-beam alignment, and penetration losses causing link failures from blocked line-of-sight paths. We address propagation loss through a novel vision-aided beam selection framework that integrates RGB imagery with received power profiles for efficient transmitter identification and beam prediction. This framework achieves 98.96% top-5 beam prediction accuracy, surpassing current state-of-the-art methods by at least 6% across all metrics. We address penetration loss through a proactive blockage prediction framework using a modified object tracker with weighted centroid-based depth estimation. This represents the first analysis of simultaneous non-uniform mobility of both transmitters and obstacles. Evaluated on completely unseen data, this framework achieves over 98% accuracy in predicting blockages up to three frames ahead, establishing strong performance benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 毫米波通信面临两个关键挑战：传播损耗需要昂贵的窄波束对齐，以及穿透损耗会导致视距路径阻塞时链路中断。我们通过一种新颖的视觉辅助波束选择框架来解决传播损耗问题，该框架将RGB图像与接收功率分布进行融合，用于高效的发射器识别和波束预测。该框架实现了98.96%的Top-5波束预测准确率，在所有指标上超越当前最优方法至少6%。我们通过一种基于改进目标跟踪器的主动阻塞预测框架来解决穿透损耗问题，该跟踪器采用基于加权质心的深度估计。这是首次对发射器和障碍物同时具有非均匀移动性的场景进行分析。在完全未见的数据上进行评估，该框架在三帧提前预测阻塞任务上实现了超过98%的准确率，建立了强大的性能基准。

</details>


### [86] [Two-Point Resolution in Spectral Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04601)
*Xiaole He,Ping Liu,Junling Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文建立了单快照复数两点超分辨率理论，推导了振幅比和相对相位影响下可分辨性的上下界，发现相位在特定区域可提升分辨率缩放阶数，并验证了多种算法的最优性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有两点超分辨率理论大多假设实数或同相位模型，忽略了相对相位对分辨率极限的影响。本文旨在建立包含非平凡相对相位的复数两点超分辨率理论，揭示相位不仅是噪声因素，更是可被利用以提升稳定可分辨性的关键因素。

Method: 考虑复数两点模型（不等幅、非平凡相对相位），从信号处理角度推导超分辨率上界（保证可分辨）和下界（无法稳定重建），分析振幅比和相对相位对源数检测和位置估计分辨率极限的影响，并通过数值实验验证理论边界及算法最优性。

Result: 同相区保持经典缩放阶：源数检测为(σ/m)^{1/2}，位置估计为(σ/m)^{1/3}；大相位区缩放阶提升至σ/m和(σ/m)^{1/2}。ℓ0、ML和ESPRIT算法达到最优，SVT、MUSIC和凸方法非最优。

Conclusion: 相位是超分辨率中的关键因素而非干扰项，可利用相位信息显著提升分辨率极限。

Original Abstract: Two-point super-resolution is an important problem in many signal processing applications. In this paper, we aim to establish a resolution theory for two-point super-resolution from a single snapshot. We consider a complex two-point model with unequal amplitudes and a nontrivial relative phase, and derive super-resolution upper bounds (SRUs) guaranteeing resolvability as well as super-resolution lower bounds (SRLs) below which stable reconstruction is impossible. The resulting bounds provide an explicit characterization of how the amplitude ratio and, more importantly, the relative phase affect the resolution limit for both source-number detection and location estimation. In the in-phase regime, the classical resolution exponents are retained: \((σ/m)^{1/2}\) for source-number detection and \((σ/m)^{1/3}\) for location estimation. In the out-of-phase regimes, the phase term significantly changes the resolution limit: it acts as a direct subtractive term in the near-endpoint regime, and improves the scaling orders in the large-phase regime to \(σ/m\) for source-number detection and \((σ/m)^{1/2}\) for location estimation. Extensive numerical experiments across different phase regimes and reconstruction algorithms validate the predicted scaling laws and theoretical resolution boundaries. Moreover, comparison with our resolution limit in all phase regimes reveals the optimality of \(\ell_0\), ML, and ESPRIT algorithms, and the non-optimality of SVT, MUSIC, and the convex method, a finding that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been reported before. Collectively, our results show that the phase of amplitudes is not merely a nuisance in super-resolution, but a key factor that can be exploited to improve stable resolvability.

中文摘要翻译: 两点超分辨率是许多信号处理应用中的一个重要问题。本文旨在建立基于单快照的两点超分辨率理论。我们考虑一个具有不等幅和非平凡相对相位的复数两点模型，推导了保证可分辨性的超分辨率上界（SRU）以及低于其则无法稳定重建的超分辨率下界（SRL）。所得边界显式刻画了振幅比以及更重要的是相对相位如何影响源数检测和位置估计的分辨率极限。在同相区域，保留了经典分辨率指数：源数检测为(σ/m)^{1/2}，位置估计为(σ/m)^{1/3}。在异相区域，相位项显著改变了分辨率极限：在近端点区域作为直接减性项，在大相位区域将缩放阶提升至σ/m（源数检测）和(σ/m)^{1/2}（位置估计）。跨不同相位区域和重建算法的广泛数值实验验证了预测的缩放定律和理论分辨率边界。此外，与所有相位区域中分辨率极限的比较揭示了ℓ0、ML和ESPRIT算法的最优性，以及SVT、MUSIC和凸方法的非最优性，据我们所知，这一发现此前尚未被报道。综上，我们的结果表明，振幅相位不仅仅是超分辨率中的干扰因素，而是可以被利用来提升稳定可分辨性的关键因素。

</details>


### [87] [Multi-AP Cooperative Beamforming for Cell-Free ISAC Networks: Balancing Communication SINR and Sensing SCNR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04623)
*Jijin Guo,Lixin Li,Yufeng Zheng,Dongwei Zhao,Wensheng Lin,Zhu Han*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对无小区通信感知一体化（ISAC）系统中的多接入点（AP）协同波束赋形问题，提出了一种基于半定松弛的凸优化方法，在通信速率约束下最大化感知信杂噪比（SCNR），有效平衡了通信与感知的双重功能目标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单目标方法无法解决无小区ISAC系统中AP地理分布带来的协调复杂性和资源共享冲突，需要一种能同时兼顾通信与感知性能的多AP协同波束赋形方案。

Method: 将多AP协同波束赋形问题建模为非凸二次约束二次规划（QCQP），通过半定松弛（SDR）将其转化为可解的凸半定规划（SDP），从而在多项式时间内获得高效解，避免了传统交替优化方法的局部收敛问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在通信信干噪比（SINR）和感知信杂噪比（SCNR）两方面均优于现有方案，验证了其在平衡双功能目标方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于半定松弛的协同波束赋形方法能够有效解决无小区ISAC系统的资源分配冲突，实现通信与感知性能的联合优化。

Original Abstract: Cell-free integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems are facing the resource allocation challenges due to the deployment of access points (APs) and conflicting beamforming requirements between the communication and sensing functions. Unlike traditional ISAC architectures, the geographic distribution of APs introduces coordination complexity and resource-sharing conflicts that existing single-objective methods cannot adequately address. To address this challenge, we formulate an optimization problem for multi-AP cooperative beamforming that maximizes the sensing signal-to-clutter-plus-noise ratio (SCNR) under the communication rate constraints. The non-convex quadratically constrained quadratic program is transformed into a tractable convex semidefinite program via semidefinite relaxation, enabling efficient polynomial-time solutions and overcoming the local convergence limitations of traditional alternating optimization approaches. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves superior performance in both communication signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) and SCNR compared to existing schemes, confirming its effectiveness for balancing dual-functional objectives.

中文摘要翻译: 无小区通信感知一体化（ISAC）系统由于接入点（AP）的部署方式以及通信与感知功能之间相互冲突的波束赋形需求，面临着资源分配的挑战。与传统ISAC架构不同，AP的地理分布引入了现有单目标方法无法充分解决的协调复杂性和资源共享冲突。为应对这一挑战，我们构建了一个多AP协同波束赋形的优化问题，在通信速率约束下最大化感知信杂噪比（SCNR）。通过半定松弛，将非凸二次约束二次规划转化为易于处理的凸半定规划，从而在多项式时间内获得高效解，并克服了传统交替优化方法的局部收敛局限性。仿真结果表明，与现有方案相比，所提方法在通信信干噪比（SINR）和SCNR两方面均展现出更优的性能，证实了其在平衡双功能目标方面的有效性。

</details>


### [88] [Spacing-Based Coupling Radiation Control in Pinching-Antennas Systems for Heterogeneous NOMA Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04655)
*Ishtiaque Ahmed,Leila Musavian*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了在可调辐射模型下，基于Pinching天线系统（PASS）的语义通信（SC）性能，通过NOMA支持异构用户，并利用交替优化（AO）方法最大化语义频谱效率（SE）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PASS覆盖机制多针对等功率Pinching天线的传统比特通信，灵活的辐射控制（特别是针对异构语义用户和比特用户）尚缺乏研究。

Method: 基于天线-波导间距可调的辐射模型，通过NOMA服务异构用户，采用交替优化（AO）方法联合优化用户功率分配和Pinching天线位置，在满足比特用户QoS、SIC可行性和最小天线间距约束下最大化语义SE。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的比例功率PASS模型在不同几何和数值设置下，相比传统基准方案能提供更高的语义频谱效率。

Conclusion: 可调辐射控制的PASS结合NOMA与AO优化，能有效提升语义通信系统的频谱效率，验证了所提模型的有效性与优越性。

Original Abstract: Pinching-antennas systems (PASS) offer reconfigurable wireless channels via low-cost dielectric mediums by creating line-of-sight (LoS) communication links. Most of the existing PASS cover mechanisms of equal power pinching antennas for conventional bit-based communication, whereas flexible radiation control remains largely unexplored, particularly for heterogeneous semantic and bit users. In this paper, we investigate the performance of semantic communication (SC) using an adjustable radiation model over PASS, where the coupling strength between the dielectric waveguide and each pinching antenna is determined by the antenna-waveguide spacing. Specifically, the non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-assisted heterogeneous users are served by multiple pinching antennas using spacing-controlled adjustable radiation ratios. Uunder this setting, we maximize the semantic spectral efficiency (SE) subject to the bit-user quality of service (QoS) requirement, successive interference cancellation (SIC) feasibility, and the minimum adjacent antennas spacing constraint. An alternating optimization (AO) approach optimizes users power allocation and positions of pinching antennas. Simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proportional power PASS model in providing higher semantic SE in different geometrical and numerical settings compared to conventional benchmark schemes.

中文摘要翻译: Pinching天线系统（PASS）通过创建视距（LoS）通信链路，利用低成本介质实现可重构无线信道。现有的大多数PASS覆盖机制针对传统比特通信采用等功率Pinching天线，而灵活的辐射控制——特别是针对异构语义用户和比特用户——仍未得到充分探索。本文研究了在PASS上使用可调辐射模型的语义通信（SC）性能，其中介质波导与每个Pinching天线之间的耦合强度由天线-波导间距决定。具体而言，非正交多址接入（NOMA）辅助的异构用户由多个Pinching天线服务，这些天线采用间距可控的可调辐射比。在此设置下，我们在满足比特用户服务质量（QoS）要求、串行干扰消除（SIC）可行性以及最小相邻天线间距约束的条件下，最大化语义频谱效率（SE）。采用交替优化（AO）方法联合优化用户功率分配和Pinching天线位置。仿真结果表明，与传统的基准方案相比，所提出的比例功率PASS模型在不同几何和数值设置下能提供更高的语义频谱效率。

</details>


### [89] [RF-Analyzer: Can Vision-Language Models Learn RF Understanding from Synthetic Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04676)
*Anis Bara,Lina Bariah,Hang Zou,Brahim Mefgouda,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）能否仅通过合成频谱图数据学习无线频谱理解能力，并泛化到真实空中射频环境。通过搭建RF-Analyzer平台和建立基准评估框架，发现VLM在物理属性提取上泛化良好，但在缺少上下文先验时语义接地不可靠，尤其在低信噪比区域泛化失败。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统机器学习方法无法从频谱图中提取多物理属性并推理信号结构，而视觉语言模型（VLM）展现出直接从数据中学习这种解释能力的可能性。本文核心动机是探究VLM能否仅通过合成数据学习这一能力，以及所学表示能否泛化到真实空中射频环境。

Method: 提出RF-Analyzer平台，将SDR（软件定义无线电）实时频谱捕获与VLM分析能力集成，用于直接评估VLM对实时空中信号的输出。建立包含三个指标（物理属性提取得分PAES、提示泄漏率PLR、幻觉计数）的基准框架，评估模型在纯合成频谱图数据上训练后的信号理解和接地能力。

Result: 实验表明，仅用合成频谱图数据训练的VLM能够泛化到真实RF环境，特别是在提取频谱占用、时序行为和SNR等物理信号属性方面表现良好。但泛化存在局限性：合成训练无法在没有上下文先验的情况下提供可靠的语义接地，且在合成分布未覆盖的条件下（尤其是低SNR区域）泛化会失效。

Conclusion: 合成数据足以学习RF信号结构的可迁移表示，但仅靠合成训练无法实现完整的语义接地，低信噪比等分布外场景仍是主要挑战。

Original Abstract: Understanding the wireless spectrum is a fundamen- tal requirement for intelligent communication systems, however, interpreting spectrograms requires extracting multiple physical attributes and reasoning about signal structure, which is a capability that is not achieved by traditional ML approaches. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) demonstrated the possibility of learning such interpretation capabilities directly from data. This paper investigates whether VLMs can learn this capability from synthetic data alone, and more importantly, whether such learned representations generalize to real over-the- air RF environments. To address this question, we introduce RF-Analyzer, an SDR-to-AI analysis platform that integrates live spectrum captures associated with the corresponding VLM- based interpretation, enabling direct evaluation of VLMs outputs on live over-the-air signals. Using this platform, we assess a model trained exclusively on synthetic spectrogram data with general-purpose baselines. To enable systematic analysis, we establish a benchmark framework comprising three metrics, Physical Attribute Extraction Score (PAES), Prompt Leakage Rate (PLR), and hallucination count, to assess signal understanding and grounding. The obtained results demonstrate that VLMs trained on synthetic spectrogram data can generalize to real RF environments, particularly for extracting physical signal attributes such as spectral occupancy, temporal behavior, and SNR. This indicates that synthetic data is sufficient for learning transferable representations of RF signal structure. However, this generalization is limited due to the fact that synthetic training does not provide reliable semantic grounding without contextual priors. In particular, generalization breaks under conditions that are not covered in the synthetic distribution, particularly low-SNR regimes

中文摘要翻译: 理解无线频谱是智能通信系统的基本要求，然而，解释频谱图需要提取多个物理属性并推理信号结构，这是传统机器学习方法无法实现的能力。视觉语言模型（VLM）的最新进展表明，直接从数据中学习这种解释能力是可能的。本文探究VLM能否仅从合成数据中学习这一能力，更重要的是，所学表示能否泛化到真实的空中射频环境。为解决这一问题，我们提出了RF-Analyzer，一个集成实时频谱捕获与相应VLM分析的SDR-to-AI分析平台，可直接评估VLM在实时空中信号上的输出。利用该平台，我们评估了仅在合成频谱图数据上训练的模型与通用基线的表现。为进行系统分析，我们建立了一个包含三个指标——物理属性提取得分（PAES）、提示泄漏率（PLR）和幻觉计数——的基准框架，用于评估信号理解与接地能力。结果表明，在合成频谱图数据上训练的VLM能够泛化到真实RF环境，特别是在提取频谱占用、时序行为和SNR等物理信号属性方面。这表明合成数据足以学习RF信号结构的可迁移表示。然而，这种泛化是有限的，因为合成训练在没有上下文先验的情况下无法提供可靠的语义接地。特别地，在合成分布未覆盖的条件下，尤其是低SNR区域，泛化会失效。

</details>


### [90] [Multiuser OTFS Channel Parameter Estimation Toward Grid-Independent Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04716)
*Hanning Wang,Rong-Rong Chen,Arman Farhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了多用户OTFS系统在延迟-多普勒域的信道参数估计问题，提出了MU-PCP设计，并扩展了W-MUSIC和基于矩阵束的方法，实现了高效、高分辨率的参数估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有OTFS系统在多用户场景下缺乏结构化的参数估计方法，难以实现分数倍延迟和多普勒参数的高分辨率估计。

Method: 采用多用户导频循环前缀（MU-PCP）设计，沿多普勒维度复用用户并保持可分离的指数结构；扩展加权MUSIC（W-MUSIC）到多用户OTFS实现轻网格依赖的估计；开发基于矩阵束（MP）的方法实现完全无网格的延迟-多普勒参数估计。

Result: 数值结果表明：W-MUSIC在低信噪比下性能更优，而MP在中高信噪比下估计精度更高且计算复杂度显著降低。

Conclusion: 两种方法各有优劣，存在鲁棒性与复杂度的权衡，为用户在不同信噪比场景下选择合适方法提供了依据。

Original Abstract: We study channel parameter estimation for multiuser orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) systems in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain. To enable structured parametric estimation, we adopt a multi-user pilot cyclic prefix (MU-PCP) design, which multiplexes users along the Doppler dimension while preserving a separable exponential structure. This structure facilitates high-resolution estimation of fractional delay and Doppler parameters in the multiuser setting. Building on this framework, we extend weighted MUSIC (W-MUSIC) to multiuser OTFS, providing a computationally efficient approach with mild grid dependency, and develop a matrix pencil (MP)-based method that achieves fully grid-independent delay-Doppler parameter estimation. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods and reveal a robustness-complexity tradeoff: W-MUSIC performs better at low SNR, while MP achieves higher estimation accuracy at moderate-to-high SNR with significantly lower computational complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了延迟-多普勒（DD）域中多用户正交时频空间（OTFS）系统的信道参数估计问题。为实现结构化参数估计，我们采用了一种多用户导频循环前缀（MU-PCP）设计，该设计沿多普勒维度复用用户，同时保持可分离的指数结构。该结构有助于在多用户场景下实现分数倍延迟和多普勒参数的高分辨率估计。在此框架基础上，我们将加权MUSIC（W-MUSIC）扩展至多用户OTFS，提供了一种计算高效且网格依赖性较温和的方法，并开发了一种基于矩阵束（MP）的方法，实现了完全无网格的延迟-多普勒参数估计。数值结果验证了所提方法的有效性，并揭示了一个鲁棒性与复杂度的权衡：W-MUSIC在低信噪比下表现更好，而MP在中高信噪比下能实现更高的估计精度，且计算复杂度显著更低。

</details>


### [91] [SEI-SHIELD: Robust Specific Emitter Identification Under Label Noise Via Self-Supervised Filtering and Iterative Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04721)
*Ruixiang Zhang,Zinan Zhou,Yezhuo Zhang,Guangyu Li,Xuanpeng Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出SEI-SHIELD框架，结合自监督对比预训练（MoCo）和迭代样本选择，解决特定发射机识别（SEI）中的标签噪声问题，在POWDER和ORACLE数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SEI方法依赖受污染的监督信号引导样本选择，导致确认偏差和次优特征空间；需要一种不依赖标签噪声的鲁棒SEI框架。

Method: 1) 使用MoCo和RF特化数据增强从复值I/Q信号中提取鲁棒、与标签无关的表征；2) 基于KNN的噪声过滤通过邻域标签一致性分析识别损坏样本；3) 迭代救援机制利用预测置信度和原型余弦相似度逐步恢复被误丢弃的正确标签难样本。

Result: 在POWDER和ORACLE数据集上，在不同噪声率下均达到SOTA准确率，显著优于现有噪声鲁棒范式（包括高级正则化技术和样本选择框架）。

Conclusion: SEI-SHIELD通过自监督对比预训练与迭代样本选择的有机结合，有效克服了标签噪声问题，为非合作环境下的鲁棒SEI提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Specific Emitter Identification (SEI) provides physical-layer device authentication for wireless communications and Internet of Things (IoT) systems. While deep learning (DL) has significantly advanced SEI performance, label noise severely degrades system reliability in non-cooperative environments. Label noise originates from channel-induced ambiguities, annotation errors, and deliberate data poisoning by intelligent jammers injecting misleading signals. While recent SEI methods attempt to mitigate label noise, they fundamentally rely on corrupted supervised signals to guide sample selection, inevitably leading to confirmation bias and suboptimal feature spaces. To address this challenge, we propose SEI-SHIELD, a robust SEI framework that integrates self-supervised contrastive pre-training with iterative sample selection. Specifically, SEI-SHIELD employs Momentum Contrast (MoCo) with RF-tailored augmentations to extract intrinsically robust, label-independent representations directly from complex-valued I/Q signals. In addition, K-nearest neighbors (KNN)-based noise filtering identifies corrupted samples through neighborhood label consistency analysis in the learned feature space. Furthermore, an iterative rescue mechanism using prediction confidence and prototype cosine similarity progressively recovers correctly labeled hard samples inadvertently discarded during filtering. Comprehensive experiments on the POWDER and ORACLE datasets demonstrate that SEI-SHIELD achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) accuracy under various noise rates, substantially outperforming existing noise-robust paradigms, including advanced regularization techniques and sample selection frameworks.

中文摘要翻译: 特定发射机识别（SEI）为无线通信和物联网（IoT）系统提供物理层设备认证。虽然深度学习（DL）显著提升了SEI的性能，但在非合作环境中，标签噪声严重降低了系统可靠性。标签噪声源于信道引起的模糊性、标注错误以及智能干扰器注入误导信号的蓄意数据投毒。尽管最近的SEI方法尝试缓解标签噪声，但它们从根本上依赖于受污染的监督信号来引导样本选择，不可避免地导致确认偏差和次优特征空间。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了SEI-SHIELD，一个将自监督对比预训练与迭代样本选择相结合的鲁棒SEI框架。具体而言，SEI-SHIELD采用动量对比（MoCo）和面向射频（RF）的数据增强，直接从复值I/Q信号中提取内在鲁棒且与标签无关的表征。此外，基于K近邻（KNN）的噪声过滤通过在学习的特征空间中进行邻域标签一致性分析来识别损坏样本。更进一步，一种使用预测置信度和原型余弦相似度的迭代救援机制，逐步恢复在过滤过程中被误丢弃的正确标记的难样本。在POWDER和ORACLE数据集上的全面实验表明，SEI-SHIELD在各种噪声率下均实现了最先进的准确率，显著优于现有的噪声鲁棒范式，包括高级正则化技术和样本选择框架。

</details>


### [92] [Two-Timescale Design for Rotatable-Antenna Systems With Imperfect CSI: Rate Analysis and Orientation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04775)
*Ziyuan Zheng,Qingqing Wu,Wen Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对不完美信道状态信息下的上行多用户MIMO系统，提出一种可旋转天线阵列的两时间尺度设计方法：慢时间尺度基于统计CSI优化天线朝向，快时间尺度基于LMMSE估计更新线性接收合并器。推导了MRC和加权ZF的闭式速率表达式，揭示了天线旋转对信号强度、估计误差自干扰和多用户干扰的影响，并开发了投影梯度算法求解非凸旋转优化问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有可旋转天线研究多假设完美CSI，实际系统中信道估计误差不可避免，且天线朝向对信道估计质量和系统速率的影响尚未明确。本文旨在探索不完美CSI下RA朝向优化与接收合并器设计的联合问题，平衡性能与控制开销。

Method: 提出两时间尺度框架：1) 大时间尺度基于统计CSI优化RA朝向；2) 小时间尺度每相干块利用LMMSE信道估计更新线性接收合并器。推导了MRC的UatF闭式速率和加权ZF的统计速率替代函数，并设计投影梯度算法在球冠乘积流形上求解非凸旋转优化问题。

Result: 分析表明：1) 最小化信道估计误差的朝向与最大化速率的朝向不同；2) MRC和加权ZF因信号聚合与误差感知用户分离机制不同，偏好不同的旋转配置；3) 数值结果验证了大时间尺度替代函数的准确性，RA优化带来显著性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过两时间尺度设计可有效平衡性能与开销，RA朝向优化在不完美CSI下仍能带来可观性能提升，且不同接收机需针对性设计旋转策略。

Original Abstract: This paper studies uplink multiuser MIMO with a rotatable antenna (RA) array under imperfect channel state information (CSI), where each base-station antenna can adjust its boresight direction within an angular region. To balance performance and control overhead, we propose a two-timescale design: RA orientations are optimized from statistical CSI on a large timescale, while linear receive combiners are updated per coherence block from linear minimum-mean-squared-error (LMMSE) channel estimates. Under this framework, we derive a closed-form use-and-then-forget (UatF)-based rate expression for maximum-ratio combining (MRC) and a closed-form statistical rate surrogate for weighted zero-forcing (wZF) under imperfect CSI, revealing how RA rotation influences useful signal strength, estimation-error-induced self-interference, and multiuser interference. The analysis shows that the orientation minimizing channel-estimation error differs from the rate-maximizing one, and that MRC and wZF prefer different rotation configurations due to their distinct mechanisms of signal aggregation and error-aware user separation. For the resulting non-convex rotation design problems, we develop a projected-gradient algorithm over a product of spherical caps with explicit derivatives of the required channel statistics and rate metrics. Numerical results verify the accuracy of the large-timescale surrogates and show substantial performance gains from RA optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究在不完美信道状态信息条件下，采用可旋转天线阵列的上行多用户MIMO系统，其中每根基站天线可在一定角度范围内调整其波束指向方向。为平衡性能与控制开销，我们提出一种两时间尺度设计方案：在大时间尺度上基于统计CSI优化RA朝向，在小时间尺度上基于线性最小均方误差信道估计在每个相干块内更新线性接收合并器。在该框架下，我们推导了最大比合并的基于"先使用后遗忘"的闭式速率表达式，以及不完美CSI下加权迫零的闭式统计速率替代函数，揭示了RA旋转如何影响有用信号强度、估计误差引起的自干扰以及多用户干扰。分析表明，最小化信道估计误差的朝向与最大化速率的朝向不同，且MRC和wZF由于信号聚合与误差感知用户分离机制的不同，偏好不同的旋转配置。针对由此产生的非凸旋转设计问题，我们开发了一种在球冠乘积流形上运行的投影梯度算法，其中包含所需信道统计量和速率度量的显式导数。数值结果验证了大时间尺度替代函数的准确性，并表明RA优化可带来显著的性能增益。

</details>


### [93] [Fast Full-Wave Simulation of Indoor RSS Maps for Pre-Measurement Validation in Device-Free Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04958)
*Federica Fieramosca,Anastasia Maiolli,Alexander H. Paulus,Stefano Savazzi,Michele D'Amico*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出利用紧凑型全波电磁仿真作为快速可靠的工具，模拟室内Wi-Fi传播用于人体感知，为简化传播模型提供验证基准，减少对昂贵测量实验的依赖。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 室内环境中全球导航系统不可靠，而无设备被动定位方法利用人体对无线电传播的扰动进行定位；但现有的测量实验成本高昂，简化传播模型缺乏有效的验证基准，因此需要一种快速、可靠的仿真工具来弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用紧凑型全波电磁仿真设置模拟室内Wi-Fi传播，生成基于接收信号强度的二维衰减图，并在受控环境中比较衰减统计和干涉图样；同时提出考虑衍射效应的改进方法以缓解仿真与实测之间的差异。

Result: 仿真能够再现主要的空间特征，但由于简化材料特性描述，仍存在一定差异；提出的衍射感知改进方法可以缓解这些差异带来的影响。

Conclusion: 该全波电磁仿真方法能够为无设备定位系统设计提供高效的预测量参考，指导实验规划，并作为简化传播模型的有效验证基准。

Original Abstract: Human localization is gaining momentum in security, healthcare, logistics, and smart spaces applications. While global navigation systems are unreliable indoor, device-free (a.k.a. passive) localization methods that exploit human-induced perturbations of radio propagation can be effectively used. This paper investigates the use of a compact full-wave electromagnetic (EM) setup as a fast and reliable tool to simulate indoor Wi-Fi propagation for human sensing. The goal is to provide a practical baseline for validating simplified propagation models, such as diffraction-based descriptions, and to reduce the need for costly measurement campaigns. Two-dimensional attenuation maps from received signal strength are generated and compared in controlled environments, focusing on attenuation statistics and interference patterns. The simulations reproduce the main spatial features, though discrepancies remain due to simplified material characterization. Diffraction-aware refinements are proposed to mitigate these effects. Overall, the approach provides an efficient pre-measurement reference to support device-free system design and to guide experimental planning.

中文摘要翻译: 人体定位在安防、医疗、物流和智能空间等应用领域日益受到关注。由于全球导航系统在室内环境中不可靠，利用人体对无线电传播扰动的无设备（即被动）定位方法可以有效发挥作用。本文研究采用紧凑型全波电磁仿真设置，作为快速可靠的室内Wi-Fi传播模拟工具，用于人体感知。目标是提供实用的基线，以验证基于衍射等简化传播模型，并减少对昂贵测量实验的需求。在受控环境中生成并比较基于接收信号强度的二维衰减图，重点关注衰减统计和干涉图样。仿真再现了主要的空间特征，但由于材料特性描述的简化，仍存在一定差异。本文提出了考虑衍射的改进方法来缓解这些影响。总体而言，该方法为支持无设备系统设计和指导实验规划提供了高效的预测量参考。

</details>

### [94] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的统计精化方法，在未知噪声分布且无干净图像或校准数据的情况下，通过利用噪声数据的统计信息提升现有去噪器的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有去噪方法通常需要对图像和噪声进行精确建模（隐式或显式），但在实际应用中常因模型不完善、噪声假设不可靠或数据质量低导致去噪结果次优。特别是在无法获取干净图像样本且缺乏噪声分布知识时，去噪结果与噪声统计特性不一致。

Method: 基于贝叶斯公式构建辅助信号，在假设噪声在给定干净信号条件下像素独立的前提下，无需精确噪声分布信息即可评估去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，并据此进行统计精化。

Result: 该方法能够增强去噪结果与噪声统计特性之间的一致性，从而提升整体去噪质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统计精化方法能在缺乏干净图像和噪声分布先验知识的实际场景中，有效利用噪声数据中的统计信息改善去噪效果。

Original Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于学习的统计精化方法，用于在未知精确噪声分布且无法获取干净图像或校准数据的情况下，改进给定去噪器的去噪结果。尽管已有许多成功处理不同类型噪声的去噪方法，但它们通常需要对图像和噪声进行（隐式或显式）精确建模，因此在实际因素（如模型不完善、噪声假设不可靠或数据质量低）的影响下，去噪结果可能次优。特别是在无法获取干净图像样本且缺乏底层噪声分布知识（这在实际场景中很常见）的情况下，去噪结果可能与噪声统计特性不一致。对这种有用统计信息的忽视导致了次优结果。本文旨在充分利用统计信息，在假设噪声在给定干净信号条件下逐像素独立的前提下，提升给定去噪结果与噪声统计特性之间的一致性。该方法基于含噪数据中辅助信号的贝叶斯公式，无需精确的噪声分布信息即可评估去噪结果的一致性。通过利用含噪数据中的统计信息，该方法增强了噪声统计一致性并改善了去噪质量。

</details>


### [95] [Endogenous Regime Switching Driven by Scalar-Irreducible Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04054)
*Sheng Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了标量可约与标量不可约动力学的二分法，论证标量不可约动力学能够通过快慢变量反馈实现内生性体制切换，为构建自主智能系统提供了新范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器学习框架中，体制切换（regime switching）通常由外部施加，缺乏内生性切换机制，这阻碍了自主智能的产生。本文旨在探索一种能自发产生体制切换的内在动力学机制。

Method: 提出标量可约（scalar-reducible）与标量不可约（scalar-irreducible）动力学的分类框架。标量可约动力学可表示为标量目标驱动的梯度流；标量不可约动力学则不能。通过一个最小动力学模型，展示快慢变量反馈如何产生持续的内生体制切换。

Result: 标量不可约动力学能够自然地通过快速动力学变量与慢速结构适应之间的反馈产生内部生成的体制切换，无需外部调度即可维持持续的内生体制转换。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种用于体制探索的新动力学范式，为构建自适应行为由内部组织而非外部规定的自主学习系统提供了潜在路径。

Original Abstract: Achieving endogenous regime switching is crucial for the emergence of autonomous intelligence, yet remains a central challenge for existing machine learning frameworks, where such transitions are typically externally imposed. In this work, we introduce a classification that distinguishes scalar-reducible dynamics, which can be expressed as gradient flows driven by a scalar objective, from scalar-irreducible dynamics that cannot be reduced to such a form. While most existing machine learning systems operate within the scalar-reducible class, we demonstrate that scalar-irreducible dynamics naturally enable internally generated regime switching through feedback between fast dynamical variables and slow structural adaptation. Using a minimal dynamical model, we illustrate how this mechanism produces sustained endogenous regime transitions without external scheduling. Our results suggest a new dynamical paradigm for regime exploration and provide a potential route toward autonomous learning systems whose adaptive behavior is organized internally rather than externally prescribed.

中文摘要翻译: 实现内生的体制切换对于自主智能的出现至关重要，但这仍然是现有机器学习框架面临的核心挑战——在这些框架中，此类切换通常由外部强加。本文提出了一种分类方法，将标量可约动力学（可表示为标量目标驱动的梯度流）与标量不可约动力学（无法约简为此类形式）区分开来。尽管大多数现有机器学习系统属于标量可约类，但我们证明，标量不可约动力学能够通过快速动力学变量与缓慢结构适应之间的反馈，自然地产生内部生成的体制切换。通过一个最小动力学模型，我们说明了该机制如何在无需外部调度的情况下产生持续的内生体制转换。我们的研究结果为体制探索提供了一种新的动力学范式，并为构建自适应行为由内部组织而非外部规定的自主学习系统提供了潜在途径。

</details>


### [96] [A Self-Attentive Meta-Optimizer with Group-Adaptive Learning Rates and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04055)
*JiangBo Zhao,ZhaoXin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaAdamW 是一种通过自注意力机制和元学习目标动态调节每参数组学习率和权重衰减的优化器，在五个任务上一致优于 AdamW，最多可减少 17.11% 的训练时间或提升 11.08% 的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AdamW 等自适应优化器对所有参数组使用统一的超参数，忽略了不同层和模块之间异质的优化动态，导致优化效率受限。

Method: 提出 MetaAdamW 优化器，包含：(1) 轻量 Transformer 编码器，基于各参数组的统计特征（梯度范数、动量范数、相关性）生成动态调制因子，调节每组的学习率和权重衰减；(2) 元学习目标，结合梯度对齐、损失下降和泛化差距三个指标；(3) 扩展了同方差不确定性加权（HUW），引入任务特定优先级直接缩放正则化项，实现领域知识引导的自动损失平衡。

Result: 在时间序列预测（ETT）、语言建模（WikiText-2）、机器翻译（Multi30k）、图像分类（CIFAR-10）和情感分析（IMDB）五个任务上，MetaAdamW 一致优于 AdamW 基线。最多可减少 17.11% 训练时间或提升 11.08% 性能，仅引入适度开销，并能缓解早停导致的收敛不足问题。消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过动态感知参数组异质性的自适应超参数调节，MetaAdamW 能够有效提升优化效率与最终性能，为现有自适应优化器提供了一种可扩展的改进方案。

Original Abstract: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules. We address this limitation by proposing MetaAdamW - a new optimizer that integrates a self-attention mechanism to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay. The modulation factors are produced by a lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) extracted from each parameter group. To train the attention module, we introduce a meta-learning objective that combines gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap. A key novel contribution is the extension of homoscedastic uncertainty weighting (HUW) with task-specific priorities that directly scale the regularization terms - enabling domain knowledge to guide automatic loss balancing. Extensive experiments on five diverse tasks-time series forecasting (ETT), language modeling (WikiText-2), machine translation (Multi30k), image classification (CIFAR-10), and sentiment analysis (IMDB) - demonstrate that MetaAdamW consistently outperforms the standard AdamW baseline in terms of validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity. Depending on the task, MetaAdamW either reduces overall training time (by up to 17.11%) or improves performance (by up to 11.08%) while introducing only moderate overhead; in some cases, it can also mitigate issues of insufficient convergence caused by premature early stopping. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component, including feature versions, grouping strategies, and the proposed priority-injected uncertainty weighting.

中文摘要翻译: 像 AdamW 这样的自适应优化器对所有参数组采用统一的超参数，忽略了跨层和模块的异质优化动态。我们通过提出 MetaAdamW 来解决这一局限性——这是一种新型优化器，它集成了自注意机制以动态调节每组的学习率和权重衰减。调制因子由一个轻量级 Transformer 编码器生成，该编码器基于从每个参数组提取的统计特征（梯度范数、动量范数、相关性）工作。为了训练注意力模块，我们引入了一个结合梯度对齐、损失下降和泛化差距的元学习目标。一项关键的新贡献是将同方差不确定性加权（HUW）扩展为具有任务特定优先级，直接缩放正则化项——使得领域知识能够指导自动损失平衡。在五个不同任务（ETT 时间序列预测、WikiText-2 语言建模、Multi30k 机器翻译、CIFAR-10 图像分类和 IMDB 情感分析）上的广泛实验表明，MetaAdamW 在验证损失、准确率或困惑度方面一致优于标准 AdamW 基线。根据任务的不同，MetaAdamW 可以在仅引入适度开销的情况下减少总体训练时间（最多 17.11%）或提升性能（最多 11.08%）；在某些情况下，它还可以缓解由过早早停导致的收敛不充分问题。消融研究验证了各组件的有效性，包括特征版本、分组策略以及所提出的注入优先级的非确定性加权。

</details>


### [97] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对NAS中LLM辅助架构编辑时存在的“功能纠缠”问题，提出SPARK方法，通过显式选择并条件化编辑的功能因子，实现更精准、可靠的架构修改，在CLRS-DFS上取得28.1倍采样效率提升和22.9%的OOD准确率改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在神经架构搜索中，LLM可利用丰富的架构和编码先验知识通过代码编辑辅助新架构设计，但局部编辑往往因“功能纠缠”现象（一个编辑意外耦合多个交互功能因子）导致非局部的行为和性能变化，使得LLM知识难以有效利用。

Method: 提出结构化渐进知识激活（SPARK），通过显式选择待修改的功能因子，并将编辑操作条件化于该因子之上，从而减少纠缠的副作用，实现更精准、可靠的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS任务上，SPARK实现了28.1倍的样本高效架构进化加速，并在分布外（OOD）准确率上取得了22.9%的相对改进。

Conclusion: SPARK通过因子条件化编辑有效缓解了功能纠缠问题，使LLM的先验知识在NAS中得到更有效的利用，显著提升了搜索效率与泛化性能。

Original Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 本文聚焦神经架构搜索（NAS）中的一个关键挑战：在昂贵的评估代价下，如何整合既有架构知识的同时探索新设计。大型语言模型（LLM）是NAS的有力助手，能将丰富的架构与编码先验知识转化为可执行的代码编辑。然而实践中，看似局部的修改常常传播为非局部的行为和性能变化，因为单一编辑可能无意中耦合多个交互的功能因子，我们将这一现象称为功能纠缠。为了在这种纠缠下有效利用LLM知识，我们提出结构化渐进知识激活（SPARK），通过显式选择待修改的功能因子并将编辑条件化于该因子，从而激活相关先验知识。这种因子条件化编辑减少了纠缠的副作用，实现了更精准、可靠的架构修改。在CLRS-DFS上，SPARK实现了28.1倍的样本高效架构进化加速，并在分布外（OOD）准确率上取得了22.9%的相对改进。

</details>


### [98] [Physiologically Grounded Driver Behavior Classification: SHAP-Driven Elite Feature Selection and Hybrid Gradient Boosting for Multimodal Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05120)
*Sahar Askari,Mohammad Mahdi Mirza Ali Mohammadi,Fatemeh Ensafdoust,Amin Golnari,Saeid Sanei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态生理信号（EEG、EMG、GSR）的可解释且可扩展的驾驶行为解码框架，采用SHAP特征选择、贝叶斯优化的XGBoost/LightGBM以及加权软投票集成方法，在测试集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，验证了多模态融合的必要性和生理可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有驾驶行为解码研究多依赖单一模态生理信号，缺乏可解释性和多模态融合的有效框架，且难以在大规模数据集上实现高精度且可解释的预测。

Method: 首先对EEG、EMG、GSR多模态信号进行预处理和时域、频域及衍生生理指标的特征提取；然后使用基于SHAP的精化特征选择保留前250个关键特征；接着通过Optuna进行贝叶斯优化对XGBoost和LightGBM模型超参数调优；最后构建加权软投票集成模型融合两个梯度提升框架的互补优势。

Result: 所提集成模型在测试集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线和传统机器学习模型；消融研究显示多模态融合相比最佳单模态（EEG）带来8%的性能提升；SHAP分析表明EEG贡献了大部分预测权重，GSR和EMG特征为高唤醒和运动密集型操作提供关键判别信号。

Conclusion: 多模态生理信号融合结合可解释性方法能够有效解码驾驶行为，SHAP分析验证了模型的生理可解释性，EEG、EMG、GSR各自贡献了互补的判别信息。

Original Abstract: An interpretable and scalable framework for decoding driving behaviors from multimodal physiological signals is proposed in this study. We utilize multimodal physiological driving behavior large-scale dataset comprising synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG), electromyography (EMG), and galvanic skin response (GSR) signals. Our approach involves rigorous preprocessing followed by a domain-specific feature extraction pipeline targeting time-domain, frequency-domain, and derived physiological indices. To address high dimensionality, we employ SHAP-based elite feature selection, retaining the top 250 features to reduce computational overhead while preserving predictive power. Hyperparameter optimization for extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) and light gradient boosting machine (LightGBM) models is conducted using Bayesian optimization via Optuna. Finally, a weighted soft-voting ensemble is constructed to leverage the complementary strengths of both gradient boosting frameworks. The results demonstrate that the proposed ensemble achieves a test accuracy of 80.91% and a macro-F1 score of 0.79, significantly outperforming single-modality baselines and traditional machine learning models. Ablation studies confirm an 8% performance gain over the best single modality (EEG), validating the necessity of multimodal fusion. SHAP analysis further validates the physiological plausibility of the model, revealing that the EEG contributes the majority of predictive weight, GSR and EMG features provide critical discriminatory signals for high-arousal and motor-intensive maneuvers.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究提出了一个可解释且可扩展的框架，用于从多模态生理信号中解码驾驶行为。我们利用了包含同步脑电图（EEG）、肌电图（EMG）和皮肤电反应（GSR）信号的多模态生理驾驶行为大规模数据集。该方法包括严格的预处理，随后是针对时域、频域和衍生生理指标的领域特定特征提取流程。为解决高维问题，我们采用基于SHAP的精化特征选择，保留前250个特征以降低计算开销同时保持预测能力。通过Optuna进行贝叶斯优化，对极限梯度提升（XGBoost）和轻量梯度提升机（LightGBM）模型进行超参数优化。最后，构建加权软投票集成模型以利用两种梯度提升框架的互补优势。结果表明，所提出的集成模型在测试集上达到80.91%的准确率和0.79的宏F1分数，显著优于单模态基线和传统机器学习模型。消融研究证实，相较于最佳单模态（EEG），性能提升了8%，验证了多模态融合的必要性。SHAP分析进一步验证了模型的生理可解释性，揭示EEG贡献了大部分预测权重，而GSR和EMG特征为高唤醒和运动密集型操作提供了关键判别信号。

</details>


### [99] [Continual Distillation of Teachers from Different Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04059)
*Nicolas Michel,Maorong Wang,Jiangpeng He,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持续蒸馏（CD）范式，让学生模型从教师模型流中顺序学习，无需保留早期教师。针对无训练数据和教师专长异质两大挑战，提出SE2D方法，通过外部未标记数据实现未知知识转移（UKT）并缓解未知知识遗忘（UKF），实验表明该方法能减少遗忘并提升跨域泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习模型规模持续增长，部分模型的存储需求已超过许多大规模数据集。现有蒸馏方法在教师模型顺序到达且无法保留早期教师访问权限的场景下，面临两大难题：教师训练数据不可用，以及不同教师具有不同的专长领域。这促使作者探索一种新的持续学习范式。

Method: 提出SE2D（Self External Data Distillation）方法，利用外部未标记数据在教师模型上保存logits，从而在异构教师之间稳定学习过程。该方法通过外部数据实现未知知识转移（UKT），并缓解顺序蒸馏中出现的未知知识遗忘（UKF）。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，SE2D能够有效减少未知知识遗忘（UKF），并提升跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续蒸馏范式可行且有效，通过外部未标记数据和logits保存机制，学生模型能够在教师数据不可用且教师专长各异的情况下，从顺序到达的教师模型中持续学习，同时平衡知识转移与遗忘。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models continue to scale, with some requiring more storage than many large-scale datasets. Thus, we introduce a new paradigm: Continual Distillation (CD), where a student learns sequentially from a stream of teacher models without retaining access to earlier teachers. CD faces two challenges: teacher training data is unavailable, and teachers have varying expertise. We show that external unlabeled data enables Unseen Knowledge Transfer (UKT), allowing the student to acquire information from domains not present in the training data, while known to the teacher. We also show that sequential distillation causes Unseen Knowledge Forgetting (UKF) when transferred knowledge is lost after training on later teachers. To better trade off between UKT and UKF, we propose Self External Data Distillation (SE2D), a method that preserves logits on external data to stabilize learning across heterogeneous teachers. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SE2D reduces UKF and improves cross-domain generalization. The code and implementation for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习模型的规模持续增长，部分模型所需的存储空间甚至超过许多大规模数据集。为此，我们引入了一种新范式：持续蒸馏（Continual Distillation, CD），其中学生模型从教师模型流中顺序学习，而无需保留对早期教师模型的访问权限。持续蒸馏面临两大挑战：教师训练数据不可用，以及不同教师具有不同的专长领域。我们证明，外部未标记数据能够实现未知知识转移（Unseen Knowledge Transfer, UKT），使学生模型能从训练数据中未出现的领域获取信息（但这些领域为教师所知）。我们还发现，顺序蒸馏会导致未知知识遗忘（Unseen Knowledge Forgetting, UKF），即先前转移的知识在后续教师模型训练后丢失。为更好地平衡UKT与UKF，我们提出了自外部数据蒸馏（Self External Data Distillation, SE2D）方法，该方法在外部数据上保存logits，以在异构教师之间稳定学习过程。多个基准实验表明，SE2D能够有效减少UKF并提升跨域泛化能力。本工作的代码和实现已公开发布于：https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation。

</details>


### [100] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种前瞻性漂移模型（lookahead drifting model），通过迭代计算多个漂移项并加权求和来优化模型，在玩具示例和CIFAR10上取得了优于基线方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有漂移模型（drifting model）仅在每次训练迭代中计算单个漂移项，缺乏对正样本方向的高阶梯度信息的利用，限制了模型性能的提升。

Method: 在每次训练迭代中，顺序计算一组漂移项，每个后续漂移项利用之前计算的漂移项、正样本和模型输出来生成，这些漂移项捕获了朝向正样本的高阶梯度信息；最后将模型输出向这些漂移项的加权求和方向优化。

Result: 在玩具示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上优于基线漂移模型。

Conclusion: 通过前瞻性多步漂移项计算，能够捕获高阶梯度信息，从而更有效地引导模型输出朝向正样本方向，提升分布映射的质量。

Original Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 最近，一种名为"漂移模型"的新范式被提出用于分布映射，该模型通过一步神经函数评估在ImageNet上实现了最先进的图像生成性能。其基本思想是在每次训练迭代中计算一个漂移项，然后将模型输出推向漂移项的方向。本文提出了一种"前瞻性漂移模型"。在每次训练迭代中，我们顺序计算一组漂移项。每个漂移项的计算都利用了先前计算的漂移项、正样本以及模型的输出。原则上，在后期阶段获得的漂移项捕获了朝向正样本的高阶梯度信息。在每次训练迭代中，通过将模型输出推向漂移项（加权）求和的方向来优化模型。在玩具示例和CIFAR10上的实验结果表明，新方法优于基线方法。

</details>


### [101] [Investigating Trustworthiness of Nonparametric Deep Survival Models for Alzheimer's Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04063)
*Jacob Thrasher,Kaitlyn Heintzelman,Peter Martone,David Kotlowski,Binod Bhattarai,Donald Adjeroh,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了阿尔茨海默病进展分析中的公平性问题，发现深度学习生存模型虽然有效，但常对特定群体表现出显著偏见，并提出了两种新的公平性度量指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习在AD生存分析中的研究较少，且已有研究未考虑模型内部的学习偏见，可能导致对边缘群体的不公平和不可靠预测。

Method: 提出了两种新的公平性度量指标：时间依赖一致性杂质（Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity）和Kaplan-Meier公平性（Kaplan-Meier Fairness），用于量化非参数生存模型中关于性别、种族、教育等敏感属性的偏见；同时进行了特征重要性分析。

Result: 深度学习驱动的生存模型虽然在AD临床决策中是鲁棒工具，但常表现出显著的偏见，尤其是在性别、种族和教育等敏感属性方面。

Conclusion: 深度学习生存模型在AD进展分析中存在重要偏见问题，这为未来研究指明了重要方向。

Original Abstract: Alzheimer's Dementia (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease marked by irreversible decline, making reliable modeling of its progression essential for effective patient care. Progression-aware methods such as survival analysis are therefore crucial tools for the early detection and monitoring of AD. Recent advancements in deep learning have demonstrated remarkable performance in survival tasks, but alarmingly fewer studies have been conducted in the domain of AD. Further, the studies that do exist do not consider learned bias within the model itself, which could result in unfair and unreliable predictions toward certain marginalized groups. As such, we conduct a rigorous study of fairness in AD progression analysis along with a thorough feature importance study to determine the characteristics which are most important for reliable AD predictions. Furthermore, we propose two novel fairness metrics, called Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity and Kaplan-Meier Fairness, to quantify bias with respect to sensitive attributes such as sex, race, and education in nonparametric survival models. Our study demonstrates that while deep learning powered survival models are robust tools which can aid clinicians in AD care decisions, they often exhibit considerable bias, representing important avenues for future research.

中文摘要翻译: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）是一种以不可逆衰退为特征的进行性神经退行性疾病，因此对其进展进行可靠建模对于有效患者护理至关重要。诸如生存分析等进展感知方法是早期检测和监测AD的关键工具。深度学习的最新进展在生存任务中展现出卓越性能，但令人担忧的是，在AD领域开展的研究却少得多。此外，已有的研究并未考虑模型内部的学习偏见，这可能导致对某些边缘群体产生不公平且不可靠的预测。为此，我们对AD进展分析中的公平性进行了严格研究，并进行了彻底的特征重要性分析，以确定对可靠AD预测最重要的特征。此外，我们提出了两种新的公平性度量指标，称为时间依赖一致性杂质（Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity）和Kaplan-Meier公平性（Kaplan-Meier Fairness），用于量化非参数生存模型中关于性别、种族和教育等敏感属性的偏见。我们的研究表明，虽然深度学习驱动的生存模型是能够辅助临床医生做出AD护理决策的鲁棒工具，但它们通常表现出显著的偏见，这代表了未来研究的重要方向。

</details>


### [102] [Improving Medical VQA through Trajectory-Aware Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04064)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于COMCTS算法生成医学VQA推理轨迹数据集，并利用两阶段训练框架（SFT+GRPO）结合基于DTW的轨迹感知奖励，显著提升了医学视觉问答的推理能力与性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学视觉问答数据集缺乏推理过程解释，限制了模型推理能力的发展。

Method: (1) 使用COMCTS算法结合开源视觉-语言模型生成六个医学VQA基准的推理轨迹，并用LLM作为验证裁判；(2) 提出两阶段训练框架：监督微调 + GRPO强化学习，引入基于动态时间规整（DTW）的轨迹感知奖励来衡量生成推理过程与真实推理过程的相似度。

Result: 在六个基准上，DTW过程奖励+精确匹配奖励的组合一致优于纯SFT训练：平均准确率从0.598提升至0.689，平均BERTScore从0.845提升至0.881，平均ROUGE-L从0.665提升至0.748。

Conclusion: 过程监督对于训练具备推理能力的医学视觉语言模型至关重要。

Original Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are crucial for reliable medical visual question answering (VQA); however, existing datasets rarely include reasoning explanations.
  We address this by generating reasoning trajectories for six medical VQA benchmarks using the COMCTS algorithm with open-source vision-language models, with an LLM serving as the verification judge.
  Building on these generated datasets, we propose a two-stage training framework: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel process-based reward.
  While standard approaches rely solely on exact-match rewards for final answers, we introduce a trajectory-aware reward that measures the similarity between generated and ground-truth reasoning processes.
  Specifically, we embed reasoning steps using sentence transformers and compute the Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) distance between the resulting vector sequences.
  Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that combining the DTW-based process reward with exact-match reward consistently outperforms SFT-only training, raising mean accuracy from 0.598 to 0.689, mean BERTScore from 0.845 to 0.881, and mean ROUGE-L from 0.665 to 0.748.
  Our results highlight the importance of process supervision in training reasoning-capable medical VLMs.
  We make our code and generated reasoning datasets publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MICCAI-R1-MED-VQA-code-B14B/

中文摘要翻译: 推理能力对于可靠的医学视觉问答（VQA）至关重要，然而现有数据集很少包含推理解释。我们通过使用COMCTS算法结合开源视觉-语言模型为六个医学VQA基准生成推理轨迹，并以大语言模型作为验证裁判来应对这一挑战。基于所生成的数据集，我们提出了一种两阶段训练框架：监督微调，随后进行分组相对策略优化（GRPO）并引入一种新颖的基于过程的奖励。标准方法仅依赖最终答案的精确匹配奖励，而我们引入了一种轨迹感知奖励，用于衡量生成推理过程与真实推理过程之间的相似度。具体而言，我们使用句子变换器对推理步骤进行嵌入，并计算所得向量序列之间的动态时间规整（DTW）距离。在六个基准上的实验表明，将基于DTW的过程奖励与精确匹配奖励相结合，一致优于仅进行SFT训练，使平均准确率从0.598提升至0.689，平均BERTScore从0.845提升至0.881，平均ROUGE-L从0.665提升至0.748。我们的结果凸显了过程监督在训练具备推理能力的医学视觉语言模型中的重要性。我们已在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MICCAI-R1-MED-VQA-code-B14B/公开了代码和生成的推理数据集。

</details>


### [103] [LAWS: Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically -- A Self-Certifying Parametrized Cache Architecture for Neural Inference, Robotics, and Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04069)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LAWS（从实际工作负载中符号学习），一种自认证的推理缓存架构，通过在部署观测中构建带形式化误差界的专家函数库，实现无需真实标签即可验证的近似推理，并证明其泛化了混合专家模型和KV前缀缓存。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有推理缓存和混合专家模型在部署时缺乏可验证的误差保证，且表达能力受限于固定数量的专家或缓存大小。需要一种既能自适应扩展、又能提供形式化误差界、且无需真实标签即可在部署时自认证的推理架构。

Method: 提出LAWS架构：1) 基于概率语言Trie（PLT）对输入空间进行分区；2) 每个节点对应一个专家函数，带形式化误差界；3) 自认证定理：误差由专家训练误差、模型Lipschitz常数和嵌入直径共同界定，三者均可在部署时无需真实标签计算；4) 任意匹配路由确保单调命中率；5) 专家库增长率O(2^H log N)，H为工作负载熵。

Result: 1) 自认证定理：误差 ≤ ε_fit + 2Λ(W)C_E；2) 严格泛化MoE和KV前缀缓存，比任意固定K的MoE或有限缓存更具表达力；3) 单调命中率定理；4) 专家库增长率O(2^H log N)；5) 集群学习收敛速度Ω(K)加速；6) 空中更新带宽有界；7) 猜想：在线缓存算法中获取最优性，有效Lipschitz常数多项式增长。

Conclusion: LAWS是一种理论上完备、可在部署时自认证的推理缓存架构，在表达能力、误差保证和扩展性上均优于现有方法，适用于LLM推理、机器人控制和多智能体边缘部署等场景。

Original Abstract: We introduce LAWS (Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically), a self-certifying inference caching architecture that builds a growing library of certified expert functions from deployment observations. Each expert covers a region of input space defined by a node in the Probabilistic Language Trie (PLT) of the base model and carries a formal error bound holding uniformly over all inputs. The central result is a self-certification theorem: for any input x, the LAWS approximation error is bounded by epsilon_fit + 2*Lambda(W)*C_E, where Lambda(W) is the model Lipschitz constant, C_E is the maximum embedding diameter, and epsilon_fit is the expert training error -- all checkable at deployment time without ground truth. We prove that LAWS generalizes both Mixture-of-Experts and KV prefix caching as special cases and is strictly more expressive than any fixed-K MoE or finite cache. Further results include a monotone hit rate theorem (any-match routing ensures coverage only increases), an expert library growth rate of O(2^H log N) where H is workload entropy, a fleet learning convergence theorem with Omega(K) speedup for K-unit fleets, and an over-the-air update bandwidth bound. We conjecture that LAWS is acquisition-optimal among stationary online caching algorithms and that the effective Lipschitz constant on the training distribution grows polynomially rather than exponentially in depth. Applications are developed for LLM inference, robotic control, and multi-agent edge deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出LAWS（从实际工作负载中符号学习），一种自认证的推理缓存架构，通过从部署观测中构建不断增长的经过认证的专家函数库。每个专家覆盖由基础模型概率语言Trie（PLT）中节点定义的输入空间区域，并携带在所有输入上一致成立的形式化误差界。核心结果是自认证定理：对于任意输入x，LAWS近似误差由ε_fit + 2Λ(W)C_E界定，其中Λ(W)是模型Lipschitz常数，C_E是最大嵌入直径，ε_fit是专家训练误差——三者均可在部署时无需真实标签检验。我们证明LAWS作为特例泛化了混合专家模型和KV前缀缓存，并且严格比任意固定K的MoE或有限缓存更具表达力。进一步的结果包括单调命中率定理（任意匹配路由确保覆盖率只增不减）、专家库增长率O(2^H log N)（H为工作负载熵）、集群学习收敛定理（K单元集群具有Ω(K)加速）、以及空中更新带宽有界。我们猜想LAWS在静态在线缓存算法中是获取最优的，且训练分布上的有效Lipschitz常数随深度呈多项式增长而非指数增长。为LLM推理、机器人控制和多智能体边缘部署开发了应用。

</details>


### [104] [FlatASCEND: Autoregressive Clinical Sequence Generation with Continuous Time Prediction and Association-Based Pharmacological Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04071)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatASCEND是一个1450万参数的自回归临床序列模型，使用扁平复合标记和零膨胀对数正态时间头。该模型的核心价值在于从患者特定前缀进行条件生成，但在标准分布指标上无法与简单基线区分。研究发现患者特异性条件增强机制性药理效应（2.0-2.2x），而直接偏好优化会破坏所有正确的关联（3/3降至0/3），表明奖励利用问题。模型在ICU短期数据上表现最强，门诊时间保真度较弱，零样本跨站点迁移需适配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归模型可以预测临床事件，但生成患者条件化的多步轨迹并响应干预令牌，以及测试这些响应是否保留已知药理学关联的问题尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出FlatASCEND，一个14.5M参数的自回归临床序列模型，使用扁平复合标记（flat composite tokens）和零膨胀对数正态时间头（zero-inflated log-normal time head）。采用提示洗牌消融实验和事件用户框架（incident-user framework）评估条件生成效果，并与先验药理学知识进行方向一致性比较。

Result: 标准分布指标（Jaccard 0.889-0.954）无法区分FlatASCEND与简单基线；提示洗牌显示患者条件增强机制性药理效应（类固醇→葡萄糖、利尿剂→钾为2.0-2.2x），但不影响混杂驱动的关联（胰岛素→葡萄糖为0.9x）；事件用户框架中4/10恢复正确机制方向，2复制治疗-情境关联，4错误；直接偏好优化破坏所有正确关联（3/3降至0/3）；ICU短期数据最强，门诊时间保真度较弱（中位数10天vs 154天），零样本跨站点转移需适配。

Conclusion: FlatASCEND在条件生成方面具有价值，但学习的关联是观测性而非因果性的，部分恢复正确方向但受残留混杂影响。直接偏好优化存在奖励利用问题，生成证据在短期ICU数据中最强，跨域泛化仍需改进。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive models can predict clinical events, but generating patient-conditioned multi-step trajectories that respond to intervention tokens and testing whether those responses preserve known pharmacological associations has received limited attention. We present FlatASCEND, a 14.5M-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model using flat composite tokens and a zero-inflated log-normal time head. Standard distributional metrics (Jaccard 0.889-0.954) do not distinguish FlatASCEND from trivial baselines; the model's value lies in conditional generation from patient-specific prefixes. A prompt-shuffle ablation shows patient-specific conditioning amplifies mechanistic pharmacological effects (2.0-2.2x for steroid to glucose, diuretic to potassium) while leaving confounding-driven associations unchanged (0.9x for insulin to glucose). An incident-user framework assesses directional consistency against prior pharmacological knowledge on MIMIC-IV (N=500 per comparison): 4/10 recover correct mechanistic directions, 2 reproduce treatment-context associations, 4 are incorrect (9/10 significant, Wilcoxon p<0.05). This pattern - partial recovery under residual confounding - is consistent with learned observational associations without causal distinction. Direct preference optimisation with surrogate reward destroys all correct associations (3/3 to 0/3), illustrating reward exploitation when reward and evaluation share an outcome domain. Generative evidence is strongest for short-horizon ICU data; outpatient temporal fidelity is weaker (median 10 vs 154 days on INSPECT), and zero-shot cross-site transfer degrades without adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归模型可以预测临床事件，但生成患者条件化的多步轨迹以响应干预令牌，以及测试这些响应是否保留已知药理学关联的问题尚未得到充分关注。我们提出了FlatASCEND，一个1450万参数的自回归临床序列模型，使用扁平复合标记和零膨胀对数正态时间头。标准分布指标（Jaccard 0.889-0.954）无法区分FlatASCEND与简单基线；该模型的价值在于从患者特定前缀进行条件生成。提示洗牌消融实验表明，患者特异性条件增强了机制性药理效应（类固醇→血糖、利尿剂→钾为2.0-2.2倍），而混杂驱动的关联保持不变（胰岛素→血糖为0.9倍）。事件用户框架评估了与MIMIC-IV上先验药理学知识的方向一致性（每比较N=500）：4/10恢复了正确的机制方向，2个复制了治疗-情境关联，4个错误（9/10显著，Wilcoxon p<0.05）。这种模式——在残留混杂下部分恢复——与学习到的观测性关联（无因果区别）一致。使用替代奖励的直接偏好优化破坏了所有正确的关联（3/3降至0/3），说明了当奖励和评估共享结果领域时的奖励利用问题。生成证据在短期ICU数据中最强；门诊时间保真度较弱（中位数10天对比INSPECT上的154天），且零样本跨站点迁移在未适配时性能下降。

</details>


### [105] [Sparse Autoencoder Decomposition of Clinical Sequence Model Representations: Feature Complexity, Task Specialisation, and Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04072)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统性地将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于EHR基础模型（FlatASCEND），揭示了SAE特征随Transformer深度从单token检测到多类别抽象的渐进特性，并发现SAE特征在离散事件预测上优于稠密表示，但在连续量预测和泄露安全窗口设置下不如稠密表示，且特征可重复性仅21%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAE已成功应用于大语言模型和蛋白质语言模型，但尚未被系统地应用于电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究SAE在临床序列模型中的特征表示特性。

Method: 在FlatASCEND（1450万参数的自回归临床序列模型）的所有10个残差流提取点上训练TopK SAE，数据来自INSPECT（门诊）和MIMIC-IV（ICU）数据集。使用全序列线性探针和泄露安全窗口进行性能评估，并提出了delta模式干预方法以降低SAE扰动噪声。

Result: (1) SAE特征随深度渐进抽象：第0层近完美token检测（45.7%单例），第6层跨多个临床类别约30个token类型（0.5%单例）；(2) 全序列线性探针下，SAE在离散事件预测（死亡率）优于稠密表示，稠密表示在连续量预测（住院时长）占优；(3) 泄露安全窗口下稠密表示全面匹配或超越SAE；(4) delta模式将SAE扰动噪声降低86倍；(5) 特征跨随机种子可重复性仅21%。

Conclusion: SAE在EHR基础模型中的表示具有层次渐进抽象特性，但存在特征可重复性低、泄露安全窗口下性能不及稠密表示等局限。delta模式干预方法能有效降低噪声，但扰动效应未达统计显著。单个特征应被视为说明性而非稳定的表示单元。

Original Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have been applied to large language models and protein language models, but not systematically to electronic health record (EHR) foundation models. We train TopK SAEs on FlatASCEND, a 14.5-million-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model, at all 10 residual stream extraction points on INSPECT (outpatient) and MIMIC-IV (ICU). SAE decomposition reveals progressive abstraction across transformer depth: layer-0 features are near-perfect token detectors (45.7% singleton), while layer-6 features span approximately 30 token types across multiple clinical categories (0.5% singleton). Under full-sequence simple linear probes, SAE features outperform dense representations for discrete event prediction (mortality) while dense representations outperform for continuous magnitude prediction (length of stay) - a probe-level representational phenomenon that does not extend to clinically relevant leakage-safe windows, where dense representations match or exceed SAE features across all tested settings (eICU-CRD 48-hour AUC: SAE 0.871 versus dense 0.880; base model zero-shot, SAE dictionaries trained on eICU activations; MIMIC-IV: 0.836 versus 0.914; INSPECT 1-year/3-year: 0.697 versus 0.800). A delta-mode intervention method reduces SAE perturbation noise by 86x, enabling cleaner feature-level experiments, though the resulting perturbation effects are larger than random controls in 3 of 4 conditions but not formally significant. Feature reproducibility across random seeds is 21%, and individual features should be interpreted as illustrative rather than stable.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已被应用于大语言模型和蛋白质语言模型，但尚未被系统地应用于电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型。我们在FlatASCEND（一个1450万参数的自回归临床序列模型）的所有10个残差流提取点上训练TopK SAE，数据来自INSPECT（门诊）和MIMIC-IV（ICU）。SAE分解揭示了跨Transformer深度的渐进抽象：第0层特征近乎完美的token检测器（45.7%单例），而第6层特征跨越多个临床类别的约30种token类型（0.5%单例）。在全序列简单线性探针下，SAE特征在离散事件预测（死亡率）上优于稠密表示，而稠密表示在连续量预测（住院时长）上更优——这是一种探针层次的表示现象，在临床相关的泄露安全窗口中并不成立。在泄露安全窗口下，稠密表示在所有测试设置中均达到或超过SAE特征（eICU-CRD 48小时AUC：SAE 0.871 vs 稠密0.880；基础模型零样本，SAE词典在eICU激活上训练；MIMIC-IV：0.836 vs 0.914；INSPECT 1年/3年：0.697 vs 0.800）。一种delta模式干预方法将SAE扰动噪声降低86倍，从而实现更干净的特征级实验，尽管由此产生的扰动效应在4个条件中的3个大于随机对照，但未达到统计显著。跨随机种子的特征可重复性为21%，单个特征应被视为说明性而非稳定的。

</details>


### [106] [Confronting Label Indeterminacy in Automated Bail Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04073)
*Cor Steging,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了保释决策数据中因反事实结果不可观测导致的结构性标签不确定性问题，并评估了五种处理该问题的方法对机器学习模型预测行为的影响，发现方法选择比模型选择影响更大。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在保释决策中，被拒绝保释的被告是否会出现法庭是永远无法观测的反事实结果，导致历史数据存在结构性标签不确定性。基于此类数据构建自动化系统可能引入偏见并强化反馈循环，因此需要探究如何在模型开发中妥善处理被拒绝保释的案件。

Method: 以宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统的保释决策为案例，使用三种机器学习模型评估五种处理标签不确定性的方法，包括一种受保释决策动力学启发的新型标签插补方法。

Result: 每种方法均依赖不可验证的假设，但所有方法都显著影响模型的预测行为，其影响程度有时甚至超过模型本身的选择。可解释AI分析表明这些影响还延伸至模型的内部决策过程。

Conclusion: 从法律视角审视标签不确定性，评估了这些方法在保释决策语境中的合法性，指出处理标签不确定性的方法选择是数据驱动的保释决策支持系统中的关键问题。

Original Abstract: Bail decisions present a fundamental challenge for data-driven decision support systems. When bail is denied, the counterfactual outcome of whether the defendant would have appeared in court remains unobserved. As a result, historical bail data embed structural label indeterminacy: future decisions are influenced by past decisions whose outcomes are only partially knowable. Building automated systems on such data risks introducing bias and reinforcing feedback loops. This raises a core question for machine-learning systems intended to assist judicial actors: how should cases in which bail was denied be treated during model development? In a case study of bail decisions from the Unified Judicial System of Pennsylvania, we evaluate five contemporary approaches to handling label indeterminacy across three machine learning models, including a novel label imputation method motivated by the dynamics of bail decisions. Each method relies on unverifiable assumptions, yet all influence the models' predictive behaviour, sometimes even more so than the choice of model itself. Explainable AI analysis further reveals that these effects extend to the models' internal decision-making processes as well. Finally, we consider the notion of label indeterminacy from a legal perspective and assess the legitimacy of these approaches in the context of bail decision-making.

中文摘要翻译: 保释决策对数据驱动的决策支持系统提出了根本性挑战。当保释被拒绝时，被告是否会出现法庭的反事实结果始终无法被观测。因此，历史保释数据内嵌了结构性标签不确定性：未来的决策受到过去决策的影响，而过去决策的结果只有部分可被知晓。在此类数据上构建自动化系统存在引入偏见并强化反馈循环的风险。这为旨在辅助司法人员的机器学习系统提出了一个核心问题：在模型开发过程中，应如何处理被拒绝保释的案件？在以宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统保释决策为案例的研究中，我们评估了五种处理标签不确定性的当代方法，涵盖三种机器学习模型，其中包括一种受保释决策动力学启发的新型标签插补方法。每种方法均依赖不可验证的假设，但所有方法都会影响模型的预测行为，其影响程度有时甚至超过模型本身的选择。可解释AI分析进一步揭示，这些影响还延伸至模型的内部决策过程。最后，我们从法律视角审视标签不确定性的概念，并评估这些方法在保释决策语境中的合法性。

</details>


### [107] [A Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection in U.S. Banking: Integrating OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN Compliance Requirements for Model Development, Validation, and Monitoring Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04076)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RGF-AFFD治理框架，将美国四项金融监管要求整合为三级架构，通过LSTM+XGBoost集成模型在欺诈检测中实现0.9289的ROC-AUC和6:1的收益成本比，并引入RDT-FG数字孪生元模型实现持续合规监控。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 美国金融机构在部署AI欺诈检测时面临OCC 2011-12、SR 11-7、CFPB AI通知和FinCEN BSA/SAR四项监管框架的碎片化合规要求，缺乏连接这些要求与模型开发、验证及监控实践的集成治理生命周期。

Method: 提出三级治理架构RGF-AFFD，基于多研究实证项目，使用IEEE-CIS数据集（590,540笔交易）和ULB基准数据集（284,807笔交易），基准测试六种架构（包括LSTM+XGBoost集成），并进行消融实验、时间漂移分析、SHAP可解释性分析和BISG公平性分析。同时提出RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型，将指标转化为四个监管机构特定的健康分数和综合监管适应性指数。

Result: LSTM+XGBoost集成模型ROC-AUC达0.9289（F1分数0.6360），收益成本比6:1。XGBoost时间稳定性最强（delta-AUC = -0.0017，LSTM为-0.0626）。

Conclusion: RGF-AFFD是首个同时满足OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN要求的集成部署蓝图，辅以社区银行实施案例和四项基于证据的政策建议。

Original Abstract: U.S. financial institutions deploying AI-based fraud detection face a fragmented compliance landscape spanning four regulatory frameworks -- OCC Bulletin 2011-12, SR 11-7, the CFPB AI circular, and FinCEN BSA/SAR requirements -- with no integrated governance life cycle connecting these requirements to model development, validation, and monitoring practice. This paper presents the Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection (RGF-AFFD), a three-tier governance architecture empirically anchored in a multi-study empirical program. Using the IEEE-CIS dataset (590,540 transactions) and ULB benchmark (284,807 transactions), we benchmark six architectures including an LSTM+XGBoost ensemble, and conduct ablation, temporal drift, SHAP interpretability, and BISG fairness analyses. The LSTM+XGBoost ensemble achieves ROC-AUC of 0.9289 (F1: 0.6360) with a benefit-cost ratio of 6:1. XGBoost demonstrates the strongest temporal stability (delta-AUC = -0.0017 versus -0.0626 for LSTM). The RDT-FG Regulatory Digital Twin meta-model translates metrics into four regulator-specific health scores and a composite Regulatory Fitness Index for continuous compliance monitoring. The RGF-AFFD is the first integrated deployment blueprint to simultaneously satisfy OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN requirements, supported by a community bank implementation vignette and four evidence-based policy recommendations.

中文摘要翻译: 部署AI欺诈检测的美国金融机构面临碎片化的合规全景，涵盖四项监管框架——OCC公告2011-12、SR 11-7、CFPB AI通知以及FinCEN BSA/SAR要求，且缺乏将上述要求与模型开发、验证和监控实践相连接的集成治理生命周期。本文提出面向AI驱动金融欺诈检测的监管治理框架（RGF-AFFD），这是一个基于多研究实证项目的三级治理架构。利用IEEE-CIS数据集（590,540笔交易）和ULB基准数据集（284,807笔交易），我们对六种架构进行基准测试，包括LSTM+XGBoost集成模型，并开展消融分析、时间漂移分析、SHAP可解释性分析和BISG公平性分析。LSTM+XGBoost集成模型达到0.9289的ROC-AUC（F1分数：0.6360），收益成本比为6:1。XGBoost展现出最强的时间稳定性（delta-AUC = -0.0017，而LSTM为-0.0626）。RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型将指标转化为四个监管机构特定的健康分数以及一个综合监管适应性指数，用于持续合规监控。RGF-AFFD是首个同时满足OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN要求的集成部署蓝图，并辅以社区银行实施案例和四项基于证据的政策建议。

</details>


### [108] [Balanced Aggregation: Understanding and Fixing Aggregation Bias in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04077)
*Zhiyuan Zeng,Jiameng Huang,Zhangyue Yin,Jiashuo Liu,Ziniu Li,Bingrui Li,Yuhao Wu,Yining Zheng,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了GRPO式强化学习中token级和序列级两种聚合规则的不同优化偏差，提出了一种平衡聚合方法（Balanced Aggregation, BA），通过分别在正负子集内计算token级均值并以序列计数加权组合，在推理和代码生成任务上显著提升了训练稳定性和最终性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GRPO式RLVR训练中，token级和序列级两种策略梯度聚合规则各有利弊，但两者的优化偏差尚未被充分理解和对比，缺乏一种统一的、性能更优的聚合方法。

Method: 提出Balanced Aggregation (BA)，一种即插即用的替代方案：在每组样本中，分别在正负子集内计算token级均值，然后基于序列计数（sequence-count-based weights）对两者进行加权组合，从而平衡两种聚合规则的偏差。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Qwen3-1.7B模型上，使用DAPO-17k和Polaris数据集，在6个推理和代码生成基准上的实验表明，BA一致地提升了训练稳定性和最终性能，优于标准的token聚合和序列聚合。分析还表明，两种聚合规则的相对有效性主要由响应长度变化和正负长度差距决定。

Conclusion: 聚合规则是GRPO式RLVR中一个关键的设计维度，Balanced Aggregation能够有效调和token聚合和序列聚合之间的偏差，是一种更优的聚合方法。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a central paradigm for improving reasoning and code generation in large language models, and GRPO-style training is widely adopted for its simplicity and effectiveness. However, an important design choice remains underexplored: how token-level policy gradient terms are aggregated within each sampled group. Standard GRPO uses sequence aggregation, while recent work has advocated token aggregation as a better alternative. We show that these two rules induce different optimization biases: token aggregation introduces sign-length coupling, while sequence aggregation implicitly downweights longer responses through sequence-level equal weighting. To address this tension, we propose \textbf{Balanced Aggregation (BA)}, a simple drop-in replacement that computes token-level means separately within the positive and negative subsets and then combines them with sequence-count-based weights. Experiments with Qwen2.5-Math-7B and Qwen3-1.7B on DAPO-17k and Polaris, evaluated on six reasoning and coding benchmarks, show that BA consistently improves training stability and final performance over standard token and sequence aggregation. Our analysis further shows that the relative effectiveness of token and sequence aggregation is largely governed by response-length variation and the positive-negative length gap, highlighting aggregation as a critical design dimension in GRPO-style RLVR.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成为提升大型语言模型推理和代码生成能力的核心范式，其中GRPO式训练因其简洁性和有效性而被广泛采用。然而，一个重要的设计选择尚未被充分探索：在每个采样组内，token级策略梯度项是如何聚合的。标准GRPO使用序列聚合，而近期研究则主张token聚合是一种更好的替代方案。我们发现这两种规则会诱发不同的优化偏差：token聚合会引入符号-长度耦合，而序列聚合通过序列级等权加权隐式地降低了较长响应的权重。为解决这一矛盾，我们提出了平衡聚合（Balanced Aggregation, BA），一种简单的即插即用替代方案，它在正负子集内分别计算token级均值，然后以序列计数加权的方式将两者组合。使用Qwen2.5-Math-7B和Qwen3-1.7B在DAPO-17k和Polaris上进行的实验表明，BA在六个推理和代码生成基准上一致地提升了训练稳定性和最终性能，优于标准的token聚合和序列聚合。我们的进一步分析表明，token聚合和序列聚合的相对有效性在很大程度上由响应长度变化和正负长度差距所决定，这突显了聚合作为GRPO式RLVR中一个关键设计维度的重要性。

</details>


### [109] [Validity-Calibrated Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04078)
*Khouloud Saadi,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部有效性校准的推理蒸馏框架，将推理蒸馏从轨迹模仿转变为局部学习信号分配，通过动态上下文相关的监督机制提升小模型的推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏方法依赖静态的师生层次结构，将蒸馏视为轨迹模仿，但推理过程中中间步骤存在局部欠指定问题（全局正确性无法唯一确定每一步中间动作），导致路径对齐式的蒸馏与推理结构不匹配。

Method: 提出有效性校准推理蒸馏（Validity-Calibrated Reasoning Distillation），在相同前缀下比较学生模型与教师模型提出的下一步动作，利用两者相对局部有效性来调节蒸馏更新的强度，实现动态、上下文相关的监督机制。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随等多个基准测试上，该方法一致性地优于强蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 有效的LLM推理蒸馏不应依赖刚性轨迹模仿，而应基于原则性的、局部校准的学习信号分配机制。

Original Abstract: Reasoning distillation aims to transfer multi-step reasoning capabilities from large language models to smaller, more efficient ones. While recent methods have shown promising gains, they typically rely on static teacher-student hierarchies and frame distillation as trajectory imitation. This is misaligned with the structure of reasoning, where intermediate steps are often locally under-specified: global correctness constrains the final answer, but does not uniquely determine each intermediate move. We propose validity-calibrated reasoning distillation, a framework that treats reasoning distillation as a problem of local learning-signal allocation rather than path alignment. Instead of enforcing token-level imitation, we compare the student's and teacher's proposed next-step actions under the same prefix and use their relative local validity to modulate the strength of the distillation update. This yields a dynamic, context-dependent supervision mechanism that preserves the teacher's structural guidance while adapting update strength to local reasoning quality. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction-following benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms strong distillation baselines. These results indicate that effective LLM reasoning distillation is governed not by rigid trajectory imitation, but by principled, locally calibrated allocation of learning signal.

中文摘要翻译: 推理蒸馏旨在将大语言模型的多步推理能力迁移到更小、更高效的模型中。尽管近期方法已取得显著进展，但它们通常依赖静态的师生层级结构，并将蒸馏框架化为轨迹模仿。这与推理的结构特性不一致：在推理过程中，中间步骤往往是局部欠指定的——全局正确性约束了最终答案，但并不能唯一确定每一步的中间动作。我们提出有效性校准推理蒸馏（Validity-Calibrated Reasoning Distillation），该框架将推理蒸馏视为一个局部学习信号分配问题，而非路径对齐问题。我们不再强制进行词元级别的模仿，而是在相同前缀下比较学生和教师提出的下一步动作，并利用它们的相对局部有效性来调节蒸馏更新的强度。这产生了一种动态、上下文相关的监督机制，在保留教师结构指导的同时，根据局部推理质量自适应调整更新强度。在数学推理、代码生成和指令跟随等多个基准测试上，我们的方法一致性地优于强蒸馏基线方法。这些结果表明，有效的LLM推理蒸馏并非由刚性的轨迹模仿所主导，而是由原则性的、局部校准的学习信号分配所决定。

</details>


### [110] [Enhancing the interpretability of spatially variable N2O model predictions with soft sensors during wastewater treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04082)
*Mohammad Raeisi Gahrouei,Pedram Ramin,Vincenzo A. Riggio,Carlos Domingo-Felez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究分析了机器学习模型在预测污水处理厂N2O排放扰动和空间变异性方面的表现，发现模型精度高但特征重要性受场景和测量尺度影响，且机理模型揭示了自养与异养途径间的相互作用可能高估N2O路径贡献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于模型的N2O排放解决方案依赖于操作数据集和专用测量活动，但模型在不同条件下的泛化能力和可解释性尚不明确，需要系统评估机器学习模型在预测N2O排放扰动中的表现及其局限性。

Method: 使用四种机器学习模型（R²=0.79-0.89）分析真实数据集；通过全厂机理模型模拟监测活动，引入额外传感器、场地级N2O数据集和废水扰动（n=16），并分析模型特征重要性和可解释性。

Result: ML模型预测精度高（0.97±0.02, n=80），但特征重要性取决于模型类型、场景和N2O测量尺度（反应器级vs.全厂级）；机理模型结构分析显示自养与异养途径在氧化氮上的相互作用会高估好氧亚硝酸盐产量并偏置N2O路径贡献。

Conclusion: N2O软传感器模型的预测受限于测量位置和数据集的方法论不确定性，这影响模型的可解释性；机理模型中的途径交互可能导致对N2O贡献的偏差估计。

Original Abstract: Model-based solutions for nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from wastewater treatment plants (WWTP) are informed by operational datasets designed to control nutrient levels in liquid waste, coupled with dedicated campaigns for N2O measurements. We analysed how machine learning (ML) models predict disturbances to WWT operation and spatially variable N2O emissions. A real dataset was investigated to validate the modelling framework from N2O emissions predicted by four ML models (R2 = 0.79 - 0.89). Monitoring campaigns for N2O were simulated with a plant-wide mechanistic model to include additional sensors, site-level N2O datasets, and wastewater disturbances (n = 16). ML models were highly accurate (0.97 +- 0.02, n = 80), but the feature importance depended on the model, the scenario and the N2O measurement scale (reactor vs. WWTP). We argue that N2O soft sensor model predictions are limited to the measuring location and the methodological uncertainty of the dataset, which affect the interpretability of the model. Lastly, the analysis of the mechanistic model structure exposed interactions between autotrophic and heterotrophic pathways over nitric oxide which can overestimate aerobic nitrite production and bias the N2O pathway contributions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于模型的污水处理厂一氧化二氮（N2O）排放解决方案，依托于旨在控制液态废物营养水平的操作数据集，并结合专门的N2O测量活动。我们分析了机器学习（ML）模型如何预测污水处理操作的扰动以及空间变化的N2O排放。通过一个真实数据集验证了四种ML模型预测N2O排放的建模框架（R²=0.79-0.89）。利用全厂机理模型模拟N2O监测活动，以纳入额外传感器、场地级N2O数据集和废水扰动（n=16）。ML模型具有高精度（0.97±0.02, n=80），但特征重要性取决于模型类型、场景以及N2O测量尺度（反应器级vs.全厂级）。我们认为N2O软传感器模型的预测受限于测量位置和数据集的方法论不确定性，这影响了模型的可解释性。最后，对机理模型结构的分析揭示了自养与异养途径在氧化氮上的相互作用，这种作用可能高估好氧亚硝酸盐产量并偏置N2O路径贡献。

</details>


### [111] [AsymmetryZero: A Framework for Operationalizing Human Expert Preferences as Semantic Evals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04083)
*Tadhg Looram,Lucas Nuzzi,Kyle Waters,Steven Dillmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymmetryZero是一个将人类专家偏好操作化为语义评估的框架，通过定义稳定的评估合约（明确评分标准、判断方式和聚合规则），在模型评估和智能体评估场景中实现可比分数和共享审计线索。实验表明，紧凑型陪审团与前沿模型陪审团在标准层面的一致性较高（75.9%-89.6%），同时大幅降低了推理成本和延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前强化学习后训练中的评估设计面临两大挑战：一是许多真实任务受主观性、程序性和领域特异性要求约束，难以编码为精确匹配目标或开放式偏好判断；二是需要将人类专家要求忠实地编码到评估本身中。现有评估缺乏显式、可执行的评分标准定义机制。

Method: 提出AsymmetryZero框架，核心是"稳定评估合约"（stable evaluation contract），明确三要素：①评分内容（what is being graded）；②各标准的判断方式（how each criterion is judged）；③标准级决策的聚合方式（如何得出任务结果）。该合约可分别在Inspect（纯模型评估）和Harbor Framework（智能体评估）中执行。实验设计：固定任务合约，对比五模型前沿陪审团与五模型紧凑陪审团在四个前沿求解器上的表现。

Result: 标准级前沿vs紧凑一致性为75.9%-89.6%（严格共同子集一致性77.8%-92.1%）；紧凑陪审团内部异议率（3-2分裂率28.7%-32.4%）显著高于前沿陪审团（6.1%-11.5%）；紧凑陪审团将每标准评判成本降至前沿的约4.2%-5.6%，延迟降至约21.7%-27.1%，同时聚合后的任务级结果保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 后训练中的核心挑战是忠实地将专家要求编码到评估中。AsymmetryZero通过显式评估合约提供了一种可行的解决方案。紧凑型陪审团在标准级保持较高一致性的同时，大幅降低了成本和延迟，可作为前沿模型陪审团的经济高效替代方案。

Original Abstract: Much of the focus in RL today is on evaluation design: building meaningful evals that serve simultaneously as benchmarks and as well-defined reward signals for post-training. Yet, many real-world tasks are governed by subjective, procedural, and domain-specific requirements that are difficult to encode as exact-match targets or open-ended preference judgments frequently used in RL pipelines today. In this work, we present AsymmetryZero, a framework for operationalizing human expert preferences as semantic evals. AsymmetryZero represents each task as a stable evaluation contract that makes grading criteria explicit: what is being graded, how each criterion is judged, and how criterion-level decisions are aggregated into a task outcome. The same contract can be executed using Inspect for model-only evaluations, as well as the Harbor Framework for agentic evaluations, enabling comparable scores and shared audit artifacts across both settings. We argue that the central challenge in post-training today is the faithful encoding of expert requirements into the evaluation itself. To that end, we present a study using Harbor that holds task contracts fixed and compares a five-model frontier jury against a five-model compact jury across four frontier-class solvers (Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4, Grok-4.20, Gemini-3.1-Pro). We find that criterion-level frontier-vs-compact agreement ranges from $75.9\%$ to $89.6\%$ (strict common-subset agreement: $77.8\%$ to $92.1\%$), while compact juries exhibit substantially higher internal dissent (3--2 split rate $28.7\%$--$32.4\%$) than frontier juries ($6.1\%$--$11.5\%$). Verifier traces further show that compact juries reduce per-criterion judging cost to roughly $4.2\%$--$5.6\%$ of frontier and latency to roughly $21.7\%$--$27.1\%$, even as aggregated task-level outcomes often remain comparatively stable.

中文摘要翻译: 当前强化学习的大部分焦点在于评估设计：构建有意义的评估，使其同时作为基准和定义明确的奖励信号用于后训练。然而，许多现实任务受到主观性、程序性和领域特异性要求的约束，这些要求难以编码为当前RL流程中常用的精确匹配目标或开放式偏好判断。在这项工作中，我们提出了AsymmetryZero，一个将人类专家偏好操作化为语义评估的框架。AsymmetryZero将每个任务表示为一个稳定的评估合约，使评分标准显式化：评分内容、每个标准的判断方式，以及标准级决策如何聚合成任务结果。同一合约可以使用Inspect进行纯模型评估，也可以使用Harbor Framework进行智能体评估，从而在两个场景下实现可比分数和共享审计线索。我们认为，当前后训练的核心挑战是将专家要求忠实地编码到评估本身中。为此，我们使用Harbor进行了一项研究，固定任务合约，将五模型前沿陪审团与五模型紧凑陪审团在四个前沿级求解器（Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4、Grok-4.20、Gemini-3.1-Pro）上进行对比。我们发现，标准级前沿与紧凑的一致性范围为75.9%至89.6%（严格共同子集一致性为77.8%至92.1%），而紧凑陪审团的内部异议率（3-2分裂率为28.7%-32.4%）显著高于前沿陪审团（6.1%-11.5%）。验证器追踪进一步表明，紧凑陪审团将每标准评判成本降至前沿的约4.2%-5.6%，延迟降至约21.7%-27.1%，即使聚合后的任务级结果通常保持相对稳定。

</details>


### [112] [FASQ: Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization for Calibration-Free LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04084)
*Ye Qiao,Yian Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Hyoukjun Kwon,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASQ提出了一种免校准的乘积量化框架，通过调节子向量大小和码本基数实现连续压缩率（27-49%原模型大小），填补了固定位宽量化的压缩空白；定制CUDA内核使推理速度超越FP16，在消费级GPU上实现高效部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统标量量化仅支持固定的离散位宽（如8/4/3-bit），压缩率选择有限，且通常需要校准数据，难以灵活适应不同部署场景下的精度-大小权衡需求。

Method: 提出FASQ框架，对LLM权重矩阵应用乘积量化（Product Quantization），通过调节子向量大小和码本基数两个参数实现连续可调的压缩率；同时设计定制CUDA内核：用于解码阶段的免LUT直接计算GEMV，和用于预填充阶段的输出驻留双缓冲LUT GEMM，均采用split-K并行策略。

Result: 在Meta-Llama-3-8B上，FASQ在37-42%模型大小下精度（67.1-67.7 avg.）超越4-bit GPTQ和AWQ；在RTX 3090上，有效4-bit解码速度45.2 tok/s（2.56x内存压缩），有效3-bit达51.8 tok/s（2.80x压缩），均超越FP16张量核心性能（43.9 tok/s），吞吐量为AWQ的1.6-1.8x、GPTQ的2.5x、RTN的4.3-5x。

Conclusion: FASQ是唯一能在解码阶段超越FP16推理速度的压缩方法，实现了免校准压缩、连续的大小-精度权衡，并在单张消费级GPU上支持实时推理。

Original Abstract: Compressing large language models (LLMs) for deployment on commodity GPUs remains challenging: conventional scalar quantization is limited to fixed bit-widths (e.g., 8/4/3-bit), offers only a few discrete compression points, and typically requires calibration data. We present FASQ (Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization), a calibration-free framework that applies product quantization to LLM weight matrices. By tuning two parameters, sub-vector size and codebook cardinality, FASQ exposes a continuous design space spanning 27-49% of the original FP16 model size, filling compression gaps that fixed-bit schemes cannot reach. On Meta-Llama-3-8B, FASQ surpasses 4-bit GPTQ and AWQ in accuracy (67.1-67.7 avg.) at 37-42% model size, with consistent results on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B-Base. To make product quantization practical at inference time, we design custom CUDA kernels: a LUT-free direct-compute GEMV for decode and an output-stationary double-buffered LUT GEMM for prefill, both with split-K parallelism.
