Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出谱图条件可交换性（SGCE）概念及基于图小波变换的谱共形预测方法（SCALE），用于对图结构多元时间序列进行可靠的区间预测，在保持覆盖率的同时提升了覆盖率-效率权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图结构多元时间序列的点预测缺乏严格的量化不确定性方法；共形预测依赖可交换性假设，但图结构时间序列中节点间的耦合会破坏该假设，导致直接应用共形预测不可靠。

Method: 提出谱图条件可交换性（SGCE），利用谱图理论将高频近似可交换成分与低频全局趋势成分分离；基于此设计SCALE方法，使用图小波变换分解高低频，对高频残差施加自适应门控的共形预测。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，SCALE不仅达到有效的覆盖率，且持续优于现有共形预测方法在覆盖率-效率权衡上的表现。

Conclusion: SCALE通过频域分解克服了图结构时间序列中节点耦合对共形预测可交换性假设的破坏，实现了既有效又高效的谱域不确定性量化。

Original Abstract: Point forecasting for graph-structured multivariate time series is a fundamental problem, but rigorous uncertainty quantification for such predictions is still underexplored. Conformal prediction (CP) offers uncertainty estimation with a solid coverage guarantee under the exchangeability assumption, which requires the joint data distribution to be unchanged under permutation. However, in graph-structured time series, inherent cross-node coupling can violate the exchangeability condition, making direct application of CP unreliable. Inspired by the spectral graph theory, such coupling resides in global trends and can be characterized by the low-frequency components, while high-frequency components are nearly exchangeable. Therefore, we propose a novel concept named Spectral Graph Conditional Exchangeability (SGCE), which conditions exchangeable high-frequency components on low-frequency ones to preserve global trends and enable effective CP in the spectral domain. Based on SGCE, we further propose Spectral Conformal prediction via wAveLEt transform (SCALE). SCALE uses graph wavelets to decompose low/high-frequency components and conformalizes high-frequency residuals via adaptive gating over a low-frequency embedding. Experimental results on real-world traffic datasets show that SCALE not only achieves valid coverage but also consistently improves the coverage-efficiency trade-off over the state-of-the-art CP methods.

中文摘要翻译: 针对图结构多元时间序列的点预测是一个基本问题，但对此类预测进行严格的量化不确定性仍然研究不足。共形预测（CP）在可交换性假设下提供了具有可靠覆盖保证的不确定性估计，该假设要求联合数据分布在置换下保持不变。然而，在图结构时间序列中，固有的跨节点耦合可能违反可交换性条件，使得CP的直接应用不可靠。受谱图理论启发，这种耦合存在于全局趋势中，可由低频成分刻画，而高频成分几乎可交换。因此，我们提出一个名为谱图条件可交换性（SGCE）的新概念，它将可交换的高频成分以低频成分为条件，以保留全局趋势，从而在谱域中实现有效的CP。基于SGCE，我们进一步提出基于小波变换的谱共形预测（SCALE）。SCALE使用图小波分解低频/高频成分，并通过低频嵌入上的自适应门控对高频残差进行共形化。在真实交通数据集上的实验结果表明，SCALE不仅达到了有效的覆盖率，而且持续改善了与最先进CP方法相比的覆盖率-效率权衡。

</details>


### [176] [EP-GRPO: Entropy-Progress Aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization with Implicit Process Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04960)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Wenwen Zhao,Zhisheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EP-GRPO框架，通过挖掘模型内在信息流（熵门控调制、隐式过程信号、累积熵映射）解决GRPO在Token粒度、极性对齐和梯度消失方面的信用分配失败问题，在数学推理任务上取得更优的准确率和效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GRPO存在三种信用分配失败：统一Token粒度忽视异质性信息价值、统一极性惩罚正确步骤奖励错误步骤、零方差崩溃导致梯度消失。量化分析揭示了严重的不均匀Token信息量、广泛的步骤级极性错配和大量训练浪费。

Method: 提出EP-GRPO框架，集成三种机制：1) 熵门控调制，优先关注高熵决策关键点；2) 隐式过程信号，利用锚定结果优势的策略散度提供定向Token级反馈，无需外部奖励模型；3) 累积熵映射，实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下保持梯度流动。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，EP-GRPO在准确率和效率方面均优于GRPO及其变体。

Conclusion: EP-GRPO通过挖掘模型内在信息流提供密集的自监督信号，有效解决了GRPO的信用分配缺陷，为强化学习优化提供了更精细的Token级学习信号。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has advanced LLM reasoning. However, GRPO suffers from three credit assignment failures: uniform token-level granularity that ignores heterogeneous informational value, uniform polarity that penalizes correct steps and rewards incorrect ones, and zero-variance collapse that erases outcome-driven gradients. We systematically quantify these failures, revealing highly non-uniform token informativeness, widespread step-level polarity misalignment, and substantial training waste.
  To address these limitations, we propose Entropy-Progress Aligned GRPO (EP-GRPO), a framework that mines the model's intrinsic information flow for dense, self-supervised guidance. EP-GRPO integrates entropy-gated modulation to prioritize high entropy decision pivots, implicit process signals from policy divergence anchored to outcome advantages for directional token-level feedback without external reward models, and cumulative entropy mapping that enables progress-aligned advantage normalization, naturally maintaining gradient flow under zero reward variance.
  Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that EP-GRPO achieves superior accuracy and efficiency compared to GRPO and its variants. The code will be available.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），尤其是组相对策略优化（GRPO），推动了大型语言模型推理能力的发展。然而，GRPO存在三种信用分配失败：统一的Token粒度忽略了异质性的信息价值，统一的极性惩罚了正确步骤并奖励了错误步骤，以及零方差崩溃导致基于结果的梯度消失。我们系统地量化了这些失败，揭示了高度非均匀的Token信息量、广泛的步骤级极性错配以及大量的训练浪费。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了熵-进度对齐的GRPO（EP-GRPO），该框架挖掘模型内在信息流以提供密集的自监督引导。EP-GRPO集成了熵门控调制以优先关注高熵决策关键点，利用锚定结果优势的策略散度提供隐式过程信号以实现定向Token级反馈而无需外部奖励模型，以及累积熵映射以实现进度对齐的优势归一化，在零奖励方差下自然维持梯度流动。在数学推理基准上的大量实验表明，EP-GRPO在准确率和效率方面均优于GRPO及其变体。代码将公开提供。

</details>


### [177] [Reliable Modeling of Distribution Shifts via Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04965)
*Philip Naumann,Jacob Kauffmann,Klaus-Robert Müller,Grégoire Montavon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为位移重塑最优传输（ReshapeOT）的方法，通过利用样本位移信息重塑地面度量，以更准确地建模分布转移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 最优传输（OT）中地面度量的设计至关重要，但传统欧几里得度量可能无法反映真实的分布变化几何结构，需要引入额外的位移信息来改进度量。

Method: 将欧几里得度量替换为从位移二阶矩估计的马氏距离，从而在输入空间中构建类似“高速公路”的路径，使传输方案更好地与观测到的位移对齐。该方法计算轻量，可无缝集成到任何基于成本矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化扩展。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性方面取得了显著提升，并在两个实际用例中验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: ReshapeOT通过简洁有效地整合位移信息重塑地面度量，显著提升了OT的传输可靠性，且具有计算高效、易于集成等优点。

Original Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) is a central framework for modeling distribution shifts. Because OT compares distributions directly in input space, a well-designed ground metric between observations is essential to ensure that the optimizer does not violate the true geometry of change. We propose Displacement-Reshaped Optimal Transport (ReshapeOT), a method that reshapes the ground metric by integrating observed sample displacements as an additional source of knowledge. Technically, ReshapeOT replaces the Euclidean metric with a Mahalanobis distance estimated from displacement second moments. This effectively carves expressways through the input space, inviting transport solutions that better align with observed displacements. Our method is computationally lightweight, integrates seamlessly into any OT solver that operates on a cost matrix, and can be kernelized for further flexibility. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that ReshapeOT achieves substantial gains in transport reliability. We further demonstrate our method's usefulness in two practical use cases.

中文摘要翻译: 最优传输（OT）是建模分布转移的核心框架。由于OT直接在输入空间中比较分布，一个精心设计的观测间地面度量对于确保优化器不违背真实的几何变化至关重要。我们提出了位移重塑最优传输（ReshapeOT），该方法通过整合观测到的样本位移作为额外的知识来源来重塑地面度量。在技术上，ReshapeOT将从位移二阶矩估计的马氏距离替换欧几里得度量。这有效地在输入空间中开辟了“高速公路”，引导传输方案更好地与观测到的位移对齐。我们的方法计算轻量，可无缝集成到任何基于成本矩阵的OT求解器中，并支持核化以提供进一步灵活性。在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，ReshapeOT在传输可靠性方面取得了显著提升。我们进一步在两个实际用例中展示了该方法的有用性。

</details>


### [178] [Skill Neologisms: Towards Skill-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04970)
*Antonin Berthon,Nicolas Astorga,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"技能新词"（skill neologisms）——在模型词汇表中集成可学习的软token，无需权重更新即可选择性扩展模型能力，支持技能的组合与零样本迁移，为基于技能的持续学习提供可扩展路径。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在扩展模型新技能时面临困境：微调及参数高效变体易导致灾难性遗忘，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受有效上下文长度约束。需要一种既能选择性扩展技能、又避免权重更新的可扩展方法。

Method: 提出"技能新词"概念，即在预训练LLM的词汇表中集成可训练的软token，每个token针对特定技能进行优化，通过梯度下降学习这些token参数，而模型其余权重保持不变。

Result: 1) 现成的预训练LLM中已存在与程序性知识相关的token；2) 技能新词可被学习并有效提升模型在特定技能上的能力；3) 学习的技能新词可与分布外技能进行组合；4) 独立训练的不同技能新词可实现零样本组合。

Conclusion: 技能新词为基于技能的持续学习提供了一条可扩展的路径，无需修改模型权重即可灵活扩展新技能并支持技能组合。

Original Abstract: Modern LLMs show mastery over an ever-growing range of skills, as well as the ability to compose them flexibly. However, extending model capabilities to new skills in a scalable manner is an open-problem: fine-tuning and parameter-efficient variants risk catastrophic forgetting, while context-based approaches have limited expressiveness and are constrained by the model's effective context. We explore skill neologisms--i.e., soft tokens integrated in the model's vocabulary and optimized to improve capabilities over a specific skill--as a way to selectively extend model capabilities to new skills without weight updates. We first observe that off-the-shelf pre-trained LLMs already demonstrate tokens associated with procedural knowledge. We then show that skill neologisms can be learned to improve model capabilities on specific skills while being composable with out-of-distribution skills, and that independently trained skill neologisms can be composed zero-shot. These results suggest that skill neologisms may provide a scalable path towards skill-based continual learning.

中文摘要翻译: 现代大语言模型在日益广泛的技能上表现出精通能力，并能灵活地对这些技能进行组合。然而，以可扩展的方式将模型能力扩展到新技能仍是一个开放性问题：微调及其参数高效变体会导致灾难性遗忘，而基于上下文的方法表达能力有限且受模型有效上下文长度的约束。我们探索了"技能新词"（skill neologisms）——即集成在模型词汇表中并经过优化以提升特定技能能力的软token——作为一种无需权重更新即可选择性扩展模型能力至新技能的方法。我们首先观察到，现成的预训练LLM中已经存在与程序性知识相关的token。接着我们证明，技能新词可以被学习以提升模型在特定技能上的能力，同时能够与分布外技能进行组合，并且独立训练的技能新词可以实现零样本组合。这些结果表明，技能新词可能为基于技能的持续学习提供一条可扩展路径。

</details>


### [179] [Conceptors for Semantic Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04980)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Young-Min Cho,Ren Tao,Miranda Muqing Miao,Sunny Rai,Lyle Ungar,Sharath Chandra Guntuku,Neville Ryant,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conceptor（软投影矩阵）作为LLM行为控制的几何先验方法，通过保留概念的多维子空间替代单向量方法，并在可组合性、层选择诊断和输出安全性上超越基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于激活的LLM行为控制方法将每个概念简化为单一方向向量，忽略了概念的几何结构（多维子空间），缺乏对概念几何性质的深入分析。

Method: 使用Conceptor（软投影矩阵），从双极概念两端的激活中估计得到，保留概念完整的多维子空间；并利用Conceptor quota作为无参数的层选择诊断指标；同时支持闭式布尔代数运算（AND, OR, NOT）实现概念组合。

Result: 1. 几何分析表明双极子空间严格包含单向量基线；2. Conceptor quota预测概念可分离性，Pearson相关系数最高达r=0.96；3. 在系统性五轴设计空间评估中，Conceptor在概念子空间多维的层上匹配或超越加性基线，且产生更少的退化输出。

Conclusion: Conceptor steering是一种几何上更合理、可组合且实际更安全的替代方案，优于从有限对比对中学习的单方向steering方法。

Original Abstract: Activation-based steering provides control of LLM behavior at inference time, but the dominant paradigm reduces each concept to a single direction whose geometry is left largely unexamined. Rather than selecting a single steering direction, we use conceptors: soft projection matrices estimated from activations pooled across both poles of a bipolar concept, which preserve the concept's full multidimensional subspace. A geometric analysis shows the bipolar subspace strictly subsumes the single-vector baseline. We further show that the conceptor quota provides a parameter-free layer-selection diagnostic, predicting concept separability with Pearson correlations up to r=0.96 across three instruction-tuned models and three semantic dimensions. Beyond selection, conceptors admit a closed-form Boolean algebra (AND, OR, NOT): we evaluate conceptor compositionality on thematically related sub-concepts. Across a systematic five-axis design-space evaluation, conceptors match or outperform additive baselines at layers where concept subspaces are multi-dimensional while producing substantially fewer degenerate outputs. Conceptor steering is a geometrically principled, compositional, and practically safer alternative to single-direction steering from a limited number of contrastive pairs.

中文摘要翻译: 基于激活的引导（Activation-based steering）允许在推理时控制LLM的行为，但当前主流范式将每个概念简化为单一方向向量，且其几何结构基本未被深入分析。本文不选择单一引导方向，而是使用Conceptor——一种从双极概念（bipolar concept）两端汇聚的激活中估计得到的软投影矩阵——来保留概念的完整多维子空间。几何分析表明，双极子空间严格包含单向量基线。我们进一步展示，Conceptor quota提供了一种无参数的层选择诊断工具，能够在三个指令微调模型和三个语义维度上，以最高r=0.96的Pearson相关系数预测概念的可分离性。除层选择外，Conceptor还支持闭式布尔代数运算（AND、OR、NOT）：我们在主题相关的子概念上评估了Conceptor的组合性。在系统的五轴设计空间评估中，在概念子空间呈多维性的层上，Conceptor匹配或超越了加性基线，同时产生的退化输出大幅减少。Conceptor引导是一种几何上合理、可组合且实际更安全的替代方案，优于从有限对比对中学习的单一方向引导方法。

</details>


### [180] [Self-Induced Outcome Potential: Turn-Level Credit Assignment for Agents without Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04984)
*Senkang Hu,Yong Dai,Xudong Han,Zhengru Fang,Yuzhi Zhao,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SIOP，一种无需过程级奖励或任务专用验证器的自监督逐轮信用分配方法，通过对最终答案进行语义聚类作为潜在未来状态，实现基于势能的逐轮奖励塑形，在7个搜索增强Agent推理基准上优于无验证器的结果级基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长程LLM智能体依赖中间信息收集步骤，但训练反馈通常只在最终答案处获得，因为过程级奖励需要高质量人工标注。现有方法要么需要答案监督或稳定的任务专用验证器，要么只能在答案或轨迹层面提取自信号，无法将信用分配到中间轮次。

Method: SIOP对每个查询采样多个轨迹，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，构建可靠性感知的目标分布，然后使用可处理的簇级近似奖励那些增加对可靠未来状态后验支持的中间轮次。该方法将信息势塑形从金答案监督推广到无金验证器场景，同时避免了标准GRPO的广播式轨迹级优势。

Result: 在7个搜索增强的Agent推理基准上，SIOP在无需金答案验证器的情况下，平均性能优于无验证器的结果级基线，并接近有金答案监督的结果级基线。

Conclusion: SIOP框架形式化了从金答案监督到无验证器设置的信息势塑形泛化，证明了其有监督金答案极限情况，并验证了其在多种Agent推理任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents depend on intermediate information-gathering turns, yet training feedback is usually observed only at the final answer, because process-level rewards require high-quality human annotation. Existing turn-level shaping methods reward turns that increase the likelihood of a gold answer, but they require answer supervision or stable task-specific verifiers. Conversely, label-free RL methods extract self-signals from output distributions, but mainly at the answer or trajectory level and therefore cannot assign credit to intermediate turns. We propose Self-Induced Outcome Potential (SIOP), which treats semantic clusters of final answers as latent future outcome states for potential-based turn-level credit assignment. For each query, SIOP samples multiple rollouts, clusters final answers into semantic outcome modes, and builds a reliability-aware target distribution over these states. It then rewards turns for increasing posterior support for reliable future states using a tractable cluster-level approximation. The objective generalizes information-potential shaping from gold-answer supervision to settings without task-specific gold verifiers while avoiding the broadcasted rollout-level advantages used by standard GRPO. We formalize the framework, characterize its supervised gold-answer limit, and show that SIOP improves average performance over verifier-free outcome-level baselines on seven search-augmented agentic reasoning benchmarks while approaching a gold-supervised outcome baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git.

中文摘要翻译: 长程LLM智能体依赖中间信息收集步骤，但训练反馈通常仅在最终答案处观测到，因为过程级奖励需要高质量的人工标注。现有的逐轮塑形方法奖励那些增加金答案可能性的轮次，但它们需要答案监督或稳定的任务专用验证器。相反，无标签强化学习方法从输出分布中提取自信号，但主要在答案或轨迹层面，因此无法将信用分配给中间轮次。我们提出自我诱导结果势（SIOP），将最终答案的语义聚类视为潜在的未来结果状态，用于基于势能的逐轮信用分配。对于每个查询，SIOP采样多个轨迹，将最终答案聚类为语义结果模式，并构建一个对这些状态的可靠性感知目标分布。然后，它使用可处理的簇级近似，奖励那些增加对可靠未来状态后验支持的轮次。该目标将信息势塑形从金答案监督推广到没有任务专用金验证器的设置，同时避免了标准GRPO使用的广播式轨迹级优势。我们形式化了该框架，刻画了其有监督金答案极限，并表明在七个搜索增强的智能体推理基准上，SIOP在平均性能上优于无验证器的结果级基线，同时接近有金答案监督的结果级基线。代码可在 https://github.com/dl-m9/SIOP.git 获取。

</details>


### [181] [Learned Neighbor Trust for Collaborative Deployment in Model-Agnostic Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05009)
*Michael Lanier,Luise Ge,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNTrust方法，让分布式去中心化蒸馏中的每个节点学习一个紧凑的信任函数，在训练时门控辅助蒸馏、推理时构建部署集成，从而将训练时的协作直接迁移到部署阶段，显著提升异构IoT场景下的推理精度并降低通信开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有去中心化蒸馏方法仅在训练时协调，推理时各节点独立部署，忽略了异构IoT场景中邻居节点能力远强于自身的情况，导致训练时的协作无法迁移到部署阶段。

Method: 提出Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust)方法：在无服务器、模型无关的协议下，节点间仅交换查询和软预测；每个节点基于本地验证证据学习一个紧凑的信任函数，该函数在训练时门控辅助蒸馏，在推理时定义部署集成，实现训练协作到部署的直接迁移。

Result: 在多个数据集和拓扑结构上，LNTrust相比最强的仅输出基线方法，在部署准确率上有大幅提升，同时通信开销显著低于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过学习邻居信任函数，分布式节点可以更有效地在训练和推理阶段协同工作，尤其适合资源异构、数据稀缺且分布不均的IoT场景。

Original Abstract: Many decentralized distillation methods are designed around training-time coordination, yet deploy each node in isolation even when more capable neighbors remain available at inference time. This is an incomplete objective for settings such as IoT, where devices are heterogeneous, data is scarce and skewed, and a node's strongest neighbors may far exceed its own local capacity. We study how nodes should train so that their predictions compose well at deployment, and how each node should learn whom to trust. Under a server-free, model-agnostic protocol where nodes exchange only queries and soft predictions, we propose Learned Neighbor Trust (LNTrust) wherein each node learns a compact trust function over its neighborhood from local validation evidence. This trust function gates auxiliary distillation during training and defines a deployment ensemble at inference, so that collaboration learned during training transfers directly to deployment. Across datasets and topologies, LNTrust improves deployed accuracy over the strongest output-only baseline by large margins while using significantly less communication than previous methods.

中文摘要翻译: 许多去中心化蒸馏方法围绕训练时的协调进行设计，但即使在推理时仍有能力更强的邻居可用，每个节点在部署时仍被孤立地使用。这对于物联网等设备异构、数据稀缺且分布不均、节点的最强邻居可能远超自身本地能力的场景来说，是一个不完全的目标。我们研究节点应如何训练才能使其预测在部署时良好组合，以及每个节点应如何学习信任谁。在一种无服务器、模型无关的协议下（节点仅交换查询和软预测），我们提出学习邻居信任（LNTrust），其中每个节点从本地验证证据中学习一个关于其邻居的紧凑信任函数。该信任函数在训练时门控辅助蒸馏，并在推理时定义部署集成，从而使得训练阶段学到的协作直接迁移到部署阶段。跨多个数据集和拓扑结构，LNTrust在部署准确率上以较大幅度优于最强的仅输出基线方法，同时通信开销显著低于先前方法。

</details>


### [182] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-SND，通过图结构加权平均替代完整图平均来加速多智能体强化学习中的系统神经多样性（SND）度量，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(|E|)，并在保持语义不变的前提下实现精确恢复、无偏估计和快速收敛，实验验证了10倍以上的加速效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始SND指标需要对所有智能体对计算距离并取平均，每次调用复杂度为O(n²)，当智能体数量n较大时成为计算瓶颈，限制了该指标在大规模多智能体系统中的实际应用。

Method: 用任意图G的边上的加权平均替代完全图K_n上的全局平均。三种机制：(1) G=K_n时精确恢复原始SND；(2) 固定稀疏图定义局部多样性度量，复杂度O(|E|)；(3) 随机边采样提供Horvitz-Thompson无偏估计量，采样m条边时达到O(1/√m)浓度界。理论证明了扩张图的转发指数失真界和低秩距离结构下的谱改进，以及随机d-正则图的无条件概率界。

Result: 在VMAS上验证了恢复性、无偏性、浓度性和时间缩放；n=100的PPO实验中Bernoulli-0.1 Graph-SND跟踪全SND，每次度量调用时间减少约10倍；冻结策略GPU计时n=500时符合预期的(n²)/|E|加速比；随机d-正则扩张图在Θ(n log n)边数下达到SNDᵤ/SND ∈ [0.9987,1.0013]；n=50的DiCo多样性控制中，Bernoulli-0.1 Graph-SND保持设定点跟踪，每次调用成本降低约9.5倍。

Conclusion: SND的聚合瓶颈可以被消除而不改变度量语义，Graph-SND作为稀疏替代方案能够超越完全图SND的扩展限制，同时支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

Original Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

中文摘要翻译: 系统神经多样性（SND）通过平均所有$\binom{n}{2}$个智能体对的成对距离来衡量多智能体强化学习中的行为异质性，每次调用复杂度与团队规模的平方成正比。本文引入Graph-SND，用任意图$G$的边上的加权平均替代完全图平均。由此产生三种机制：$G=K_n$精确恢复SND；固定稀疏图$G$定义了局部多样性度量，计算代价为$O(|E|)$；随机边采样得到Horvitz-Thompson无偏估计量，在采样边数$m$上达到$O(1/\sqrt{m})$浓度的归一化样本均值。对于固定稀疏图，我们证明了扩张图的转发指数失真界和低秩距离结构下的谱改进；对于随机$d$-正则图，我们证明了无条件的概率界$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$。在VMAS上我们验证了恢复性、无偏性、浓度性和挂钟时间缩放，并用PettingZoo TVD面板检查了非高斯迁移。在500次迭代的$n=100$ PPO运行中，Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND跟踪完整SND，同时每次调用度量时间减少约$10\times$；冻结策略的GPU计时在$n=500$时遵循预期的$\binom{n}{2}/|E|$加速比。随机$d$-正则扩张图在$Θ(n \log n)$条边数下实验达到$\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$。在$n=50$的DiCo多样性控制中，Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND在将每次调用度量成本降低${\sim}9.5\times$的同时，保持了设定点跟踪，且九个匹配单元上的配对奖励差异与零无显著差异。这些结果共同表明，SND的聚合瓶颈可以在不改变度量语义的情况下被消除，从而得到一种随插即用的稀疏替代方案，它能够超越完全图SND的规模限制，并同时支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

</details>


### [183] [CuBridge: An LLM-Based Framework for Understanding and Reconstructing High-Performance Attention Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05023)
*Xing Ma,Yangjie Zhou,Wu Sun,Zihan Liu,Jingwen Leng,Yun Lin,Shixuan Sun,Minyi Guo,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CuBridge是一个基于LLM的框架，通过“提升-转移-降低”工作流，将专家编写的CUDA注意力核函数适配到不同注意力变体和GPU平台，在保证正确性的前提下显著优于现有框架、编译器方法和LLM方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力机制CUDA实现面临性能与灵活性难以兼得的困境：专家编写的核函数效率高但难以适配，框架和编译器灵活但性能不佳，而基于LLM生成GPU核的方法在处理注意力等复杂算子时正确性不稳定且性能差距显著。

Method: CuBridge采用结构化“提升-转移-降低”工作流：首先将专家编写的CUDA注意力核函数提升（lift）为可执行的中间表示（IR），该IR显式表达执行编排并抽象底层CUDA语法；然后根据用户提供的PyTorch规格生成并验证目标IR程序；最后通过参考引导的降低（lowering）重构优化后的CUDA代码。

Result: 在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上，CuBridge一致生成正确的核函数，并显著优于通用框架、基于编译器的方法以及先前的LLM方法。

Conclusion: CuBridge通过LLM驱动的结构化工作流，成功弥合了专家编写核函数的高效率与现有自动化方法灵活性之间的鸿沟，为注意力机制的跨平台高效适配提供了一条可行路径。

Original Abstract: Efficient CUDA implementations of attention mechanisms are critical to modern deep learning systems, yet supporting diverse and evolving attention variants remains challenging. Existing frameworks and compilers trade performance for flexibility, while expert-written kernels achieve high efficiency but are difficult to adapt. Recent work explores large language models (LLMs) for GPU kernel generation, but prior studies report unstable correctness and significant performance gaps for complex operators such as attention.
  We present CuBridge, an LLM-based framework that adapts expert-written attention kernels through a structured lift-transfer-lower workflow. CuBridge starts from expert-written CUDA attention kernels and lifts them into an executable intermediate representation that makes execution orchestration explicit while abstracting low-level CUDA syntax. Given a user-provided PyTorch specification, CuBridge generates and verifies a target IR program, then reconstructs optimized CUDA code via reference-guided lowering. Across diverse attention variants and GPU platforms, CuBridge consistently produces correct kernels and substantially outperforms general frameworks, compiler-based approaches, and prior LLM-based methods.

中文摘要翻译: 高效的CUDA注意力机制实现对现代深度学习系统至关重要，但支持多样且不断演化的注意力变体仍然具有挑战性。现有框架和编译器为灵活性牺牲了性能，而专家编写的核函数虽然效率高却难以适配。最近的工作探索了利用大语言模型（LLM）生成GPU核函数，但先前的研究报告指出，对于注意力等复杂算子，其正确性不稳定且性能差距显著。我们提出CuBridge，一个基于LLM的框架，通过结构化的“提升-转移-降低”工作流来适配专家编写的注意力核函数。CuBridge从专家编写的CUDA注意力核函数出发，将其提升为可执行的中间表示（IR），该表示显式表达执行编排并抽象底层CUDA语法。给定用户提供的PyTorch规格，CuBridge生成并验证目标IR程序，然后通过参考引导的降低过程重构优化的CUDA代码。在多种注意力变体和GPU平台上，CuBridge一致地生成正确的核函数，并显著优于通用框架、基于编译器的方法以及先前的LLM方法。

</details>


### [184] [Preference-Based Self-Distillation: Beyond KL Matching via Reward Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05040)
*Xin Yu,Liuchen Liao,Yiwen Zhang,Yingchen Yu,Lingzhou Xue,Qinzhen Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于偏好的自蒸馏方法(PBSD)，通过奖励正则化视角重审在线自蒸馏，利用偏好学习优化教师与学生样本之间的差距，在数学推理和工具使用基准上取得一致最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏方法将学习简化为对上下文增强教师的KL匹配，存在训练不稳定、推理性能随时间下降的问题，且缺乏真正外部教师提供的探索多样性。

Method: 提出PBSD框架，从奖励正则化视角推导出奖励重新加权的教师分布作为解析最优目标，通过偏好学习优化教师与学生样本之间的偏好差距，同时保持在线学生采样。

Result: 在数学推理和工具使用基准上，PBSD在不同模型规模下一致取得最强平均性能，相比先前自蒸馏基线训练更稳定，同时保持token效率。

Conclusion: PBSD通过偏好学习而非直接KL匹配，有效解决了在线自蒸馏的训练不稳定问题，并在理论上证明了在线自蒸馏何时优于外部教师学习。

Original Abstract: On-policy distillation is an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning, offering dense token-level training signals. However, its reliance on a stronger external teacher has driven recent work on on-policy self-distillation, where the same model serves as both teacher and student under different prompt contexts. Yet, existing self-distillation methods largely reduce learning to KL matching toward the context-augmented teacher model. This approach often suffers from training instability and can degrade reasoning performance over time. Moreover, self-distillation from the same model with prompt augmentation lacks the exploratory diversity provided by a genuine external teacher. To address these limitations, we move beyond fixed-teacher KL matching and propose \textbf{P}reference-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{D}istillation (\textbf{PBSD}), which revisits on-policy self-distillation through a reward-regularized perspective. Instead of directly matching the teacher distribution, we derive a reward-regularized objective whose analytic optimum is a reward-reweighted teacher distribution, yielding a target policy provably superior to the original teacher under this objective. Practically, PBSD optimizes preference gaps between teacher and student samples while maintaining on-policy student sampling. We support this framework with a statistical analysis of the induced preference-learning problem, formally establishing when on policy self-distillation is preferable to learning from an external teacher in our setting. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and tool-use benchmarks across multiple model scales demonstrate that PBSD consistently achieves the strongest average performance among comparable baselines, showing improved training stability over prior self-distillation baselines while preserving token efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 在线蒸馏是强化学习的一种高效替代方案，能够提供密集的token级训练信号。然而，它对外部更强教师的依赖推动了近期关于在线自蒸馏的研究，其中同一模型在不同提示上下文中同时充当教师和学生。然而，现有的自蒸馏方法在很大程度上将学习简化为对上下文增强教师模型的KL匹配。这种方法常常遭受训练不稳定，并且会随时间推移降低推理性能。此外，来自同一模型（通过提示增强）的自蒸馏缺乏真正外部教师所提供的探索多样性。为了解决这些局限性，我们超越了固定教师的KL匹配，提出了基于偏好的自蒸馏（PBSD），通过奖励正则化的视角重新审视在线自蒸馏。我们不直接匹配教师分布，而是推导出一个奖励正则化目标，其解析最优解是一个奖励重新加权的教师分布，从而得到一个在该目标下可证明优于原始教师的目标策略。在实践中，PBSD优化教师和学生样本之间的偏好差距，同时保持在线学生采样。我们通过一个对诱导偏好学习问题的统计分析来支持该框架，正式确定了在我们的设定中在线自蒸馏何时优于从外部教师学习。在多个模型规模下的数学推理和工具使用基准实验表明，PBSD在可比较的基线中一致取得最强的平均性能，相比先前的自蒸馏基线显示出改进的训练稳定性，同时保持了token效率。

</details>


### [185] [Provable imitation learning for control of instability in partially-observed Vlasov--Poisson equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05081)
*Xiaofan Xia,Qin Li,Wenlong Mou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了等离子体动力学（Vlasov-Poisson系统）的稳定控制问题，提出一种模仿学习方法，将依赖全相空间状态的专家策略蒸馏为仅使用宏观诊断观测的控制器，并证明了该学习策略的稳定性保证与误差界，验证了从宏观观测学习稳定反馈策略的理论可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 核聚变中Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学的稳定控制面临核心矛盾：理想控制器需要全相空间状态信息，但实际实验只能获取稀疏的宏观诊断测量。本文旨在弥合这一"理想与现实的差距"，探索是否能够从有限的宏观观测中学习到能够稳定系统的反馈策略。

Method: 采用模仿学习方法，将依赖于全相空间状态的"专家策略"蒸馏为仅基于宏观测量值的控制器。通过行为克隆（behavior cloning）实现策略蒸馏，并理论刻画了该过程中最小行为克隆损失与初始分布熵之间的关系。

Result: 理论证明了学习策略的稳定性保证，误差下界由观测约束下的最小行为克隆损失决定，该损失进一步由初始分布复杂度（熵）量化。大量数值实验表明，学习策略仅使用宏观观测就能稳定系统，且时间窗口显著长于非自适应基线控制器，验证了该方法对低复杂度结构的自适应性。

Conclusion: 从理论层面证明了从宏观观测学习稳定化反馈策略用于动力学等离子体动力学的可行性，展示了学习方法对低复杂度结构的自适应能力，为核聚变中等离子体控制提供了新的理论支撑和方法路径。

Original Abstract: We consider the stabilization of Vlasov--Poisson plasma dynamics, a central control problem in nuclear fusion. Our focus is the gap between what an ideal controller would use and what experiments can actually observe: while optimal policy may rely on the full phase-space state, practical feedback is typically limited to sparse macroscopic diagnostics. We therefore study imitation learning methods that distill a fully observed expert policy into controllers operating only on macroscopic measurements. We show the stability guarantees of the learned policy, where the error floor depends on the minimal behavior cloning loss achievable under the observation constraints. We further characterize this minimal loss in terms of a notion of entropy that quantifies the complexity of the initial distribution. Our results demonstrates the theoretical feasibility of learning stabilizing feedback policies for kinetic plasma dynamics from macroscopic observations, and exhibits the adaptivity of the learning approach to low-complexity structures. Through extensive numerical experiments, we validate our theory and show that the learned policies can stabilize the system using only macroscopic observations, within a significantly longer time horizon than non-adaptive baseline controllers.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了Vlasov-Poisson等离子体动力学的稳定化问题，这是核聚变中的一个核心控制问题。我们关注的是理想控制器所能使用的信息与实际实验可观测信息之间的差距：最优策略可能依赖于完整的相空间状态，而实际反馈通常局限于稀疏的宏观诊断测量。因此，我们研究模仿学习方法，将完全观测的专家策略蒸馏为仅基于宏观测量进行操作的控制器。我们证明了学习策略的稳定性保证，其中误差下界取决于观测约束下可实现的最小行为克隆损失。我们进一步通过一种熵的概念来刻画这一最小损失，该熵量化了初始分布的复杂度。我们的结果从理论上证明了从宏观观测学习动力学等离子体动力学的稳定反馈策略是可行的，并展示了学习方法对低复杂度结构的自适应性。通过大量数值实验，我们验证了我们的理论，并表明学习策略仅使用宏观观测就能在显著长于非自适应基线控制器的时间范围内稳定系统。

</details>


### [186] [Order Matters: Improving Domain Adaptation by Reordering Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05084)
*Andrea Napoli,Paul White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORDERED是一种新颖的无偏随机方差缩减技术，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来降低无监督域自适应中域差异估计的方差，从而提升目标域精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无监督域自适应中，域差异估计在随机设置下存在高方差问题，这会抑制方法的理论优势，因此需要一种有效的方差缩减技术。

Method: 提出ORDERED方法，针对相关对齐和最大均值差异两种域差异损失，将随机估计误差形式化为数据采样顺序的函数，并设计了一种实用的优化算法来寻找最优采样顺序，从而实现无偏的随机方差缩减。

Result: 模拟实验表明ORDERED相比相关方法具有更低的方差；在两个域迁移图像分类基准上，目标域准确率得到提升。

Conclusion: ORDERED通过优化数据采样顺序有效降低了域差异估计的方差，是一种实用的无偏方差缩减技术，能够提升无监督域自适应的性能。

Original Abstract: Domain shift remains a key challenge in deploying machine learning models to the real world. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to address this by minimising domain discrepancy during training, but the discrepancy estimates suffer from high variance in stochastic settings, which can stifle the theoretical benefits of the method. This paper proposes Optimal Reordering of Data for Error-Reduced Estimation of Discrepancy (ORDERED), a novel unbiased stochastic variance reduction technique which reduces the discrepancy estimation error by optimising the order in which the training data are sampled. We consider two specific domain discrepancy losses (correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy), formulate their stochastic estimation error as a function of the data sampling order, and propose a practical optimisation algorithm. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on two domain shift image classification benchmarks show improved target domain accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 域偏移仍然是将机器学习模型部署到现实世界中的关键挑战。无监督域自适应（UDA）旨在通过最小化训练过程中的域差异来应对这一挑战，但差异估计在随机设置中存在高方差问题，这可能抑制该方法的理论优势。本文提出ORDERED（通过数据的最优重排序实现差异的误差缩减估计），一种新颖的无偏随机方差缩减技术，通过优化训练数据的采样顺序来降低差异估计误差。我们考虑了两种具体的域差异损失（相关对齐和最大均值差异），将其随机估计误差形式化为数据采样顺序的函数，并提出了一种实用的优化算法。我们的模拟实验表明，与相关方法相比，方差有所降低；在两个域迁移图像分类基准上的实验显示，目标域准确率得到了提升。

</details>


### [187] [Gated Multimodal Learning for Interpretable Property Energy Performance Prediction and Retrofit Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05088)
*Yunfei Bai,Aaron Tesfa Tsion,Raul Rosales,Barbara Shollock,Wei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种门控多模态模型，通过融合EPC表格变量、评估师自由文本和GIS空间特征，预测住宅建筑能效等级与环境影响评分，在伦敦威斯敏斯特案例中达到MAE 4.39、R²约0.75的预测性能，并支持改造场景分析，为城市级净零排放住房转型提供可扩展的决策依据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 住宅建筑脱碳对实现韧性可持续城市至关重要，但现有能源绩效证书（EPC）依赖现场巡检，难以进行及时的城市尺度评估。为克服这一局限，需要一种可扩展的方法来预测建筑能效评分。

Method: 提出门控多模态模型，融合三类特征：(1) EPC表格变量；(2) 评估师撰写的自由文本；(3) 基于GIS的空间特征（足迹几何、高度、面积、朝向）。采用样本级门控机制学习每个样本的属性特定模态权重，并引入辅助波段分类头稳定训练。

Result: 在伦敦威斯敏斯特案例中，SAP能效评分预测MAE为4.03，EI环境影响评分MAE为4.76，R²分别为0.757和0.748，平均MAE为4.39。全模态融合优于单模态和双模态基线。可解释性分析显示：门控权重表明模型严重依赖评估师文本；SHAP突出主要燃料、建筑形式和建造年代；文本遮蔽优先考虑屋顶和墙面字段；空间归因以高度和足迹面积为主。

Conclusion: 该框架为改造筛查、干预优先级排序和净零排放住房转型提供了可扩展的建筑级证据支持，并已进一步应用于墙体保温、屋顶保温和窗户玻璃升级等改造场景，显示出在SAP、EI、年度能源成本和等效CO₂排放方面的预期改善。

Original Abstract: Achieving resilient and sustainable cities requires scalable approaches to decarbonising residential buildings, which account for about 20% of UK greenhouse gas emissions and 25% of energy-related emissions in the European Union. Energy Performance Certificates (EPCs) support regulation and retrofit planning, but their reliance on on-site inspections limits timely city-scale assessment. This study introduces a gated multimodal model to predict Standard Assessment Procedure (SAP) energy efficiency and Environmental Impact (EI) scores by integrating EPC tabular variables, assessor-written free text, and Geographic Information System (GIS)-derived spatial features describing footprint geometry, height, area, and orientation. Sample-wise gating learns property-specific modality weights, while an auxiliary band classification head stabilises training. In a Westminster, London case study, the model predicts SAP and EI scores with MAEs of 4.03 and 4.76 points and R2 values of 0.757 and 0.748, respectively, achieving a mean MAE of 4.39. Ablation results show that full multimodal fusion outperforms unimodal and bimodal baselines for both score prediction and band-level classification. Interpretability analyses provide decision-relevant evidence: gating weights indicate strong reliance on assessor text; SHAP highlights main fuel, built form, and construction age band; text occlusion prioritises roof and wall fields; and spatial attribution is dominated by height and footprint area, with sensitivity to footprint shape. The validated framework is further applied to retrofit scenarios for wall insulation, roof insulation, and window glazing upgrades, indicating projected improvements in SAP, EI, annual energy cost, and equivalent CO2 emissions. Overall, the framework provides scalable property-level evidence for retrofit screening, intervention prioritisation, and net-zero housing transitions.

中文摘要翻译: 实现韧性可持续城市需要可扩展的住宅建筑脱碳方法。住宅建筑约占英国温室气体排放的20%和欧盟能源相关排放的25%。能源绩效证书（EPC）支持法规遵从和改造规划，但其对现场巡检的依赖限制了城市尺度的及时评估。本研究引入一种门控多模态模型，通过整合EPC表格变量、评估师撰写的自由文本以及基于地理信息系统（GIS）的空间特征（描述足迹几何形状、高度、面积和朝向），来预测标准评估程序（SAP）能效评分和环境影响（EI）评分。样本级门控机制学习每个样本的属性特定模态权重，辅助波段分类头用于稳定训练。在伦敦威斯敏斯特的案例研究中，该模型预测SAP和EI评分的平均绝对误差（MAE）分别为4.03和4.76分，R²值分别为0.757和0.748，平均MAE为4.39。消融实验结果表明，在评分预测和波段级分类任务上，全多模态融合均优于单模态和双模态基线。可解释性分析提供了与决策相关的证据：门控权重显示模型严重依赖评估师文本；SHAP突出主要燃料、建筑形式和建造年代；文本遮蔽优先考虑屋顶和墙面字段；空间归因以高度和足迹面积为主，并对足迹形状敏感。经验证的框架进一步应用于墙体保温、屋顶保温和窗户玻璃升级的改造场景，表明在SAP、EI、年度能源成本和等效CO₂排放方面均有预期改善。总体而言，该框架为改造筛查、干预优先级排序和净零排放住房转型提供了可扩展的建筑级证据支持。

</details>


### [188] [Continual Knowledge Updating in LLM Systems: Learning Through Multi-Timescale Memory Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05097)
*Andreas Pattichis,Constantine Dovrolis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Memini，一种基于Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型的双时间尺度关联记忆系统，将外部记忆重构为能通过自身动力学自组织的学习基质，实现情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘的统一机制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM的外部记忆系统大多采用显式管理方式，缺乏自主适应能力；而生物记忆通过耦合的多时间尺度动力学实现高效记忆组织，启发我们设计类似原理的外部记忆系统。

Method: 基于Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型，在有向图结构的关联记忆中为每条边配备快、慢两个耦合的内部变量，通过双时间尺度动力学实现记忆的自组织。

Result: 情景敏感性（快速编码新关联）、渐进巩固（重复强化后转入慢变量长期存储）和选择性遗忘（未被重复的记忆自然衰减）作为同一机制的不同侧面涌现出来。

Conclusion: 外部记忆应遵循生物记忆的多时间尺度耦合原理，将自身重构为一个通过自身动力学持续重组的学习基质，而非被动存储。

Original Abstract: LLMs are trained once, then deployed into a world that never stops changing. External memory compensates for this, but most systems manage it explicitly rather than letting it adapt on its own. Biological memory works differently: coupled multi-timescale dynamics make new associations immediately usable, strengthen what repetition confirms, and let the rest fade. We argue that external memory should follow a similar principle. In Memini, this view takes the form of an associative memory that organizes knowledge as a directed graph. Each edge carries two coupled internal variables, one fast and one slow, following the Benna-Fusi model of synaptic consolidation. From this coupling, episodic sensitivity, gradual consolidation, and selective forgetting emerge as facets of a single mechanism, reframing external memory as a learning substrate that reorganizes through its own dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型经过一次性训练后，被部署到一个永不停歇变化的世界中。外部记忆在一定程度上弥补了这一问题，但大多数系统采用显式管理，而非让记忆自主适应。生物记忆的工作原理则不同：耦合的多时间尺度动力学使得新关联可以立即被使用，重复确认的内容得到巩固，其余部分则逐渐消退。我们认为外部记忆应遵循类似的原理。在Memini中，这一观点体现为一种将知识组织为有向图的关联记忆。每条边携带两个耦合的内部变量，一个快变、一个慢变，采用Benna-Fusi突触巩固模型。通过这种耦合，情景敏感性、渐进巩固和选择性遗忘作为同一机制的不同侧面涌现出来，将外部记忆重新构建为一个通过自身动力学持续重组的学习基质。

</details>


### [189] [Unified Framework of Distributional Regret in Multi-Armed Bandits and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05102)
*Harin Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究了随机多臂赌博机和情节式强化学习中的遗憾分布问题，形式化了均匀分布的概率保证，提出了基于UCBVI的算法，并首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári关于分布遗憾界的猜想。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于期望遗憾，缺乏对遗憾整个分布特征的系统性刻画，特别是未能给出对所有置信水平δ一致成立的分布遗憾界。此外，Lattimore & Szepesvári关于多臂赌博机分布遗憾界为O(√(AT) log(1/δ))的猜想尚未被证实。

Method: 提出一个UCBVI风格的算法，使用形如min{c1,k/N, c2,k/√N}的探索奖励，其中N为访问次数，(c1,k,c2,k)为用户指定参数。通过统一框架推导出与参数序列无关和相关的通用分布遗憾界，系统刻画参数如何控制期望性能、尾部风险和实例相关行为之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出通用的gap无关和gap依赖分布遗憾界，实现了最小最大和实例依赖两种情境下期望遗憾与分布遗憾的最优权衡。对于多臂赌博机，证明了分布遗憾界为O(√(AT) log(1/δ))，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文建立的统一框架和分布遗憾界为理解和控制遗憾的全分布特征提供了理论工具，验证了参数选择对期望性能与尾部风险权衡的核心作用，并在重要特例中解决了长期悬而未决的猜想。

Original Abstract: We study the distribution of regret in stochastic multi-armed bandits and episodic reinforcement learning through a unified framework. We formalize a distributional regret bound as a probabilistic guarantee that holds uniformly over all confidence levels $δ\in (0,1]$, thereby characterizing the regret distribution across the full range of $δ$. We present a simple UCBVI-style algorithm with exploration bonus $\min\{c_{1,k}/N, c_{2,k}/\sqrt{N}\}$, where $N$ denotes the visit count and $(c_{1,k},c_{2,k})$ are user-specified parameters. For arbitrary parameter sequences, we derive general gap-independent and gap-dependent distributional regret bounds, yielding a principled characterization of how the parameters control the trade-off between expected performance, tail risk, and instance-dependent behavior. In particular, our bounds achieve optimal trade-offs between expected and distributional regret in both minimax and instance-dependent regimes. As a special case, for multi-armed bandits with $A$ arms and horizon $T$, we obtain a distributional regret bound of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{AT}\log(1/δ))$, confirming the conjecture of Lattimore & Szepesvári (2020, Section 17.1) for the first time.

中文摘要翻译: 我们通过一个统一框架研究了随机多臂赌博机和情节式强化学习中的遗憾分布问题。我们将分布遗憾界形式化为一个概率保证，该保证对所有置信水平δ∈(0,1]一致成立，从而刻画了δ在整个取值范围上的遗憾分布。我们提出了一个简单的UCBVI风格算法，其探索奖励为min{c1,k/N, c2,k/√N}，其中N表示访问次数，(c1,k,c2,k)是用户指定的参数。对于任意参数序列，我们推导出通用的gap无关和gap依赖分布遗憾界，从而系统地表征了参数如何控制期望性能、尾部风险和实例依赖行为之间的权衡。特别地，我们的界在最小最大和实例依赖两种情境下均实现了期望遗憾与分布遗憾之间的最优权衡。作为一个特例，对于具有A个臂和水平线T的多臂赌博机，我们获得了O(√(AT) log(1/δ))量级的分布遗憾界，首次证实了Lattimore & Szepesvári（2020，第17.1节）的猜想。

</details>


### [190] [Rollout Pass-Rate Control: Steering Binary-Reward RL Toward Its Most Informative Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05112)
*Tianshu Zhu,Wenyu Zhang,Xiaoying Zuo,Lun Tian,Haotian Zhao,Yucheng Zeng,Jingnan Gu,Daxiang Dong,Jianmin Wu,Dawei Yin,Dou Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出前缀采样（Prefix Sampling）方法，通过控制轨迹前缀回放将采样组的通过率调整至50%这一最优信息点，从而提升SWE-bench风格智能体强化学习的样本效率，实现2.01倍（14B模型）和1.55倍（32B模型）的端到端加速，并提升最终验证性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SWE-bench风格的智能体强化学习依赖昂贵的有状态轨迹，但大量计算浪费在通过率偏斜的采样组上，二元奖励提供的对比信号微弱。本文将该问题建模为通过率控制问题，发现50%通过率是最具信息量的操作点，因此需要设计一种方法将偏斜的采样组引导至该最优区域。

Method: 提出前缀采样（Prefix Sampling, PS）：对于大多数失败的组，回放成功的前缀作为"头启动"；对于大多数成功的组，回放失败的前缀作为" handicaps"（障碍）。在有状态智能体环境中，通过重放重建前缀状态，同时将回放的token从损失计算中排除，将优化限制在当前策略生成的延续部分。

Result: 在SWE-bench风格智能体RL上：Qwen3-14B实现2.01倍端到端加速，Qwen3-32B实现1.55倍加速，同时保持或提升最终验证性能。14B模型SWE-bench Verified峰值从基线0.273提升至PS下的0.295。AIME 2025数学推理实验同样验证了通过率控制模式。

Conclusion: 50%通过率是智能体强化学习中最具信息量的操作点，最大化奖励熵、组筛选生存概率、RLOO优势能量和正负对比结构。前缀采样通过回放轨迹前缀有效控制通过率，显著提升样本效率和最终性能。

Original Abstract: SWE-bench-style agentic reinforcement learning relies on expensive stateful trajectories, yet substantial compute is wasted on sampled rollout groups with skewed pass rates, where binary rewards provide a weak contrastive signal. We frame this inefficiency as a pass-rate control problem and show that a 50% pass rate is the most informative operating point: it maximizes reward entropy, the probability of surviving group filtering, RLOO advantage energy under GRPO, and success--failure contrastive structure. Guided by this principle, we propose Prefix Sampling (PS), which replays trajectory prefixes to steer skewed groups toward this regime: successful prefixes serve as head starts for mostly failing groups, while failing prefixes serve as handicaps for mostly passing groups. In stateful agent environments, prefix states are reconstructed through replay while replayed tokens are excluded from the loss, restricting optimization to continuations generated by the current policy. On SWE-bench-style agentic RL, PS delivers end-to-end wall-clock speedups of 2.01x on Qwen3-14B and 1.55x on Qwen3-32B while preserving or improving final verified performance. For 14B, the SWE-bench Verified peak rises from the baseline peak of 0.273 to 0.295 under PS. Additional mathematical reasoning experiments on AIME 2025 show the same pass-rate control pattern and decompose the gains into replay, bidirectional coverage, and adaptive control.

中文摘要翻译: SWE-bench风格的智能体强化学习依赖昂贵的有状态轨迹，然而大量计算被浪费在通过率偏斜的采样组上，其中二元奖励仅提供微弱的对比信号。我们将这一低效问题建模为通过率控制问题，并证明50%通过率是最具信息量的操作点：它最大化了奖励熵、组筛选生存概率、GRPO下的RLOO优势能量以及成功-失败对比结构。基于这一原则，我们提出前缀采样（Prefix Sampling, PS），该方法通过回放轨迹前缀将偏斜组引导至该最优区间：成功前缀作为大多数失败组的"头启动"，而失败前缀作为大多数通过组的"障碍"。在有状态智能体环境中，前缀状态通过重放重建，同时重放的token被排除在损失计算之外，将优化限制在当前策略生成的延续部分上。在SWE-bench风格的智能体强化学习中，PS在Qwen3-14B上实现了2.01倍的端到端加速，在Qwen3-32B上实现了1.55倍的端到端加速，同时保持或提升了最终的验证性能。对于14B模型，SWE-bench Verified峰值从基线峰值0.273提升至PS下的0.295。在AIME 2025上的额外数学推理实验也显示了相同的通过率控制模式，并将增益分解为回放、双向覆盖和自适应控制三部分。

</details>


### [191] [How Long Does Infinite Width Last? Signal Propagation in Long-Range Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05113)
*Mariia Seleznova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性循环模型在有限宽度下的信号传播，推导出精确的有限宽度公式，并识别出三种深度-宽度联合缩放机制（次临界、临界、超临界），揭示了无限宽度理论在长程线性循环中失效的精确深度尺度，表明循环模型中有限宽度效应随深度积累比前馈模型更快。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有信号传播理论主要依赖无限宽度极限假设，但当循环深度t与宽度n同时增长时，该近似何时失效尚不清楚。这一问题对现代循环序列模型尤为重要，因为其自然运行模式涉及长输入序列（即大t）。

Method: 推导了复高斯初始化下线性循环隐藏状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式，并基于这些公式分析深度-宽度的联合缩放行为。

Result: 识别出三种联合缩放机制：(i) 次临界区 t=o(√n)，无限宽度近似有效；(ii) 临界区 t~c√n，出现不可忽略的偏差及非平凡联合缩放极限；(iii) 超临界区 t≫√n，有限宽度效应占主导。同时表明标准初始化方案（如Glorot）在超临界区会变得不稳定。

Conclusion: 论文精确指出了无限宽度理论在长程线性循环中失效的深度尺度，并证明循环模型中有限宽度效应随深度积累比前馈模型更快，导致定性不同的信号传播行为。

Original Abstract: We study signal propagation in linear recurrent models at finite width. While existing signal propagation theory relies predominantly on the infinite-width limit, it remains unclear for how long that approximation remains accurate when recurrent depth $t$ grows jointly with width $n$. This question is especially relevant for modern recurrent sequence models, whose natural operating regime involves long input sequences, i.e., large $t$. We derive exact finite-width formulas for the hidden state signal energies in linear recurrences under complex Gaussian initialization. Using these formulas, we identify the joint depth-width scaling regimes that govern signal propagation: (i) a subcritical regime $t=o(\sqrt n)$, in which the infinite-width approximation remains valid; (ii) a critical regime $t\sim c\sqrt n$, in which non-negligible deviations from infinite-width predictions appear and a nontrivial joint scaling limit emerges; and (iii) a supercritical regime $t\gg \sqrt n$, in which finite-width effects dominate. Thus, our results pinpoint the precise recurrent depth scale at which infinite-width theory breaks down in long-range linear recurrences. In turn, this shows when standard initialization schemes, such as Glorot, become unstable. More broadly, our results demonstrate that finite-width effects accumulate more rapidly with depth in recurrent models than in feedforward ones, leading to qualitatively different signal propagation behavior.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了有限宽度下线性循环模型中的信号传播问题。虽然现有信号传播理论主要依赖无限宽度极限，但当循环深度t与宽度n同时增长时，该近似在多大范围内仍然准确尚不清楚。这一问题对于现代循环序列模型尤为重要，因为其自然运行模式涉及长输入序列（即大t）。我们推导了复高斯初始化下线性循环隐藏状态信号能量的精确有限宽度公式。利用这些公式，我们识别出了控制信号传播的联合深度-宽度缩放机制：(i) 次临界区 t=o(√n)，无限宽度近似在该区域内仍然有效；(ii) 临界区 t~c√n，该区域内出现与无限宽度预测不可忽略的偏差，并涌现出非平凡的联合缩放极限；(iii) 超临界区 t≫√n，该区域内有限宽度效应占据主导。因此，我们的结果精确指出了长程线性循环中无限宽度理论失效的循环深度尺度。进而，这揭示了标准初始化方案（如Glorot）何时变得不稳定。更广泛而言，我们的结果表明，循环模型中有限宽度效应随深度积累的速度比前馈模型更快，从而导致定性不同的信号传播行为。

</details>


### [192] [On the Hardness of Junking LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05116)
*Marco Rando,Samuel Vaiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM中的“自然后门”问题（即无需任何语义指令即可触发有害输出的token序列），将其形式化为“junking问题”，并通过贪心随机搜索方法证明该问题虽比标准越狱攻击更难，但简单策略即可高成功率解决，且发现这些token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有越狱攻击依赖精心设计的语义结构（如对抗性后缀/前缀），但最近观察到仅凭优化后的token序列（无任何有意义的指令）也能诱发有害行为，暗示训练过程中自然出现了“后门”token序列。然而这一现象尚未被充分探索，特别是寻找这类自然后门的难度尚未被评估。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 形式化定义“junking问题”：寻找能最大化目标有害响应前缀生成概率的token序列。2) 提出一种贪心随机搜索方法（greedy random-search）来评估这类序列是否容易被发现。

Result: 1) junking问题比标准越狱攻击更难，证实语义信息在提示设计中的重要性。2) 简单的贪心随机搜索策略能以高成功率解决该问题，表明自然后门确实存在且容易被恢复。3) 困惑度分析发现，这些token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持它们是从训练过程中隐式涌现的假设。

Conclusion: 自然后门（无语义指令的纯token序列即可触发有害输出）是真实存在的且容易被发现，尽管该问题比标准越狱攻击更具挑战性。这一发现揭示了LLM训练过程中隐式产生的安全风险，对模型对齐和安全性研究具有重要意义。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to be vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, which typically rely on carefully designed prompts containing explicit semantic structure. These attacks generally operate by fixing an adversarial instruction and optimizing small adversarial components (e.g., suffixes or prefixes). In this setting, prompt structure is fundamental for performance, and recent results show that even simple random search can achieve strong performance when combined with sophisticated prompt design. Recently, it has been observed that harmful behaviors can be elicited even without the adversarial prompt, relying solely on optimized token sequences. This suggests the existence of natural backdoors, i.e., token sequences naturally emerged during LLMs training that trigger unsafe outputs without any meaningful instruction. However, despite these observations, this setting remains largely unexplored, and in particular the hardness of finding natural backdoors has not been assessed yet. In this work, we provide a first proof-of-concept study investigating the hardness of this task, which we refer to as the junking problem. We formalize it as the problem of finding token sequences that maximize the probability of generating a target prefix of harmful responses, propose a greedy random-search method to assess is such sequences can be discovered easily. Our results show that this problem is harder than standard jailbreak attacks, confirming the importance of semantic information in prompt design. At the same time, we find that our simple strategy is sufficient to solve it with a high success rate, suggesting that natural backdoors are present and easily recoverable. Finally, through perplexity analysis, we observe that the discovered token sequences lie in low-probability regions of the model distribution, supporting the hypothesis that they emerged implicitly from the training process.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）已知容易受到越狱攻击，这类攻击通常依赖于包含显式语义结构的精心设计的提示。这些攻击通常通过固定对抗性指令并优化小型对抗性组件（如后缀或前缀）来实现。在此设定中，提示结构对性能至关重要，最近结果显示，当结合复杂的提示设计时，即使是简单的随机搜索也能取得强大性能。近期观察到，即使没有对抗性提示，仅依赖优化的token序列也能引发有害行为。这表明存在自然后门，即LLM训练过程中自然涌现的、无需任何有意义指令即可触发不安全输出的token序列。然而，尽管有这些观察，这一设定仍然很大程度上未被探索，特别是寻找自然后门的难度尚未被评估。本文提供了首次概念验证研究，调查这一任务的难度，我们将其称为"junking问题"。我们将其形式化为寻找能最大化目标有害响应前缀生成概率的token序列的问题，并提出一种贪心随机搜索方法来评估此类序列是否容易被发现。我们的结果表明，该问题比标准越狱攻击更难，证实了语义信息在提示设计中的重要性。同时，我们发现我们的简单策略足以高成功率地解决该问题，表明自然后门确实存在且容易被恢复。最后，通过困惑度分析，我们观察到所发现的token序列位于模型分布的低概率区域，支持它们是从训练过程中隐式涌现的假设。

</details>


### [193] [On the Wasserstein Gradient Flow Interpretation of Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05118)
*Arthur Gretton,Li Kevin Wenliang,Alexandre Galashov,James Thornton,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）视角分析了Deng等人（2026）提出的生成式漂移建模（GMD）框架，揭示了GMD与WGF之间的理论联系，并指出了其实际实现与理论算法之间的差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为GMD提供严格的理论基础，通过WGF的几何视角解释其工作原理，澄清Deng等人提出的算法与实际实现之间的差异，并探索将GMD思想扩展到其他散度度量（如MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数）的可能性。

Method: 从Wasserstein梯度流的理论框架出发，将GMD建模为直接针对特定WGF的不动点进行优化。具体分析了三种情形：（1）理论算法对应于KL散度上的WGF结合Parzen平滑；（2）实际实现对应于Sinkhorn散度上的WGF不动点，但缺乏某些理想性质；（3）该思想可推广到其他WGF，包括MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数。

Result: （1）Deng等人提出的一个算法等价于寻找KL散度上带Parzen平滑的WGF的极限点；（2）实际实现的算法对应一个不同的过程，类似于Sinkhorn散度WGF的不动点但缺乏其理想性质；（3）GMD思想可扩展到其他WGF的不动点，如MMD、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数。

Conclusion: GMD可被理解为直接优化特定WGF的不动点，这一视角为理解GMD提供了坚实的理论基础，但也揭示了其理论算法与实际实现之间的不一致性，同时该视角为生成建模提供了新的统一框架。

Original Abstract: Recently, Deng et al. (2026) proposed Generative Modeling via Drifting (GMD), a novel framework for generative tasks. This note presents an analysis of GMD through the lens of Wasserstein Gradient Flows (WGF), i.e., the path of steepest descent for a functional in the space of probability measures, equipped with the geometry of optimal transport. Unlike previous WGF-based contributions, GMD can be thought of as directly targeting a fixed point of a specific WGF flow. We demonstrate three main results: first, that one algorithm proposed by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to finding the limiting point of a WGF on the KL divergence, with Parzen smoothing on the densities. Second, that the algorithm actually implemented by Deng et al. (2026) corresponds to a different procedure, which bears some resemblance to the fixed point of a WGF on the Sinkhorn divergence, but lacks certain desirable properties of the latter. Third, the same same idea can be extended to the limiting point of other WGFs, including the Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), the sliced Wasserstein distance, and GAN critic functions.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，Deng等人（2026）提出了生成式漂移建模（GMD），一种新颖的生成任务框架。本文通过Wasserstein梯度流（WGF）的视角对GMD进行分析，WGF即概率测度空间中泛函的最速下降路径，其几何结构由最优输运赋予。与以往基于WGF的工作不同，GMD可被视为直接针对特定WGF的不动点进行优化。我们证明了三个主要结果：第一，Deng等人（2026）提出的一个算法等价于寻找KL散度上带Parzen平滑的WGF的极限点；第二，Deng等人（2026）实际实现的算法对应一个不同的过程，该过程类似于Sinkhorn散度上WGF的不动点，但缺乏后者的某些理想性质；第三，同样的思想可扩展到其他WGF的极限点，包括最大均值差异（MMD）、切片Wasserstein距离和GAN判别器函数。

</details>


### [194] [Adaptive Policy Selection and Fine-Tuning under Interaction Budgets for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05123)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Xiaoan Xu,Shangtong Zhang,Miroslav Pajic,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应策略选择与微调方法，用于解决离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）中，在有限在线交互预算下如何高效地从多个候选策略中选择并微调最优策略的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有O2O-RL流程存在两个主要问题：1）离线策略评估（OPE）不可靠，仅基于OPE选择策略风险高，而在线评估（OE）虽可靠但消耗了大量本可用于微调的在线交互；2）预先无法确定某个预训练策略在部署后微调是否会有提升，尤其在非平稳环境中。因此，固定选择单个策略进行部署在实际场景中不可行，而穷举微调所有候选策略又会违反交互预算约束。

Method: 首先使用不同离线RL算法和超参数训练一组候选策略，然后通过OPE获得初始性能估计。接着，基于上置信界（UCB）方法自适应地选择策略并对其进行微调，根据策略的预测性能动态分配在线交互预算，从而高效利用有限的在线交互资源。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，所提出的方法优于现有的O2O-RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应策略选择与微调方法能够在有限在线交互预算下，有效解决O2O-RL中策略选择与微调的权衡问题，提升整体性能。

Original Abstract: In offline-to-online reinforcement learning (O2O-RL), policies are first safely trained offline using previously collected datasets and then further fine-tuned for tasks via limited online interactions. In a typical O2O-RL pipeline, candidate policies trained with offline RL are evaluated via either off-policy evaluation (OPE) or online evaluation (OE). The policy with the highest estimated value is then deployed and continually fine-tuned. However, this setup has two main issues. First, OPE can be unreliable, making it risky to deploy a policy based solely on those estimates, whereas OE may identify a viable policy with substantial online interaction, which could have been used for fine-tuning. Second--and more importantly--it is also often not possible to determine a priori whether a pretrained policy will improve with post-deployment fine-tuning, especially in non-stationary environments. As a result, procedures committing to a single deployed policy are impractical in many real-world settings. Moreover, a naive remedy that exhaustively fine-tunes all candidates would violate interaction budget constraints and is likewise infeasible. In this paper, we propose a novel adaptive approach for policy selection and fine-tuning under online interaction budgets in O2O-RL. Following the standard pipeline, we first train a set of candidate policies with different offline RL algorithms and hyperparameters; we then perform OPE to obtain initial performance estimates. We next adaptively select and fine-tune the policies based on their predicted performance via an upper-confidence-bound approach thereby making efficient use of online interactions. We demonstrate that our approach improves upon O2O-RL baselines with various benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 在离线到在线强化学习（O2O-RL）中，策略首先利用先前收集的数据集进行离线安全训练，然后通过有限的在线交互进一步针对任务进行微调。在典型的O2O-RL流程中，使用离线RL训练得到的候选策略通过离线策略评估（OPE）或在线评估（OE）进行评估。具有最高估计价值的策略随后被部署并持续微调。然而，这种设置存在两个主要问题。首先，OPE可能不可靠，因此仅基于这些估计值部署策略具有风险，而OE虽然能够识别出可行的策略，但需要大量的在线交互，而这些交互本可用于微调。其次——也是更重要的一点——通常无法预先确定一个预训练策略在部署后微调是否会有所提升，尤其是在非平稳环境中。因此，承诺只部署单一策略的程序在许多实际场景中并不实用。此外，穷举微调所有候选策略这种简单粗暴的补救措施会违反交互预算约束，同样不可行。在本文中，我们提出了一种新颖的自适应方法，用于在O2O-RL的在线交互预算约束下进行策略选择和微调。遵循标准流程，我们首先使用不同的离线RL算法和超参数训练一组候选策略；然后执行OPE以获取初始性能估计。接着，我们通过一种基于上置信界的方法，根据预测性能自适应地选择和微调策略，从而高效利用在线交互。我们通过多种基准测试证明，该方法相比现有的O2O-RL基线有显著提升。

</details>


### [195] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估了一种基于电子病历数据的异常检测方法，用于识别患者管理中的异常行为，这些异常行为可能提示潜在医疗错误。基于4486名心脏术后患者数据的专家评估表明，该方法具备较低的误报率，且异常强度与警报率正相关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医疗预警系统多基于显式规则，但许多潜在医疗错误难以通过规则提前定义。因此，作者提出利用历史患者数据中隐含的常规管理模式，通过检测与常规模式偏离的异常行为来发现可能存在的医疗错误。

Method: 采用数据驱动的方法，基于电子健康档案（EHR）中存储的既往患者案例，检测当前患者管理行为中与历史常规模式不一致的异常行为，并据此触发警报。收集4486名心脏术后患者数据进行实验验证。

Result: 基于一组专家意见进行评估，结果表明：基于异常检测的警报系统具有较低的误报率；且异常程度越强，警报率越高，两者呈正相关关系。

Conclusion: 异常驱动的警报方法在医疗场景中具有可行性和有效性，能够以合理的低误报率识别潜在错误，异常强度可作为警报优先级的有用指标。

Original Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

中文摘要翻译: 我们开发并评估了一种数据驱动的方法，利用存储在电子健康档案（EHR）系统中的既往患者病例来检测异常的患者管理行为。我们的假设是：与既往患者相比不寻常的患者管理行为可能是潜在错误所致，当遇到此类情况时，值得触发警报。我们使用来自4486名心脏术后患者的电子健康档案数据对该假设进行了评估。评估基于一组专家意见。结果支持：基于异常检测的警报机制能够拥有合理的低误报率，并且更强的异常与更高的警报率呈正相关。

</details>


### [196] [Joint Treatment Effect Estimation from Incomplete Healthcare Data: Temporal Causal Normalizing Flows with LLM-driven Evolutionary MNAR Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05125)
*Olivia Jullian Parra,Sara Zoccheddu,David Catalan Cerezo,Tom Forzy,Franziska Ulrich,William Sutcliffe,Jakob Martin Burgstaller,Oliver Senn,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段因果效应估计流水线：CausalFlow-T（基于DAG约束的归一化流+精确反事实推理）处理因果识别，LLM驱动的进化插补器处理MNAR缺失数据。在合成/半合成基准测试和瑞士2型糖尿病真实EHR数据上验证了有效性，GLP-1受体激动剂相比SGLT-2抑制剂的体重减轻差异为-0.98 kg，与RCT证据一致。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实EHR数据中时间依赖性混杂和MNAR（非随机缺失）生物标志物缺失率高达50%-80%，现有方法将因果估计、缺失数据处理和时间结构分开处理，缺乏鲁棒性。需一种能同时处理因果推断、时空缺失且无需变分推断近似误差的方法。

Method: 两阶段流水线：（1）CausalFlow-T：DAG约束的归一化流结合LSTM编码患者历史，进行精确可逆反事实推理，避免变分推断误差，通过显式因果结构分离混杂；（2）LLM驱动的进化插补器：提出可执行的插补算子而非单个条目的插补策略，使用多个LLM后端（含两个开源模型）处理30%-80%的MNAR缺失。

Result: 在四个合成和一个半合成基准上，DAG约束和精确推理分别解决不同失败模式且不可相互替代。在30%-80% MNAR缺失下，LLM进化插补器在生物标志物和因果指标上取得最佳综合排名，领先于点级准确性和时间外推性能，同时保留ATE恢复效果（统计基线退化时仍有效）。在瑞士2型糖尿病真实EHR数据上，估计每组方案体重减轻差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，与随机对照试验证据一致。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段流水线能够在现实且不完整的EHR数据中可靠估计因果效应，DAG约束与精确推理互补解决不同失败模式，LLM驱动的进化插补策略在处理高比例MNAR缺失时优于传统统计方法。

Original Abstract: Target trial emulation (TTE) enables causal questions to be studied with observational data when randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are infeasible. Yet treatment-effect methods often address causal estimation, missingness, and temporal structure separately, limiting their robustness in electronic health records (EHRs), where time-varying confounding and missing-not-at-random (MNAR) biomarkers can reach 50%--80%. We propose a two-stage pipeline for treatment effect estimation from incomplete longitudinal EHRs. First, CausalFlow-T, a directed acyclic graph (DAG)-constrained normalizing flow with long short-term memory (LSTM)-encoded patient history, performs exact invertible counterfactual inference, avoiding approximation errors from variational inference and separating confounding through explicit causal structure. Ablations on four synthetic and one semi-synthetic benchmark with known counterfactuals show that DAG constraints and exact inference address distinct failure modes: neither compensates for the other. Second, because CausalFlow-T requires completed inputs, we introduce an LLM-driven evolutionary imputer that proposes executable imputation operators rather than individual entries, and evaluate it with three large language model (LLM) backends, including two open-source models. Across 30%--80% MNAR missingness, this imputer achieves the best pooled rank over biomarker and causal metrics, leading in point-wise accuracy and temporal extrapolation while preserving average treatment effect (ATE) recovery as statistical baselines degrade. On Swiss primary-care EHRs from adults with type 2 diabetes initiating a GLP-1 receptor agonist or SGLT-2 inhibitor, the pipeline estimates a per-protocol weight-loss difference of -0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96] favoring GLP-1 receptor agonists, consistent with randomized evidence and obtained from realistically incomplete real-world EHRs.

中文摘要翻译: 目标试验模拟（TTE）使得在随机对照试验（RCT）不可行时，能够利用观察性数据研究因果问题。然而，现有处理效应方法通常将因果估计、缺失值和时间结构分开处理，限制了在电子健康记录（EHR）中的鲁棒性——因为在EHR中，时间依赖性混杂和非随机缺失（MNAR）的生物标志物缺失率可达50%-80%。我们提出了一种从非完整纵向EHR中估计处理效应的两阶段流水线。首先，CausalFlow-T——一种基于有向无环图（DAG）约束的归一化流，结合长短期记忆（LSTM）编码的患者历史——执行精确可逆的反事实推理，避免了变分推断的近似误差，并通过显式因果结构分离混杂。在四个合成和一个半合成基准上的消融实验表明，DAG约束和精确推断分别应对不同的失败模式：两者不可相互替代。其次，由于CausalFlow-T需要完整的输入，我们引入了LLM驱动的进化插补器，它提出可执行的插补算子而非单个条目，并使用三个大语言模型（LLM）后端（包括两个开源模型）进行评估。在30%-80%的MNAR缺失率下，该插补器在生物标志物和因果指标上取得了最佳综合排名，在点级准确性和时间外推方面表现领先，同时在统计基线退化时仍能保持平均处理效应（ATE）的恢复能力。在瑞士初级保健EHR中，对于启动GLP-1受体激动剂或SGLT-2抑制剂的2型糖尿病成人患者，该流水线估计出每组方案体重减轻差异为-0.98 kg [95% CI -1.01, -0.96]，倾向于GLP-1受体激动剂，这一结果与随机对照试验证据一致，且来源于现实世界中非完整的EHR数据。

</details>


### [197] [Low-Cost Black-Box Detection of LLM Hallucinations via Dynamical System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05134)
*Dan Wilson,Mohamed Akrout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将LLM视为黑盒动力系统的方法，利用Koopman算子理论对事实性和幻觉性响应进行建模，通过单次前向传播和差分残差得分实现低成本的幻觉检测，并引入偏好感知校准机制以适应不同需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法依赖计算昂贵的采样一致性检查或外部知识检索，成本高且效率低，需要一种低成本、单次前向传播即可完成检测的方法。

Method: 将LLM视为黑盒动力系统，通过嵌入模型将LLM响应投影到高维流形，利用Koopman算子理论对事实性响应和幻觉性响应分别拟合转移算子，基于预测误差定义差分残差得分，并引入偏好感知校准机制优化分类阈值。

Result: 在三个数据基准上的实验表明，该方法以更低的资源开销实现了最先进的检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了计算开销，为LLM幻觉检测提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently generate plausible but non-factual content, a phenomenon known as hallucination. While existing detection methods typically rely on computationally expensive sampling-based consistency checks or external knowledge retrieval, we propose a new method that treats the LLM as a black-box dynamical system. By projecting LLM responses into a high-dimensional manifold via an embedding model, we characterize the resulting vector sequences as observable realizations of the model's latent state-space dynamics. Leveraging Koopman operator theory, we fit the transition operators for both factual and hallucinated regimes and define a differential residual score based on their respective prediction errors. To accommodate varying user requirements and domain-specific sensitivities, we introduce a preference-aware calibration mechanism that optimizes the classification threshold based on a small set of demonstrations. This approach enables low-cost hallucination detection in a single-sample pass, avoiding the need for secondary sampling or external grounding. Extensive testing across three data benchmarks demonstrates that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with reduced resource overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）经常生成看似合理但非事实的内容，这种现象被称为幻觉。现有检测方法通常依赖计算昂贵的基于采样的一致性检查或外部知识检索，我们提出了一种新方法，将LLM视为黑盒动力系统。通过嵌入模型将LLM响应投影到高维流形，我们将得到的向量序列表征为模型潜在状态空间动力学的可观实现。利用Koopman算子理论，我们分别拟合事实性和幻觉性两种模态下的转移算子，并基于各自的预测误差定义差分残差得分。为适应不同用户需求和领域敏感性，我们引入一种偏好感知校准机制，基于少量演示样本优化分类阈值。该方法能够在单次样本前向传播中实现低成本幻觉检测，无需二次采样或外部知识支撑。在三个数据基准上的广泛测试表明，我们的方法以更低的资源开销实现了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [198] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了⼀个由⾼中生和低年级本科生组成的团队，在研究生导师和AI⼯具助⼒下开展AI研究项⽬的经验。项⽬强调⼯作流设计，学⽣通过AI⼯具迭代编写代码，每⽇站会聚焦问题定义和概念理解。结果显⽰，尽管学⽣在AI和⾦融⽅⾯背景有限，但仍能借助AI⼯具构建具有实际应⽤价值的模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探讨⾼中生在有限技术背景下如何通过AI⼯具加速研究流程，使其能够专注于⾼阶问题建模与解决，并验证这种新型教学⽅式的有效性。

Method: 项⽬采⽤⼯作流设计⽅法：学⽣⾸先识别解决问题所需的步骤序列，然后使⽤AI驱动⼯具执⾏每⼀步骤。学⽣通过与AI⼯具迭代协作开发代码，并利⽤每⽇站会进⾏调试和概念答疑。

Result: 学⽣在⼀个暑假期间取得了显著进展，每位学⽣都能深⼊探索⾃⼰感兴趣的领域（计算机科学或⾦融），并借助AI⼯具构建了具有实际应⽤意义的模型。

Conclusion: AI⼯具可以有效⽤于指导⾼中⽣进⾏研究项⽬，使学⽣能够聚焦于⾃⼰的具体兴趣，通过每⽇站会强化问题定义和概念理解，从⽽在有限技术背景下实现有意义的成果。

Original Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

中文摘要翻译: 本⽂回顾了⼀个由⾼中⽣和低年级本科⽣组成的团队，在研究生导师的指导及AI⼯具的辅助下开展的AI研究项⽬。我们分享了⾼中⽣的学习体验，以及AI⼯具如何加速研究流程，使学⽣能够专注于⾼阶问题建模与求解。尽管参与者在AI和⾦融⽅⾯的背景有限，但他们展现了对技术市场分析和ETF价格预测的浓厚热情。传统的学习环境会先在课堂上教授必要⽅法，然后才让学⽣进⾏应⽤。与之相对，我们的项⽬强调⼯作流设计：学⽣⾸先识别解决问题所需的步骤序列，然后使⽤AI驱动⼯具执⾏每⼀步骤。我们注意到，⾼中⽣通过与AI⼯具的迭代协作开发了必要的代码，并通过每⽇站会进⾏调试和概念答疑。每位学⽣都能在计算机科学或⾦融领域深⼊探索⾃⼰的兴趣，同时在2025年夏季的协作中取得了显著进展。该项⽬是⼀次重要的教学实践，展示了如何利⽤AI⼯具指导⾼中⽣，使他们能够专注于⾃⼰的具体兴趣，并通过每⽇站会集中关注问题定义和概念理解。尽管学⽣的技术资质有限，他们仍能够借助AI⼯具构建具有实际应⽤价值的模型。

</details>
